Namo tassa bhagavato arahato sammāsambuddhassa

Homage to the Blessed One, the Arahant, the Perfectly Self-Enlightened One.

Buddhaguṇagāthāvalī

A Garland of Verses on the Qualities of the Buddha

Yatheva puppharāsimhā, gantheyya mālā sobhanā;

Evaṃ buddhaguṇagāthā, viraciṃ sahassādhikā.

Just as from a heap of flowers a beautiful garland might be strung, so have I composed these verses on the Buddha’s qualities, more than a thousand in number.

Sopi bhagavā arahaṃ, sammāsambuddho sayambhū;

Vijjācaraṇasampanno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1.

He, the Blessed One, is an Arahant, a Perfectly Self-Enlightened One, the Self-Arisen; accomplished in true knowledge and conduct, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Purisadammasārathī, lokavidū anuttaro;

Satthādevamanussānaṃ, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.2.

The unsurpassed guide of persons to be tamed, the knower of the world, the teacher of devas and humans; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sugato sugado sāmī, sukhado santipadāyako;

Sabbalokānukampako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.3.

The Well-Gone One, the Speaker of Good, the Lord, the Giver of Happiness, the Bestower of the State of Peace, the one compassionate to the entire world; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Varo varaññū varado, varuttamo varāharo;

Varadhammaṃ adesayi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.4.

The Excellent One, knower of the excellent, giver of the excellent, the most excellent, bringer of the excellent, who taught the excellent Dhamma; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāmaṅgalamaṅgalyo, maṅgalo maṅgalālayo;

Maṅgalāyatano nātho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.5.

The great auspicious blessing, the auspicious one, the abode of auspiciousness, the source of auspiciousness, the Protector; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Maṅgalindo maṅgaliko, mahāmaṅgalanāyako;

Maṅgaladhammaṃ desesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.6.

The Lord of Auspiciousness, the Auspicious One, the great leader of auspiciousness, who taught the Auspicious Dhamma; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Maṅgalaggo maṅgalaññū, maṅgalatthapadāyako;

Maṅgalapanthadassāvī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.7.

The pinnacle of auspiciousness, the knower of the auspicious, the bestower of the auspicious goal, the shower of the auspicious path; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Maṅgaliccho maṅgaliddho, maṅgalamabhivaddhano;

Maṅgalehi paripuṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.8.

Desiring the auspicious, endowed with auspicious power, promoting auspiciousness, completely filled with auspicious qualities; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Maṅgalābhā ujjotesi, maṅgalatthapabhākaro;

Maṅgalālokamaṇḍito, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.9.

You shine with an auspicious radiance, you are the maker of the light of the auspicious goal, you are adorned with the light of auspiciousness; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Maṅgalatthamanuppatto, maṅgalatthaparāyaṇo;

Sabbadā maṅgalakaro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.10.

Having attained the auspicious goal, devoted to the auspicious goal, always a maker of the auspicious; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Maṅgalamaggamanvesi, patto maṅgalamuttamaṃ;

Maṅgalāyanaṃ dassetā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.11.

You sought the auspicious path and attained the supreme auspiciousness; you are the revealer of the way to the auspicious; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Maṅgalamahimādhārī, maṅgalakārī nāyako;

Sabbadhi maṅgaladātā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.12.

Bearer of the majesty of auspiciousness, the leader who creates the auspicious, the giver of auspiciousness everywhere; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Maṅgalassa kovido ca, amaṅgalassa kovido;

Paramaṃ maṅgalaṃ laddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.13.

Skilled in the auspicious and skilled in the inauspicious, having attained the supreme auspiciousness; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbamaṅgalasampanno, maṅgalaghosaghosako;

Maṅgaluttamaṃ desesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.14.

Accomplished in all that is auspicious, proclaimer of the auspicious message, you who taught the supreme auspiciousness; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāmaṅgalatthaṃ maggī, laddho dhammasumaṅgalo;

Maṅgala’ddhānaṃ ñāpesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.15.

You sought the goal of great auspiciousness, you obtained the good auspiciousness of the Dhamma, you made known the auspicious journey; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Agārikatthaṃ ñāpesi, mahāmaṅgalamuttamaṃ;

Gihī sahāyako sāmī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.16.

You made known the supreme great auspiciousness for the householder; the Lord, the companion of laypeople; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāmaṅgala’laṅkito, mahāmaṅgalabhūsano;

Mahāmaṅgalasekharo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.17.

Adorned with great auspiciousness, ornamented with great auspiciousness, crowned with a crest of great auspiciousness; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāmaṅgalaṃsumālī, maṅgalābho samujjalo;

Maṅgalaṃsuṃ ujjotesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.18.

Wearing a garland of rays of great auspiciousness, shining brightly with auspicious radiance, you illuminate with rays of auspiciousness; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahaggha maṅgalodadhi, mahantamaṅgalaṇṇavo;

Mahāmaṅgalasāgaro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.19.

The priceless ocean of auspiciousness, the vast ocean of auspiciousness, the great sea of auspiciousness; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Amaṅgalanisāhantā, maṅgalabhānubhassaro;

Maṅgalālokavikiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.20.

Destroyer of the night of the inauspicious, radiant as the sun of auspiciousness, scattering the light of auspiciousness; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bāhusacco bujjhanako, dhammacakkhuuppādako;

Tibhavamaṅgalaṅkārī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.21.

One of great learning, the Awakener, producer of the Dhamma-eye, the auspicious ornament of the three worlds; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāvattā pavattā ca, atthassa ninnetā pabhū;

Amatassa dātā viññū, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.22.

The great speaker and expounder, the powerful guide to the goal, the wise giver of the Deathless; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Divā tapati ādicco, rattiṃ dippati candimā;

Rattiṃdivaṃ tapi nātho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.23.

The sun shines by day, the moon glows by night; the Protector shines day and night; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sannaddho khattiyo tapati, jhāyī tapati brāhmaṇo;

Nirantaraṃ tapi nātho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.24.

The warrior shines in his armor, the brahmin shines in meditation; the Protector shines unceasingly; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dukkhadomanassanāsī, sokaparidevakkhayo;

Jātimaccujarātigo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.25.

Destroyer of pain and displeasure, he in whom sorrow and lamentation are exhausted, who has gone beyond birth, death, and old age; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Yathāvādī tathākārī, tathavādī tathāgato;

Yathākārī tathāvādī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.26.

As he speaks, so he acts; as he acts, so he speaks. One who speaks thus is the Tathāgata; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhaggarāgo bhaggadoso, bhaggamoho so bhagavā;

Bhaggamāno bhaggamāyo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.27.

He, the Blessed One, has broken lust, broken hatred, and broken delusion; he has broken conceit and broken deceit; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhaggakāmo bhaggakodho, bhaggakopo bhaggakuho;

Bhaggakasāvo bhaggindho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.28.

He has broken sensual desire, broken anger, broken wrath, and broken deceit; he has broken the stains and broken the fuel of existence; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhaggajāti bhaggamaccū, bhaggaloko bhaggabhavo;

Bhaggasaṃsāro bhaggogho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.29.

He has broken birth, broken death, broken the world, and broken becoming; he has broken saṃsāra and broken the floods; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhogabhaggo sokabhaggo, rogabhaggo bhaggarajo;

Sūlabhaggo sallabhaggo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.30.

He has broken attachment to enjoyments, broken sorrow, broken disease, and broken defilement; he has broken the stake and broken the dart; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Āsābhaggo issābhaggo, ejābhaggo bhaggajaṭā;

Chandabhaggo bandhabhaggo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.31.

He has shattered craving, shattered envy, shattered agitation, and shattered the tangle; he has shattered desire and shattered bondage; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Vītarāgo vītadoso, vītamoho vītāsavo;

Vītakāmo vītakodho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.32.

Devoid of lust, devoid of hatred, devoid of delusion, devoid of taints; devoid of sensual desire, devoid of anger; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Vītapāpo vītapuñño, vītabhāro vītamalo;

Vītavikāro vītindho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.33.

Devoid of evil and devoid of merit, with burden laid down, devoid of stain; devoid of agitation, devoid of fuel; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Vantarāgo vantadoso, vantamoho vantamalo;

Vantakasāvo vantīgho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.34.

Having relinquished lust, relinquished hatred, relinquished delusion, and relinquished stain; having relinquished the corruptions and relinquished anguish; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Vantarāgo vantadoso, vantamoho vantamalo;

Vantakasāvo vantīgho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.35.

Having relinquished lust, relinquished hatred, relinquished delusion, and relinquished stain; having relinquished the corruptions and relinquished anguish; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sagguru tilokaguru, sabbasattānamaggagurū;

Netāresu mahānetā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.36.

Teacher of the heavens, teacher of the three worlds, the foremost teacher of all beings, the great leader among leaders; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāguru naraguru, devaguru guruttamo!

Jeṭṭhaguru seṭṭhaguru, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.37.

Great teacher, teacher of humans, teacher of devas, the supreme teacher! Chief teacher, the best of teachers; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Vissaguru lokaguru, dhammaguru gurūttamo;

Natthi etādiso guru, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.38.

Teacher of all, teacher of the world, teacher of the Dhamma, the supreme teacher; there is no other teacher like him; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāsatthā mahāsotthi, mahāmitto mahāsakhā;

Mahākalyāṇamitto yo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.39.

The great teacher, the great well-being, the great friend, the great companion; he who is the great spiritual friend; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāpañño mahāviññū, mahāvidvā mahāvidū;

Mahāmedhāvī sumedho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.40.

Of great wisdom, greatly discerning, greatly learned, greatly knowing; of great intelligence, the one of good wisdom; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāsuddho mahābhaddo, mahādayo mahāsayo;

Mahādibbo mahābhabbo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.41.

Greatly pure, greatly auspicious, greatly compassionate, greatly magnanimous; greatly divine, greatly capable; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāvīro mahādhīro, mahāsūro mahabbalo;

Mahāmāracamū maddi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.42.

The great hero, the greatly steadfast, the great warrior, the one of great strength, who crushed the great army of Māra; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahājeṭṭho mahāseṭṭho, mahaggo mahānāyako;

Mahācheko mahādakkho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.43.

The great chief, the great best, the great foremost, the great leader; the greatly skillful, the greatly adept; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahābyatto mahāñāto, mahākhyāto mahāyasī;

Mahiddho mahāvikhyāto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.44.

Greatly accomplished, greatly known, greatly famed, of great glory; of great psychic power, greatly renowned; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahātuṭṭho mahāhaṭṭho, mahājavo mahājayo;

Mahāsantuṭṭho visiṭṭho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.45.

Greatly satisfied, greatly joyful, of great energy, of great victory; greatly contented, the distinguished one; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahānātho mahāsāmī, mahāpabhū mahāvibhū;

Mahissaro bodhissaro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.46.

The great protector, the great lord, the great master, the great sovereign; the great ruler, the lord of enlightenment; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāvaṇṇo mahākanto, manopiyo manāpiko;

Mahāsobhano manuñño, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.47.

Of great complexion, greatly lovely, dear to the mind, delightful; greatly beautiful, charming; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahājhāyī mahāñāṇī, mahādhīmā mahāsudhī;

Mahāvibhāvī medhāvī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.48.

The great meditator, one of great knowledge, the greatly wise, the greatly intelligent; the greatly resplendent, the wise one; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahākhantī ca titikkho, mahānikkaṅkho dhammagū;

Mahāsaṃyato saṃvuto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.49.

Of great patience and forbearance, greatly free from doubt, a master of the Dhamma; greatly restrained and self-controlled; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāthomito pūjito, mahāmānito vandito;

Mahābhivādito bhiyyo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.50.

Greatly praised and honored, greatly respected and venerated; greatly saluted again and again; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahatattho mahatthiko, mahantattho mahattaro;

Mahanto mahattappatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.51.

Of great purpose, seeking the great goal, of immense purpose, the greater one; the great one who has attained greatness; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahālābhī mahākārī, mahādāyī mahādhanī;

Mahāvijjo mahāpujjo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.52.

Of great gain, of great action, the great giver, of great wealth; of great knowledge, greatly worthy of honor; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahābuddhi mahābudhā, mahāpabodhipuṅgavo;

Mahābhavaṇṇavaṃ tiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.53.

Of great intelligence, the greatly awakened, the great bull of supreme enlightenment, who has crossed the great ocean of existence; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahānāgo mahābhāgo, mahābāho mahiddhiko;

Mahāyodho mahaggato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.54.

The great Nāga, the greatly fortunate, the great-armed, the one of great psychic power; the great warrior, who has gone to the highest; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahādanto mahāsanto, mahāgaṇī mahāguṇī;

Mahāsantipadāyako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.55.

Greatly tamed, greatly peaceful, with a great following, of great virtue; the bestower of the state of great peace; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāsīlo mahācitto, mahābudho mahesino;

Mahāmohodadhiṃ tiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.56.

Of great virtue, of great mind, the greatly awakened, the great seer, who has crossed the great ocean of delusion; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahātapassī dhammesī, mahāyasassī nāyako;

Mahāpatāpī tejassī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.57.

The great ascetic, the seeker of Dhamma, the one of great glory, the leader; of great majesty, the radiant one; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāābho mahāpabho, mohandhakārabhindako;

Ñāṇapadīpaṃ dīpesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.58.

Of great radiance, of great light, the breaker of the darkness of delusion, who lit the lamp of knowledge; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāpassaddhidāyako, munirājā munissaro;

Santidado sukhadado, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.59.

Giver of great tranquility, king of sages, lord of sages; giver of peace, giver of happiness; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahānīvaraṇā’tīto, mahāmohasamūhato;

Mahoghatiṇṇo mokkhako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.60.

Gone beyond the great hindrances, with great delusion uprooted, having crossed the great flood, the liberator; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahābhavopadhicatto, lobhadosavināsako;

Mahāripuṃ nimaddesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.61.

Having abandoned the great substrata of existence, the destroyer of greed and hatred, who crushed the great enemy; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahā’bhiññābalappatto, mahāmaññitanāsako;

Mahāsaddhamaṃ niddisi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.62.

Having attained the power of the great direct knowledges, the destroyer of great conceit, who pointed out the great true Dhamma; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ahorattiṃ sadāsuddho, mahālokagganāyako;

Samattavissavissuto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.63.

Always pure day and night, the great foremost leader of the world, renowned throughout the entire universe; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāavijjāucchinno, mahāāsattiriñcako;

Mahājaṭā vijaṭesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.64.

With great ignorance cut off, the relinquisher of great attachment, who has disentangled the great tangle; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāsūlavivattako, mahāsallavinodano;

Mahāsavaṃ byantikaro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.65.

Remover of the great stake, dispeller of the great dart, who has made an end to the great taints; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāparakkamī sūro, sabbathā aparājayo;

Accantaabhayo vīro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.66.

Greatly valiant and heroic, in every way unconquered, the utterly fearless hero; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahātaṇhāvītivatto, mahāsokapanūdano;

Mahādukkhamatikkanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.67.

He who has overcome great craving, who has dispelled great sorrow, who has transcended great suffering; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāmānamatikkamo, mārasenanimmaddano;

Kammaklesaṃ nijjaresi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.68.

He who has overcome great conceit, the crusher of Māra's army, who has worn away the defilements of kamma; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahābhavodadhittiṇṇo, pāpaṇṇavapāraṅgato;

Mahāsaṃsaraṇātigo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.69.

He who has crossed the great ocean of becoming, who has gone to the far shore of the sea of evil, who has transcended the great wandering; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāsaṃyojanātīto, mahāvaṭṭavināsako;

Mahāsaṃsāroghatiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.70.

He who has transcended the great fetters, the destroyer of the great round, who has crossed the great flood of saṃsāra; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahārāgavirañjako, mahādosanimmaddako;

Mahāsotaṃ visosesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.71.

He who is detached from great passion, the crusher of great aversion, who has dried up the great stream; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāmettāvihārī yo, mahāpuñño mahāraho;

Mahākaruṇāsāgaro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.72.

He who dwells in great loving-kindness, of great merit and great worth, an ocean of great compassion; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahābhavasindhulaṅghī, bhavamutto bhavantagū;

Sokasūlasamucchinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.73.

He who has crossed the great ocean of becoming, freed from becoming, gone to the end of becoming, who has utterly cut off the stake of sorrow; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāvanapathātīto, mahākantārapāragū;

Mahābhavoghanittiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.74.

He who has gone beyond the great forest path, gone to the far shore of the great wilderness, crossed over the great flood of becoming; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāmaggaphalappatto, supatto amatodadhī;

Bhavacakkaṃ vibhañjesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.75.

He who has attained the great fruit of the path, who has well reached the deathless ocean, who has shattered the wheel of becoming; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāsubodhisampanno, muttimaggasubhāvito;

Bhavabandhanaṃ bhañjesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.76.

Endowed with great perfect awakening, who has well developed the path to liberation, who has broken the bonds of becoming; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahānandīsamucchinno, mahākilesanissaṭo;

Mahāpāpoghamuttiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.77.

He who has utterly cut off great delight, who has escaped from the great defilements, who has crossed beyond the great flood of evil; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāpihāparikkhīṇo, mahāsaṃyojane nudo;

Sabbāsave vinodesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.78.

He in whom great longing is exhausted, the dispeller of the great fetters, who has removed all the taints; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mohatimiraṃ bhañjesi, suriyo’va pabhaṅkaro;

Mahājutiṃ pabhāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.79.

He who shattered the darkness of delusion, the light-maker like the sun, who shone forth with great brilliance; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahābhayaṃ padālesi, chambhacchinno bhayanudo;

Mahādukkhakkhandhanudo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.80.

He who dispelled great fear, who has cut off trembling, the dispeller of fear, the remover of the great mass of suffering; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāsattuṃ vināsesi, sattidhārī mahābalī;

Mahāsiddho mahesakkho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.81.

He who destroyed the great foe, the wielder of power, greatly strong, the greatly accomplished one of great influence; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahātamaṃ panudi ca, mahājotipabhāsako;

Ādicco viya dīpesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.82.

He who dispelled the great darkness, the illuminator of great light, who shone like the sun; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahābhogapariccāgī, tiṇṇamahātaṇhaṇṇavo;

Mohudadhiṃ pāraṅgato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.83.

He who has abandoned great possessions, who has crossed the great ocean of craving, who has gone to the far shore of the ocean of delusion; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahābhiññābalappatto, mahāpadhānanāyako;

Mahābandhanavimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.84.

He who has attained the power of the great direct knowledges, the leader in the great striving, who is freed from the great bonds; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahābodhimahaṇṇavo, mahāsambodhisāgaro;

Mahāñāṇamahodadhi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.85.

A great ocean of great enlightenment, an ocean of perfect self-enlightenment, a great ocean of great knowledge; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahābhisakko yatīndo, taṇhārogatikicchako;

Dukkhato bahū mocesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.86.

The great physician, lord of ascetics, the healer of the disease of craving, who has freed many from suffering; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāmahimāmaṇḍito, chabbaṇṇaraṃsidhārako;

Mahāpabhāya byāpako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.87.

Adorned with great majesty, bearer of the six-colored rays, pervading all with great radiance; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahākaruṇāsampatto, sadā mettāya’bhirato;

Mahājanahitarato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.88.

Endowed with great compassion, always delighting in loving-kindness, devoted to the welfare of the multitude—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāmicchādiṭṭhiṃ hantā, mahāsaddhammuddesako;

Mahāantakupacchinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.89.

Slayer of great wrong view, expounder of the great true Dhamma, he who has cut off the great End-Maker—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāsokasallakatto, mahākhīlavisodhano;

Mahāsantikaro loke, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.90.

Extractor of the great dart of sorrow, purifier of the great obstructions, maker of great peace in the world—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāpāpapaṅkaṃ dhotā, mahādukkhavimocako;

Mahāsajjanaṃ sodhesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.91.

He who has washed away the great mire of evil, the great liberator from suffering, who has purified the virtuous multitude—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāantakahantā yo, maccusenavināsako;

Mahātaṇhaṃ vinodesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.92.

He who is the slayer of the great End-Maker, the destroyer of the army of Death, who has dispelled great craving—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahādiṭṭhiṃ viddhaṃsesi, pāpapuññasamūhato;

Mānaṃ māyañca maddesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.93.

He who has destroyed great wrong view, who has uprooted both evil and merit, who has crushed conceit and deceit—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāmohaṃ vināsesi, mahārāganirodhako;

Mahādosaṃ niddhaṃsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.94.

He who has destroyed great delusion, the bringer of cessation to great lust, who has destroyed great aversion—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Vicitradhammakathiko, abbhutamagguddesako;

Mahakkhāto mahākathī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.95.

Expounder of the variegated Dhamma, teacher of the wonderful path, greatly renowned and a great speaker—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāvādī kammavādī, mahāsakyamunīvaro;

Munipuṅgavo munindo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.96.

The great speaker, proponent of kamma, the excellent Great Sage of the Sakyans, bull among sages and lord of sages—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāmohanisānāsī, mahāpabhā vitthārako;

Mahājuṇhā pajjotesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.97.

Destroyer of the great night of delusion, spreader of great radiance, who illuminates with great moonlight—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahā’vijjānisā hantā, vijjāraṃsivibhūsito;

Mahādhammābhā vikari, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.98.

Slayer of the great night of ignorance, adorned with the rays of true knowledge, who spread the great light of the Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāmohaṃ nimmaddesi, māyājālavimocano;

Micchāvādaṃ pamaddesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.99.

He who has crushed great delusion, the liberator from the net of deceit, who has crushed wrong speech—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Āsavasotaṃ sosesi, mahāvyāpādamaddano;

Mohamahodadhitiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.100.

He who has dried up the stream of taints, the crusher of great ill will, who has crossed the great ocean of delusion—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāmohamatikkanto, mahāsaṅkāvinodano;

Mahābhavoghanittiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.101.

He who has transcended great delusion, the dispeller of great doubt, who has crossed over the great flood of becoming—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pāṇātipātā virato, cattadaṇḍo ahiṃsako;

Amitamettavāridhi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.102.

He who has abstained from killing living beings, who has laid down the rod, the non-violent one, an ocean of immeasurable loving-kindness—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Adinnādānā virato, aniccho apariggaho;

Sabbassa cāgī virāgī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.103.

He who has abstained from taking what is not given, desireless and without possessions, the renouncer of all, the dispassionate one—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Abrahmacariyātīto, kāmabhogapariccajo;

Māradhītāmānaṃ maddi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.104.

He who has gone beyond unchastity, the abandoner of sensual pleasures, who crushed the pride of Māra's daughters—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kāyānurakkhī sudanto, vācānurakkhī subbato;

Mahāsīlena sampanno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.105.

Guarding his body, well-tamed; guarding his speech, of good vows; endowed with great virtue—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sammāājīvasampanno, suddhācaraṇacārako;

Sīlasiromaṇi sāmī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.106.

Perfect in right livelihood, practicing pure conduct, the crest-jewel of virtue, the Lord—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Subharo sīlasampanno, suddhācārasiromaṇī;

Thāmavā javasampanno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.107.

Easily supported, endowed with virtue, the crest-jewel of pure conduct, strong and endowed with energy—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Avikappo avitakko, avicāro acintako;

Cittaekaggatāppatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.108.

Without thought-construction, without initial thought, without sustained thought, unthinking; he who has attained one-pointedness of mind—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Indriyāni surakkhesi, sato saṃvaramānaso;

Sammāsamāhitacitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.109.

He who guarded his sense faculties well, mindful and restrained in mind, with his mind rightly concentrated—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pabalapañño paññaññū, gambhīrapaññāsobhano;

Sampannapañño paññakkho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.110.

Mighty in wisdom, a knower of wisdom, resplendent with profound wisdom, perfect in wisdom, the eye of wisdom—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pahūtapañño supañño, puthulapañño paññavā;

Paguṇañāṇo ñāṇakkho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.111.

Of abundant wisdom, of good wisdom, of broad wisdom, the one possessing wisdom, skilled in knowledge, the eye of knowledge—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Tikkhapañño khippapañño, puṇṇapañño paññāpabhū;

Paññānātho paññāsāmī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.112.

Of sharp wisdom, of quick wisdom, of full wisdom, a master of wisdom, the Lord of wisdom, the Master of wisdom—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhuvapañño ṭhitapañño, thirapañño paññādado;

Pariyodātapañño yo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.113.

Of steadfast wisdom, of established wisdom, of firm wisdom, the giver of wisdom; he whose wisdom is utterly pure—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paññāya pāramīppatto, anantapaññasekharo;

Puññapāramīsampanno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.114.

He who has attained the perfection of wisdom, crowned with endless wisdom, endowed with the perfection of merit—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paññādhanī paññābalī, paññāsīlasamāhito;

Paññāpati paññādhārī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.115.

Rich in wisdom, powerful in wisdom, composed in wisdom and virtue, lord of wisdom, bearer of wisdom—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paññavanto sudhīmanto, sīdhapañño paññānidhi;

Paññāvāridhi paññagū, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.116.

Wise and very intelligent, of firm wisdom, a treasury of wisdom, an ocean of wisdom, one who has gone to the end of wisdom—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paguṇapaññākusalo, anantapaññavā vibhū;

Seṭṭhapañño jeṭṭhapañño, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.117.

Skilled in proficient wisdom, possessed of endless wisdom, the sovereign; of foremost wisdom, of pre-eminent wisdom—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paripuṇṇapañño puṇṇo, pavarapaññapāragū;

Paññissaro ñāṇissaro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.118.

Of complete wisdom, the full one, gone to the far shore of excellent wisdom, sovereign of wisdom, sovereign of knowledge—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Accantaamalapañño, sampuṇṇapaṭibhānavā;

Paṭivedhapaññādhārī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.119.

Of utterly stainless wisdom, perfectly endowed with ready wit, the bearer of penetrating wisdom—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Anomapañño anomo, bhūripañño paññavaro;

Parisuddhapañño suddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.120.

Of peerless wisdom, the peerless one; of vast wisdom, excellent in wisdom; of perfectly pure wisdom, the pure one—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Nibbedhikapañño nātho, paṭibodhapañño paṭū;

Visuddhapañño saṃsuddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.121.

Of penetrative wisdom, the protector; of awakening wisdom, the clever one; of pure wisdom, the thoroughly pure one—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paññācakkhu ñāṇacakkhu, buddhacakkhu sucakkhumā;

Samantacakkhu sampuṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.122.

The eye of wisdom, the eye of knowledge, the Buddha-eye, the one with the clear eye, the universal eye, the complete one—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pabalapaññāsaṃyutto, tikkhamedho medhāmayo;

Puṇṇābhiññā aññātāvī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.123.

Endowed with powerful wisdom, sharp-witted and made of wisdom, of full direct knowledge, the final knower—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paññāvudhena sampanno, ajeyyo ajito pabhū;

Jino ajini pāpimaṃ, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.124.

Endowed with the weapon of wisdom, invincible, unconquered, the mighty one; the Conqueror who conquered the Evil One—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Asaṅkhatamanuppatto, paññā’bharaṇabhūsito;

Paramatthaṃ paññāpesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.125.

He who has attained the Unconditioned, adorned with the ornament of wisdom, who made known the ultimate truth—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paññavā paññaṃ pasāresi, ñāṇī ñāṇasaṃvaḍḍhano;

Adhammadhaṃsako dhammī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.126.

The wise one who spread wisdom, the knowing one who increases knowledge, the destroyer of unrighteousness, the righteous one—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paññāsekharo paññindo, ñāṇindo ñāṇasekharo;

Dhammasekharo dhammindo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.127.

The crest of wisdom and the lord of wisdom, the lord of knowledge and the crest of knowledge, the crest of the Dhamma and the lord of the Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Javanapañño jitatto, sabbaganthappamocano;

Gaṇṭhimutto guttadvāro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.128.

Of swift wisdom, with self conquered, the liberator from all bonds, released from knots, with sense doors guarded—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bahupañño bahukārī, bahuñāṇī bahuguṇo;

Bahulaguṇasampanno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.129.

Of much wisdom and greatly helpful, of much knowledge and many virtues, endowed with abundant virtues—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paññāvimuttisampanno, mohātīto dukkhātigo;

Kilesasallakantako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.130.

Endowed with liberation by wisdom, gone beyond delusion and gone beyond suffering, the extractor of the dart of the defilements—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bodhihadayo sambuddho, paññindo paññamānaso;

Ñāṇacetaso ñāṇābho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.131.

The Perfectly Enlightened One with a heart of enlightenment, the lord of wisdom with a mind of wisdom; with a mind of knowledge, radiant with knowledge—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhuvasīlo dhuvacitto, dhuvamedho dhuvaṅgato;

Dhuvamutto dhuvaladdho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.132.

Steadfast in virtue, steadfast in mind, steadfast in wisdom, gone to the steadfast state; steadfastly liberated, having attained the steadfast—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sīlabalī cittabalī, paññābalī aññābalī;

Dhammabalī dhīrabalī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.133.

Powerful in virtue, powerful in mind, powerful in wisdom, powerful in final knowledge; powerful in the Dhamma, powerful in steadfastness—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sīladhanī cittadhanī, paññādhanī aññādhanī;

Dhammadhanī dhīradhanī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.134.

Wealthy in virtue, wealthy in mind, wealthy in wisdom, wealthy in final knowledge; wealthy in the Dhamma, wealthy in steadfastness—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kāyāsaṃvarasaṃyato, saṃyatavācāsaṃvaro;

Cittasaṃvara saṃyato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.135.

Restrained with bodily restraint, restrained with verbal restraint, restrained with mental restraint—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sīlaggo samādhippatto, paññappatto subhāvito;

Dhammasudhārasaṃ pāyī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.136.

Foremost in virtue, having attained concentration, having attained wisdom, well-developed; the drinker of the nectar-essence of the Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Susīlo samādhippatto, paññālaṅkāra’laṅkato;

Dhammābharaṇabhūsito, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.137.

Of good virtue, having attained concentration, adorned with the ornament of wisdom, embellished with the adornment of the Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sammāsamādhi’laṅkito, sīlapaññavibhūsato;

Sabbābhiññābalappatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.138.

Adorned with right concentration, embellished with virtue and wisdom; having attained the power of all the direct knowledges—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Suddhasīlo suddhacitto, suddhapañño suddhamano;

Suddhadhammaṃ pakāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.139.

Of pure virtue, of pure mind, of pure wisdom, of pure thought; he proclaimed the pure Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sīlavā susamāhito, paññavā yasavā isi;

Lokavissuto vidito, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.140.

The virtuous, well-composed, wise, and renowned sage; famed in the world and celebrated—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sīlasamādhisampanno, vidhuro paññapuṅgavo;

Sabbesaṃ sabbaṃ bodhesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.141.

Endowed with virtue and concentration, detached, a bull among the wise; he awakened all beings to all things—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Anantañāṇī nijjhānī, ñāṇasīsacūḷāmaṇi;

Ñāṇakkho narapāmokkho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.142.

Possessing infinite knowledge, the contemplative, the crest-jewel on the head of knowledge; the eye of knowledge, the foremost among men—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Nāyakānaṃ varo nātho, ñāṇiko ñāṇapuṇṇiko;

Ñāṇasamattho ñāṇaṭṭho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.143.

The best of leaders, the protector; the wise one, full of knowledge; competent in knowledge, established in knowledge—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ñāṇarāmo ñāṇarato, gambhīrañāṇakovido;

Ñāṇadassanasampanno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.144.

Delighting in knowledge, finding pleasure in knowledge, skilled in profound knowledge; endowed with knowledge and vision—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ñāṇasobhito ñāṇaggū, ñāṇa’laṅkāra’laṅkato;

Ñāṇasiromaṇi ñāṇī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.145.

Resplendent with knowledge, foremost in knowledge, adorned with the ornament of knowledge; the crest-jewel of knowledge, the wise one—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ñāṇavanto ñāṇavaro, ñāṇaneru suññāṇavā;

Ñāṇasindhu ñāṇodadhi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.146.

Possessing knowledge, the best in knowledge, the Meru of knowledge, possessor of excellent knowledge; the ocean of knowledge, the sea of knowledge—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ñāṇamoli ñāṇamuddho, ñāṇadīpo ñāṇasikho;

Ñāṇameru ñāṇasiṅgo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.147.

The diadem of knowledge, the peak of knowledge, the lamp of knowledge, the pinnacle of knowledge; the Meru of knowledge, the horn of knowledge—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Gambhīraññāṇī medhāvī, tikkhaññāṇī vicakkhaṇo;

Aññāṇamūlaṃ bhañjesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.148.

Possessing profound knowledge, intelligent, possessing sharp knowledge, discerning; he shattered the root of ignorance—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbañāṇī sabbaññāto, satthā sammapavattako;

Suddhadhammaṃ vitthāresi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.149.

The all-knower, the knower of all, the Teacher, the one who sets things going rightly; he expounded the pure Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbaññutañāṇapatto, sabbaviññū vināyako;

Nibbānasukhasampatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.150.

Having attained the knowledge of omniscience, the all-knower, the guide; having attained the bliss of Nibbāna—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbaññū sabbañāṇiko, sabbato maññanājaho;

Sabbamathitavikkhīṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.151.

The omniscient one, possessing all knowledge, one who has abandoned conceiving in every way; one for whom all agitations are destroyed and scattered—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbañāṇādhipatiko, samattho pavaro pabhū;

Sabbato ñāṇadassāvī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.152.

The sovereign of all knowledge, capable, excellent, and powerful; seeing with knowledge in every way—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dasabalādhipo nāgo, amitañāṇādhipati;

Bahuṃ iddhiṃ anuppatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.153.

The lord of the ten powers, the great being, the lord of immeasurable knowledge; having attained great psychic power—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paṭivedhañāṇayutto, māyāpaṭapacchedako;

Avijjaṃ paridhaṃsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.154.

Endowed with the knowledge of penetration, the cutter of the veil of illusion; he utterly destroyed ignorance—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Muttiñāṇaṃ gavesi yo, maggasaccapakāsako;

Magga’kkhāyī maggavidū, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.155.

He who sought the knowledge of release, the proclaimer of the truth of the path; the declarer of the path, the knower of the path—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbāññāṇaṃ vināsesi, mahāpaññānamuttamo;

Bodhiñāṇamahāsindhu, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.156.

He destroyed all ignorance, the supreme among those with great wisdom; the great ocean of the knowledge of enlightenment—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sammādassanasampatto, sammāñāṇapatiṭṭhito;

Sammāvimuttijitatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.157.

Having attained right vision, established in right knowledge; with a self conquered through right liberation—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Arahā vijjāsampanno, ñāṇasikharasekharo;

Paramaṃ sukhaṃ sampatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.158.

The Arahant, accomplished in true knowledge, the crest-jewel on the summit of knowledge; having attained the supreme bliss—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paramañāṇasampanno, pavarapaññāpuṇṇiko;

Sammāvimuttiṃ sampatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.159.

Endowed with supreme knowledge, full of excellent wisdom; having attained right liberation—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbadassāvī suññāṇī, sabbakiccesu paṇḍito;

Sabbadhi suguṇupeto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.160.

The all-seeing one, possessing excellent knowledge, wise in all endeavors; endowed with good qualities in every respect—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Saṃvedanā saṃveditvā, sammāsambodhiṃ bodhayi;

Sokavigato sumato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.161.

Having experienced feelings, he awakened to perfect full enlightenment; free from sorrow, of wise mind—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Vedanāsu vītagijjho, vedanāmutto sabbathā;

Vedagū ca vedantagū, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.162.

Free from greed for feelings, completely released from feelings; a master of feeling and one gone to the end of feeling—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Vissāsabhūmi sattānaṃ, andhānaṃ nayanūpamo;

Arakkheyyo ārakkhako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.163.

A ground of confidence for beings, like an eye for the blind; needing no protector, yet a protector—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dukkhasakkhī dukkhakkhīṇo, dukkhavidū dukkhantagū;

Dukkhappahīno dukkhaññū, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.164.

A witness to suffering, one for whom suffering is destroyed, a knower of suffering, one gone to the end of suffering; one who has abandoned suffering, an understander of suffering—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kāyasakkhī cittasakkhī, vedanānusakkhī sukhī;

Dhammānusakkhī susakkhī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.165.

The body-witness, the mind-witness, the blissful witness of feelings; the witness of phenomena, the good witness—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Santakāyo santavāco, santacitto samāhito;

Sabbūpadhivūpasanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.166.

Peaceful in body, peaceful in speech, peaceful in mind, and well-composed; with all substrates of existence pacified—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sammāsamāhitacitto, sammāsīle patiṭṭhito;

Sammāpaññāparipuṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.167.

With mind rightly concentrated, established in right virtue; completely full of right wisdom—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Nikhilaniṭṭha’dhigamo, accāraddhavipassako;

Samattasantisampatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.168.

One who has attained the complete conclusion, extremely energetic in insight; having attained perfected peace—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbakāyaṃ saṃveditvā, sabbadhammavipassako;

Bhavasaṃsāraṃ riñcesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.169.

Having experienced the whole body, one who has insight into all phenomena; he abandoned the round of existence—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbadā satisampanno, sampajaññarato sadā;

Santataṃ samaṇo sāmī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.170.

Always endowed with mindfulness, always delighting in clear comprehension; the ever-serene ascetic, the master—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Saccadassanadassāvī, saccadhammavipassako;

Saccacakkaṃ pavattesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.171.

The seer of the vision of truth, one with insight into the true Dhamma; he set in motion the Wheel of Truth—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Jhānasokhummasampanno, attapaṇidhipāragū;

Bhavādīnavadassāvī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.172.

Endowed with the subtlety of the jhānas, one who has gone beyond self-aspiration; the seer of the danger in existence—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Cakkhumanto vatavanto, sīlavanto susīlavā;

Medhāvanto paññāvanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.173.

Possessing the eye, possessing observances, possessing virtue, of excellent virtue; possessing intelligence, possessing wisdom—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Vaṇṇavanto guṇavanto, jutimanto jutidharo;

Yasavanto kittimanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.174.

Possessing splendor, possessing virtue, possessing radiance, the bearer of radiance; possessing fame, possessing renown—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Satimanto yati santo, matimanto medhāvino;

Patāpavanto dhīmanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.175.

Mindful, a peaceful ascetic, intelligent, and wise; majestic and steadfast—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Yogavanto khemavanto, paṭibhānavanto pabhū;

Hirīmano sirīmano, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.176.

The yogi, the secure one, the eloquent one, the powerful lord; possessing moral shame, possessing glory—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Attadanto anussado, dhīradhārī dhurandharo;

Dhitimanto dhīsampanno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.177.

Self-tamed, without insolence, a bearer of wisdom, a bearer of the burden; possessing fortitude, endowed with wisdom—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Thāmavanto iddhimanto, dayāvanto dayālayo;

Kantimanto rūpavanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.178.

Possessing might, possessing psychic power, possessing compassion, an abode of compassion; possessing radiance, possessing beauty—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Khantimanto santimanto, bhagavanto sukevalī;

Ñāyavanto nayavanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.179.

Possessing patience, possessing peace, the Blessed One, the perfectly accomplished one; possessing the right method, possessing guidance—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bodhimanto buddhimanto, bodhiñāṇo bodhiguṇo;

Bodhidhammamanuppatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.180.

Possessing enlightenment, possessing intelligence, with knowledge of enlightenment, with the quality of enlightenment; having attained the Dhamma of enlightenment—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Muttadoso mettāvanto, karuṇāvanto modito;

Sabbathā upekkhāvanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.181.

Released from faults, possessing loving-kindness, possessing compassion, and rejoicing; in every way possessing equanimity—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Yatavanto yatacārī, khamavanto sukhemino;

Tapavanto tathavanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.182.

Possessing restraint, of restrained conduct, possessing patience, possessing happiness; possessing ardor, possessing truthfulness—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pabhāvavanto puriso, patāpī paṭibhānavā;

Sadatthapaṭidīpako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.183.

The majestic person, powerful and eloquent; the illuminator of the true goal—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Saṅgachinno raṅgachinno, rāgachinno chinnaratī;

Nandīchinno taṇhāchinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.184.

One who has cut off association, cut off passion, cut off lust, cut off delight; one who has cut off joy, cut off craving—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Chinnāsaṅko chinnātaṅko, chinnākaṅkho chinnaṅgaṇo;

Chinnasaṃyojanā sabbe, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.185.

One who has cut off fear, cut off anxiety, cut off longing, cut off defilement; for whom all fetters are severed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Chinnāsaṅgo chinnāsatto, chinnādāno chinnāvilo;

Chinnalitto chinnālambo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.186.

One who has cut off attachment, cut off clinging, cut off grasping, cut off turmoil; one who is unsmeared, without support—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Chinnakāmo chinnakodho, chinnakopo chinnamado;

Chinnakleso chinnakhobho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.187.

One who has cut off sensual desire, cut off anger, cut off wrath, cut off pride; one who has cut off defilements, cut off agitation—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Chinnapāpo chinnatāpo, chinniccho chinnasaṃsayo;

Chinnasoto chinnasneho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.188.

One who has cut off evil, cut off torment, cut off desire, cut off doubt; one who has cut the stream, cut off affection—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Chinnabhogo chinnayogo, chinnabhīti chinnabhayo;

Chinnakhandho chinnachando, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.189.

One who has cut off enjoyment, cut off the yokes, cut off fear, cut off danger; one who has cut off the aggregates, cut off desire—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhinnāsaṅko bhinnātaṅko, bhinnākaṅkho bhinnussuko;

One for whom fear is shattered, anxiety is shattered, longing is shattered, and worry is shattered;

Bhinnābhirato bhinnāso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.190.

one for whom delight is shattered and hope is shattered—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhinnakhobho bhinnadukkho, bhinnakleso bhinnakhilo;

Bhinnābhimāno bhinnejo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.191.

One for whom agitation is shattered, suffering is shattered, defilements are shattered, and impediments are shattered; one for whom conceit is shattered and craving is shattered—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Lobhabhinno lolabhinno, rosabhinno bhinnaraṇo;

Nehabhinno khedabhinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.192.

One for whom greed is shattered, covetousness is shattered, anger is shattered, and conflict is shattered; one for whom affection is shattered and distress is shattered—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhogabhinno sokabhinno, rogabhinno bhinnarajo;

Sūlabhinno sallabhinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.193.

One for whom enjoyments are shattered, grief is shattered, sickness is shattered, and the dust of passion is shattered; one for whom the stake is shattered and the dart is shattered—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Saṅgabhinno raṅgabhinno, rāgabhinno bhinnaratī;

Nandībhinno taṇhābhinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.194.

He who has shattered association, shattered worldly pleasure, shattered passion, and shattered delights; who has shattered enjoyment and shattered craving—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbabhavābādhabhinno, bhinnamahāmohatamo;

Ādhibhinno vyādhibhinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.195.

He who has shattered the affliction in all states of being, who has shattered the great darkness of delusion; who has shattered mental anguish and shattered physical disease—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Khinnagiddho khinnamuddho, khinnābhilāso khinniccho;

Khinnalobho khinnābhijjho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.196.

He whose greed is destroyed, whose bewilderment is destroyed, whose longings are destroyed, and whose wishes are destroyed; whose covetousness is destroyed and whose avarice is destroyed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Āsākhinno issākhinno, ejākhinno khinnaiṇo;

Chandakhinno bandhakhinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.197.

He whose hope is destroyed, whose envy is destroyed, whose agitation is destroyed, and whose debt is destroyed; whose desire is destroyed and whose bonds are destroyed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kappakhinno kālakhinno, kilesakhinno sabbathā;

Mūlakhinno sūlakhinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.198.

He for whom concepts are destroyed, for whom time is destroyed, for whom defilements are in every way destroyed; for whom the roots are destroyed and the stakes are destroyed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Tiṇṇapāpo tiṇṇapuñño, tiṇṇamoho tiṇṇamalo;

Tiṇṇavikāro tiṇṇīṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.199.

He who has crossed over evil and crossed over merit, crossed over delusion and crossed over stain; who has crossed over change and crossed over debt—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Tiṇṇadoso tiṇṇadoho, tiṇṇarāgo tiṇṇamamo;

Tiṇṇatāpo tiṇṇatāso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.200.

He who has crossed over hatred and crossed over malevolence, crossed over passion and crossed over possessiveness; who has crossed over torment and crossed over craving—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Tiṇṇajāti tiṇṇamaccu, tiṇṇaloko tiṇṇabhavo;

Tiṇṇogho tiṇṇasaṃsāro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.201.

He who has crossed over birth and crossed over death, crossed over the world and crossed over existence; who has crossed over the flood and crossed over saṃsāra—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Cuṇṇamoho cuṇṇamakkho, cuṇṇamāno cuṇṇamado;

Cuṇṇarāgo cuṇṇadoso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.202.

He who has crushed delusion and crushed detraction, crushed conceit and crushed intoxication; who has crushed lust and crushed hatred—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Cuṇṇakodho cuṇṇakopo, cuṇṇakhobho cuṇṇabhayo;

Cuṇṇasūlo cuṇṇasallo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.203.

He who has crushed anger and crushed rage, crushed agitation and crushed fear; who has crushed the stakes and crushed the darts—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pāpacuṇṇo puññacuṇṇo, ahaṃcuṇṇo cuṇṇamamo;

Kaṅkhācuṇṇo saṅkācuṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.204.

He who has crushed evil and crushed merit, who has crushed the 'I' and crushed the 'mine'; who has crushed doubt and crushed uncertainty—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Saṃsāracakkaṃ cuṇṇesi, bhavarajjūnikantako;

Sabbasaṅkhāravicuṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.205.

He who crushed the wheel of saṃsāra, the cutter of the rope of existence; he for whom all formations are shattered—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Nimmamo nirahaṃkāro, nirālambo nirālayo;

Nippapañco nirārambho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.206.

Without possessiveness, without egoism, without support, and without attachment; free from conceptual proliferation, without undertakings—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Nirāsatto nirāsaṃso, nissaṃsayo nirussuko;

Nirāsavo nirapekkho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.207.

Without attachment, without expectation, without doubt, and without anxiety; without taints and without dependence—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nirāsaṅko nirātaṅko, nirākaṅkho niraṅgaṇo;

Nissaṅkiliṭṭho nikkaṅkho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.208.

Without fear, without distress, without longing, and without blemish; unstained and free from doubt—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nhāto dhoto niddhoto, nibbuto nittharaṇiko;

Nimmalo nirodhappatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.209.

Bathed, washed, and cleansed; quenched and one who has crossed over; spotless and one who has attained cessation—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nibbhayo naranisabho, nikkampo narakesarī;

Narakuñjaro nicchambhī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.210.

The fearless one, the bull among men; the unshaken one, the lion among men; the elephant among men, the untrembling one—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nirāgo cāpi nikkopo, nicchuddho cāpi nimmado;

Niddukkho cāpi nissoko, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.211.

Without lust and without anger, without baseness and without pride; without suffering and without sorrow—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nāsako titthiye nānā, nānāvādaviddhaṃsako;

Nānādiṭṭhiṃ nivāresi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.212.

Destroyer of the various sectarians, demolisher of diverse doctrines; he who checked diverse views—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nandikkhayo nibbanatho, niricchako nirindhano;

Nissaṭṭhamāno nittaṇho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.213.

The destroyer of delight, free from craving, without longing, and without fuel; he who has abandoned conceit, free from thirst—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nippaṭibaddho nibaddho, nirābādho nirabbudo;

Niraggalo nikkaṇṭako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.214.

Unfettered and unbound, free from affliction and free from tumors; without barriers and without thorns—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nirupatāpo nippāpo, nippipāso nirākulo;

Nippariphando niriñjo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.215.

Without vexation and without evil, without thirst and untroubled; unagitated and unperturbed—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nihatamāno nepuñño, nayaniyāmañāṇiko;

Nittiṇṇakaṅkho nissaṅko, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.216.

He whose conceit is struck down, the proficient one, the knower of the guiding, fixed course; he who has crossed beyond doubt, free from hesitation—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nippagabbho nippaṭigho, nātimāno nirāvilo;

Nibbānapatto nikhilo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.217.

Not insolent and without resistance, not conceited and without turbidity; he who has attained Nibbāna, the one wholly complete—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nirosamāno nivero, nikkodho ca nikkupito;

Niyāmappatto pamudo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.218.

Without resentment and without enmity, without anger and unprovoked; he who has attained the fixed course, the joyful one—to that Buddha I bow down.

Sunipuṇo nayaladdho, nirāgu nibbānagato;

Namucidheyyamaccago, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.219.

Most skillful, one who has attained the guide, free from defilements, gone to Nibbāna; he who has overcome the domain of Namuci—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nillobhamāno nisneho, nikkamano nikkāmano;

Nirabhimānī nilluddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.220.

Without greed and without affection, one who has gone forth and is without desire; without conceit and not cruel—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nirāgamāno ninneho, nandīrāgavināsako;

Niricchamāno nillaggo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.221.

Without passion and without affection, the destroyer of delight and lust; without desire and unattached—to that Buddha I bow down.

Niddosamāno nimmakkho, bhavanettinikantako;

Nikkasāvo nikkaluso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.222.

Without hatred and without hypocrisy, the cutter of the guide to existence; without stains and without defilement—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nikkilissako nikkuppo, niccalo ca niccañcalo;

Niccapalo ca nillolo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.223.

Without defilements and unprovoked, motionless and not unsteady; not fickle and not covetous—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nikkuho ca nikkuṭilo, nibbikāro nibbijjano;

Niggaṇṭho nirāparādho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.224.

Without deceit and without crookedness, unchanging and without slander; without knots and without offense—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nikkukkucco niruddhacco, nirāso ca bhavābhave;

Nibbicikiccho nibbijjo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.225.

Without remorse and without restlessness, without longing for any state of existence; free from doubt and free from aversion—to that Buddha I bow down.

Namucisenasūdako, animittarato yatī;

Nipuṇo nittiṇṇaogho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.226.

The destroyer of Namuci's army, the ascetic delighting in the signless; the skillful one who has crossed the floods—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nikkhīlo cāpi nissallo, nissūlo nibbānarato;

Nippamādo nibbandhano, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.227.

Without spiritual barrenness and without darts, without stakes and delighting in Nibbāna; vigilant and without bonds—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nirabhimāno nimmāno, nittaṇhamāno nicchalo;

Nikkaṅkhamāno nicchando, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.228.

Without arrogance and without conceit, with craving and conceit gone, without deceit; without doubt and without desire—to that Buddha I bow down.

Naravīro naradhīro, narājañño naravaro;

Naranāgo narasīho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.229.

The hero among men, the wise one among men, the noble steed among men, the best of men; the great one among men, the lion among men—to that Buddha I bow down.

Narajeṭṭho naraseṭṭho, narusabho naruttamo;

Naradammasusārathī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.230.

The foremost of men, the chief of men, the bull among men, the supreme among men; the excellent charioteer of persons to be tamed—to that Buddha I bow down.

Varanātho naranātho, lokanātho lokajino;

Devanātho brahmanātho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.231.

The excellent protector, the protector of humans, the protector of the world, the conqueror of the world; the protector of devas, the protector of Brahmās—to that Buddha I bow down.

Jhānanirato nijjhāyī, kilesavisanāsako;

Sukhumajhānasampanno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.232.

Delighting in jhāna, contemplative, destroyer of the poison of defilements; endowed with subtle jhāna—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nittaṇhamānaso nātho, sadā nimmalamānaso;

Alittamānaso cāgī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.233.

The protector whose mind is without craving, whose mind is ever stainless; with mind unsoiled, the generous one—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nunnajāti nunnamaccu, nunnaloko nunnabhavo;

Nunnavibhavo nunnogho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.234.

He who has driven out birth, driven out death, driven out the world, driven out existence; driven out non-existence, driven out the flood—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nunnāsaṅko nunnātaṅko, nunnāso ca nunnussuko;

Nunnābhirato nunniccho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.235.

He who has driven out fear, driven out distress, driven out hope, and driven out anxiety; driven out delight and driven out wishes—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nunnānutāpo nunnāgu, nunnadāho nunnāsavo;

Nunnamānābhimāno yo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.236.

He who has driven out remorse, driven out transgression, driven out burning, driven out the taints; he who has driven out conceit and arrogance—to that Buddha I bow down.

Lobhanudo lolanudo, rosanudo nunnamado;

Disanudo dessanudo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.237.

Dispeller of greed, dispeller of fickleness, dispeller of anger, he who has driven out pride; dispeller of hate, dispeller of aversion—to that Buddha I bow down.

Kammanudo klesanudo, kalinudo nunnamalo;

Sabbathā avijjānudo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.238.

Dispeller of kamma, dispeller of defilements, dispeller of strife, he who has driven out stains; the dispeller of ignorance in every way—to that Buddha I bow down.

Sabbabhavābādhanudo, nunnamahāmohatamo;

Ādhinudo vyādhinudo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.239.

Dispeller of the affliction in all states of being, he who has driven out the great darkness of delusion; dispeller of mental anguish, dispeller of physical disease—to that Buddha I bow down.

Lahupañño paññaṇṇavo, kāruṇo karuṇaṇṇavo;

Medhaṇṇavo mettaṇṇavo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.240.

Swift in wisdom, an ocean of wisdom; compassionate, an ocean of compassion; an ocean of intelligence, an ocean of loving-kindness—to that Buddha I bow down.

Ñāṇanidhi ñāṇaṇṇavo, dhammanidhi dhammaṇṇavo;

Guṇanidhi guṇaṇṇavo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.241.

A treasury of knowledge, an ocean of knowledge; a treasury of Dhamma, an ocean of Dhamma; a treasury of virtues, an ocean of virtues—to that Buddha I bow down.

Muttāsaṅgo muttāsaṅko, muttālambo muttālayo;

Muttāsaṃso muttāsatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.242.

Freed from clinging, freed from fear, freed from support, freed from attachment; freed from expectation, freed from passion—to that Buddha I bow down.

Muttalālaso muttindho, muttachambho muttadaro;

Muttamaccharo mutticcho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.243.

Freed from longing, freed from fuel, freed from trembling, freed from dread; freed from stinginess, freed from wishes—to that Buddha I bow down.

Muttakāmo muttataṇho, muttarāgo muttapiho;

Muttachando muttanandī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.244.

Freed from sensual desire, freed from craving, freed from passion, freed from longing; freed from desire, freed from delight—to that Buddha I bow down.

Muttagiddho muttaluddho, muttābhilāso muttiñjo;

Muttalobho muttābhijjho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.245.

Freed from greed, freed from covetousness, freed from longing, freed from agitation; freed from avarice, freed from covetousness—to that Buddha I bow down.

Jālamutto’va sakuṇo, muttāso yo bhavābhave;

Saṃyojanehi vimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.246.

Like a bird freed from a net, he is freed from longing for any state of existence; completely released from the fetters—to that Buddha I bow down.

Cetovimutto jhānavā, paññāvimutto paññavā;

Ubhatobhāga vimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.247.

Liberated in mind, possessing jhāna; liberated by wisdom, possessing wisdom; one liberated in both ways—to that Buddha I bow down.

Sabbāyatanehi mutto, munindo parinibbuto;

Jātisaṃsāravimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.248.

Freed from all sense bases, the chief of sages, fully extinguished; released from the cycle of births—to that Buddha I bow down.

Sabbakilesā vimutto, sabbadomanassacuto;

Sabbadohavinimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.249.

Completely freed from all defilements, fallen away from all dejection; completely released from all malevolence—to that Buddha I bow down.

Kāmesanā vinimutto, mutto cāpi bhavesanā;

Vibhavesanā vimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.250.

Completely released from the craving for sensual pleasures, freed also from the craving for existence; completely released from the craving for non-existence—to that Buddha I bow down.

Kāmiñjanā vinimutto, mutto cāpi bhaviñjanā;

Vibhaviñjanā vimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.251.

Completely released from sensual agitation, freed also from agitation about existence; completely released from agitation about non-existence—to that Buddha I bow down.

Kāmataṇhā vinimutto, bhavataṇhāvibhañjako;

Vibhavataṇhāvikkhīṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.252.

Completely released from craving for sensual pleasures, one who shatters craving for existence; with craving for non-existence exhausted—to that Buddha I bow down.

Sabbakāmarogamutto, bhavarogabhesajjagū;

Rāgarogavinimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.253.

Freed from the disease of all sensual pleasures, the physician for the disease of existence; completely released from the disease of passion—to that Buddha I bow down.

Dukkhamutto sukhappatto, attamutto anattagū;

Aniccamutto niccaññū, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.254.

Freed from suffering, attained to bliss; freed from self, knower of not-self; freed from the impermanent, knower of the permanent—to that Buddha I bow down.

Kodhamutto kopamutto, klesamutto muttakuho;

Chandamutto chalamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.255.

Freed from anger, freed from rage, freed from defilements, freed from deceit; freed from desire, freed from guile—to that Buddha I bow down.

Māyāmutto mohamutto, makkhamutto muttamado;

Mānamutto mucchāmutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.256.

Freed from illusion, freed from delusion, freed from hypocrisy, freed from pride; freed from conceit, freed from bewilderment—to that Buddha I bow down.

Dosamutto dohamutto, dessamutto muttadiso;

Diṭṭhimutto aghamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.257.

Freed from hatred, freed from malice, freed from what is odious, freed from animosity; freed from views, freed from misfortune—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Lobhamutto lolamutto, rosamutto muttaraṇo;

Khobhamutto khedamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.258.

Freed from greed, freed from fickleness, freed from anger, freed from strife; freed from agitation, freed from sorrow—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhogamutto sokamutto, rogamutto muttarajo;

Sūlamutto sallamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.259.

Freed from possessions, freed from sorrow, freed from disease, freed from defilement; freed from the stake, freed from the dart—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Saṅgamutto raṅgamutto, rāgamutto muttaratī;

Nandīmutto taṇhāmutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.260.

Freed from attachment, freed from worldly amusements, freed from passion, freed from sensual delight; freed from delight, freed from craving—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Iñjāmutto indhāmutto, icchāmutto muttapiho;

Edhamutto eḷamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.261.

Freed from wavering, freed from fuel, freed from desire, freed from longing; freed from kindling, freed from impurity—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ītimutto bhītimutto, chambhamutto muttabhayo;

Ādhimutto vyādhimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.262.

Freed from misfortune, freed from fear, freed from stupefaction, freed from terror; freed from mental anguish, freed from disease—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Gabbamutto dambhamutto, kūṭamutto muttadaro;

Ḍāhamutto dāhamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.263.

Freed from the womb, freed from deceit, freed from trickery, freed from distress; freed from torment, freed from burning—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kamyamutto kiccamutto, bhantimutto muttabhamo;

Vaṭṭamutto oghamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.264.

Freed from sensual desire, freed from duties, freed from delusion, freed from confusion; freed from the round of rebirths, freed from the flood—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Jātimutto maccumutto, lokamutto muttabhavo;

Nehamutto nettimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.265.

Freed from birth, freed from death, freed from the world, freed from becoming; freed from affection, freed from craving—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Āsāmutto issāmutto, ejāmutto muttaiṇo;

Vānamutto pihāmutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.266.

Freed from hope, freed from envy, freed from agitation, freed from debt; freed from craving, freed from longing—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sotamutto yogamutto, phassamutto muttaraso;

Mūlamutto bhāramutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.267.

Freed from the stream, freed from the yokes, freed from contact, freed from tastes; Freed from the roots, freed from the burdens, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Oramutto pāramutto, ghoramutto muttagaho;

Kappamutto kālamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.268.

Freed from this shore, freed from the far shore, freed from the dreadful, freed from grasping; Freed from the aeon, freed from time, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pāpamutto puññamutto, ahaṃmutto muttamamo;

Freed from evil, freed from merit, freed from 'I-making,' freed from 'mine-making';

Kaṅkhāmutto saṅkāmutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.269.

Freed from doubt, freed from uncertainty, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Thīnamutto middhamutto, tāpamutto muttupayo;

Freed from sloth, freed from torpor, freed from torment, freed from clinging;

Muttuttāpo muttuddhacco, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.270.

Freed from distress, freed from agitation, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Khandhamutto bandhamutto, kalimutto muttamalo;

Sabbathā pipāsāmutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.271.

Freed from the aggregates, freed from bonds, freed from demerit, freed from taints; In every way freed from thirst, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Vicāravitakkamutto, sadā sammāsamāhito;

Sammāpaṇihitacitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.272.

Freed from applied and sustained thought, ever rightly concentrated; With mind rightly directed, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Anvāhatacittamutto, mutto sabbabhayehi ca;

Bhīmamutto bhesmamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.273.

Freed from an afflicted mind, and freed from all fears; Freed from the terrible, freed from the dreadful, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Yo paññattiṃ ṭhapetvāna, paramatthassa dassano;

Vipallāsā vippamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.274.

He who, having established conventional designation, is a seer of the ultimate reality; Completely freed from distortions, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Abhāvitacittamutto, mutto sabbadukkhehi ca;

Sabbūpasaggā vimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.275.

Freed from an uncultivated mind, and freed from all suffering; Completely freed from all troubles, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ākulacittavimutto, sabbadā thiramānaso;

Aggalacittavimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.276.

Freed from a mind in turmoil, with a mind ever steadfast; Freed from a mind obstructed, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Abbudacittavimutto, sadā bhāvitamānaso;

Pamādacittapamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.277.

Freed from a clouded mind, with an ever-cultivated mind; Freed from a heedless mind, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Upadhicittavimutto, sadā niyyānamānaso;

Ussukacittavimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.278.

With a mind freed from the substrates, with a mind ever set on deliverance; With a mind freed from striving, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Apekkhācittavimutto, sadā cittanirālayo;

Ārambhacittavimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.279.

With a mind freed from longing, with a mind ever unattached; With a mind freed from exertion, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sammāvimutto sambuddho, suvimuttacitto munī;

Sammadaññāparimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.280.

Rightly freed, the Perfectly Enlightened One, the sage with a well-liberated mind; Completely freed through perfect knowledge, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Asaṅkiliṭṭhacitto yo, mutto sabbabhavehi ca;

Abhinivesābhimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.281.

He who has an undefiled mind, and is freed from all existences; Freed from adherence, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Tilokatimirā mutto, paññappabhāpabhāsako;

Tibhavataṇhā nittiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.282.

Freed from the darkness of the three worlds, an illuminator with the light of wisdom; Having crossed over craving for the three existences, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pamadappamutto byatto, bhāvanāratamānaso;

Bhavaṇṇavavinimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.283.

Freed from heedlessness, the wise one, with a mind delighting in development; Freed from the ocean of becoming, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Saṃsārasaṅkhārā mutto, vikāramuttamānaso;

Navasamussayamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.284.

Freed from the formations of saṃsāra, with a mind freed from distortion; Freed from new accumulations, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Jātivyādhijarāmutto, maraṇamutto mārajī;

Punabbhavavinimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.285.

Freed from birth, sickness, and old age, freed from death, the conqueror of Māra; Freed from renewed existence, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Asuciasuddhimutto, niccanimmalamānaso;

Subhāvitacitto suddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.286.

Freed from the impure and unclean, with a mind ever stainless; With a well-developed mind, purified, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Avijjāndhakāramutto, vijjālokapakāsako;

Anavasesañāṇaññū, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.287.

Freed from the darkness of ignorance, one who sheds the light of true knowledge; Knower of knowledge without remainder, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Āsattimutto alitto, āsajjāmuttacetaso;

Mohamānavinimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.288.

Freed from clinging, untainted, with a mind released from attachment; Freed from delusion and conceit, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhavabhogavippamutto, bhavasaṃyojanacchido;

Bhavabhītibhayātīto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.289.

Freed from the enjoyment of existence, the cutter of the fetters of becoming; Gone beyond the fear and dread of becoming, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kopakodhavinimutto, dosadohaṃ visosayī;

Sokasallaṃ vicuṇṇesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.290.

Freed from anger and fury, he dried up the stream of hatred; He shattered the dart of sorrow—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bījamutto balayutto, subujjhitā subuddhimā;

Gedhāmutto medhāyutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.291.

Freed from the seed of rebirth, endowed with strength, well-awakened, supremely wise; Freed from greed, endowed with wisdom—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbabhavābādhamutto, muttamahāmohatamo;

Bālyasaṭhatāvimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.292.

Freed from all ills of existence, freed from the great darkness of delusion; Freed from childishness and craftiness, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Tasamutto tāsamutto, santāsamutto sabbathā;

Sabbatassanavimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.293.

Freed from craving, freed from terror, in every way freed from anxiety; Completely released from all thirsting, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhavanettiyā vimutto, bhavoghamuttamānaso;

Sabbattha saṃyogamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.294.

Freed from the craving that guides to existence, with a mind released from the flood of becoming; Freed from all fetters, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbakāmayogamutto, sabbakāmagginibbuto;

Sabbakāmaratimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.295.

Freed from all sensual yokes, with all sensual fires extinguished; Released from all sensual delights, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbesānaṃ jaṭāmutto, vimutto sabbāsattiyā;

Sabbānusayāmutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.296.

Freed from all tangles, freed from every attachment; Freed from all latent tendencies, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbāsavā vinimutto, sadā alīnamānaso;

Sabbāsajjā vippamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.297.

Completely freed from all taints, with a mind ever unsluggish; Completely released from all clinging, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbātaṅkavinimutto, sīho abhayamānaso;

Sabbathā tassanamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.298.

Completely freed from all affliction, a lion with a fearless mind; In every way released from craving, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbāsaṅkā vinimutto, anavassutamānaso;

Sabbaṭhāne kicchāmutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.299.

Completely freed from all apprehension, with a mind without outflows; Released from trouble in all places, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbālambavinimutto, sabbathā bhavabhañjako;

Sabbānusayā vicchinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.300.

Completely freed from all supports, in every way the destroyer of existence; With all latent tendencies cut off, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbāpekkhā vippamutto, sadā alittacetaso;

Sabbābhijjhā vinimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.301.

Completely freed from all dependence, with a mind ever unstained; Completely released from all covetousness, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Iñjanāindhanāmutto, muttomohamadehi ca;

Sabbalobhavinimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.302.

Freed from agitation and fuel, and from delusion and intoxication; Completely freed from all greed, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbākkosā vinimutto, sampuṇṇamettamānaso;

Kuppanakujjhanamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.303.

Completely freed from all reviling, with a mind full of loving-kindness; Freed from irritation and resentment, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbathā saṅkopamutto, byāpādamutto sabbadā;

Sabbadhi paṭighamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.304.

In every way freed from agitation, always released from ill will; Freed from resistance everywhere, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbābhilāsā vimutto, ākaṅkhāmutto sabbathā;

Sabbalālasā vimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.305.

Freed from all longing, in every way released from desire; Freed from all greediness, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Virodhānurodhamutto, saṅkhobhamutto sabbadā;

Sabbānutāpā sumutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.306.

Freed from opposition and compliance, ever freed from agitation; Well freed from all remorse, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbadā sammosamutto, sammohamutto sabbathā;

Sabbadhi avijjāmutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.307.

Always freed from confusion, entirely released from delusion; Freed from ignorance everywhere, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbaṭhāne vibbhamamutto, vibbhantimutto sabbadhi;

Sabbavipallāsamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.308.

Freed from confusion in all places, everywhere freed from bewilderment; Freed from all distortions, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kaṭukakasāyāmutto, kakkasamutto sabbathā;

Sabbadā kalusāmutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.309.

Freed from the bitter and astringent, altogether freed from roughness; Always freed from turbidity, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbassādanā vimutto, saṃsaṭṭhamutto sabbadā;

Āsajjanā vinimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.310.

Freed from all enjoyment, ever freed from association; Completely released from clinging, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ahaṅkārā vinimutto, mamaṅkārā mutto munī;

Mānāvamānā vimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.311.

The sage, completely freed from 'I-making' and released from 'mine-making'; Freed from honor and dishonor, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbaganthavinimutto, palighamutto sabbadā;

Sabbābhibhū sabbamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.312.

Completely freed from all ties, always released from obstructions; Overcomer of all, freed from all, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbasārambhasumutto, papañcamutto sabbadā;

Sabbūpadhi vinimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.313.

Well freed from all contention, always released from proliferation; Completely freed from all substrates, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kohaññakoṭillamutto, cāpallamutto sabbathā;

Sabbathā viddesamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.314.

Freed from hypocrisy and crookedness, altogether freed from fickleness; Altogether freed from hatred, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Domanassā vinimutto, paḷāsamuttamānaso;

Sabbasantāpā sumutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.315.

Completely freed from displeasure, with a mind released from spite; Well freed from all torment, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbābhimānā vimutto, sabbatāpehi muccako;

Sabbadhi vikkhobhamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.316.

Freed from all conceit, released from every torment; Unshaken everywhere, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbanīvaraṇāmutto, siddho ekaggamānaso;

Sammāpañño sammāmutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.317.

Freed from all hindrances, accomplished, with one-pointed mind; Perfect in wisdom, perfectly liberated, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbāvilā vippamutto, suddho saṃsuddhacetaso;

Kasāvakalaṅkamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.318.

Completely freed from all turbidity, pure, with a perfectly pure mind; Released from the stain of defilement, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbabyāpādappamutto, sadā pasādamānaso;

Sabbūpanāhā vimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.319.

Freed from all ill will, with a mind ever serene; Completely released from all resentment, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbathāpamādamutto, sampajāno satiyuto;

Bhāvanābhirato yogī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.320.

In every way freed from heedlessness, clearly comprehending and endowed with mindfulness; The yogī delighting in development, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbābhisajjanā mutto, sadā kāruññamānaso;

Sabbupārambhavimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.321.

Freed from all attachments, ever compassionate in mind; freed from all reproach, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbadhi rosarahito, sabbadhi karuṇānidhī;

Sabbasāvajjā vimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.322.

Everywhere free from anger, everywhere a treasure of compassion; freed from all that is blameworthy, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbabādhāparimutto, bandhamutto ca sabbathā;

Kaṅkhicchākaṇṭakamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.323.

Freed from all afflictions, and released from bonds in every way; freed from the thorn of doubt and craving, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbārambhavinimutto, sadā samitamānaso;

Khambhanākhobhanā mutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.324.

Completely freed from all undertakings, ever with a pacified mind; freed from stiffness and agitation, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbasaṅkhatā sumutto, asaṅkhata sudassano;

Uddhaccakukkuccāmutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.325.
