Well freed from all conditioned things, one who clearly sees the Unconditioned; freed from restlessness and worry, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbabyasanavimutto, abyāsatta sumānaso;

Sabbasaṃsaraṇamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.326.

Freed from all calamity, unattached, with a good mind; freed from all wandering on, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbupādānapamutto, mutto sabbamadehi ca;

Sabbāyatanehi suñño, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.327.

Freed from all clinging, and released from all conceits; empty of all sense bases, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbapāsehi pamutto, yo vippasannamānaso;

Sabbamānānusayāmutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.328.

Freed from all snares, he who has a perfectly serene mind; freed from all underlying tendencies to conceit, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbanehavinimutto, sadā alittacetaso;

Sabbāsativinimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.329.

Freed from all affection, ever with an unsoiled mind; freed from all attachments, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Cittiñjanā vinimutto, sadā cittanirindhano;

Sabbāsavā vippamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.330.

Freed from the mind's agitation, ever with a mind without fuel; completely freed from all taints, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbadā akampacitto, thiravimuttamānaso;

Sabbavikkhepavimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.331.

Always with an unshaken mind, with a steadfast and liberated mind; freed from all distraction, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhavasnehā vippamutto, vimutto tibhavodadhi;

Sabbānugiddhisumutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.332.

Completely freed from affection for existence, freed from the ocean of the three realms; well freed from all greed, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhavacakkavinimutto, sudanto bahusaṃyato;

Visaṅkhāragatacitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.333.

Freed from the wheel of existence, well-tamed and greatly restrained; with a mind that has gone to the unconditioned, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbathā bhaviñjāmutto, vantalokāmiso isi;

Dhāresi antimaṃ dehaṃ, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.334.

In every way freed from agitation about existence, the sage who has disgorged the world's bait; he who bore his final body, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbābhijjhāvinimutto, santuṭṭho suddhamānaso;

Saṃsārasāgarā mutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.335.

Freed from all covetousness, contented, with a pure mind; freed from the ocean of saṃsāra, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbadhi sabbato mutto, sabbaññāto sabbañjayo;

Sabbathā mutto subhaddo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.336.

Freed everywhere and from everything, the All-Knower, the All-Conqueror; freed in every way, the Fortunate One, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbantarāyavimutto, aggalamutto sabbadhi;

Sabbabyavadhānamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.337.

Freed from all obstacles, unbarred everywhere; freed from all barriers, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbatāpavinimutto, sītalo sītimānaso;

Bhavarāgaggivimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.338.

Freed from all torment, become cool, with a cooled mind; freed from the fire of lust for existence, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Vaṅkamutto paṅkamutto,?..Dhamutto muttamano;

Sabbadā ātaṅkamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.339.

Freed from crookedness, freed from the mire, freed by the Dhamma, with a liberated mind; always freed from affliction, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbathā saṃsayamutto, kampamutto visārado;

Aggiidhumavimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.340.

In every way freed from doubt, freed from trembling, and confident; freed from fire and smoke, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbadā caṇḍikkamutto, dussanamutto sabbathā;

Sabbadhi sampuṇṇamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.341.

Always freed from fierceness, in every way freed from corruption; everywhere completely freed, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ujucitto sujūcitto, samacitto samācaro;

Dhīticitto vatacitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.342.

With an upright mind, a very upright mind, an even mind, and even conduct; with a resolute mind and a dutiful mind, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Alobhaadosacitto, alaggacitto sobhano;

Amohacitto dhīcitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.343.

With a mind free from greed and hate, with an unattached and splendid mind; with a mind free from delusion, a wise mind, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sādhucitto suddhacitto, sotthicitto satiyuto;

Cārucitto sivacitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.344.

With a virtuous mind, a pure mind, a mind of well-being, and endowed with mindfulness; with a charming mind and a peaceful mind, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammacitto atthacitto, dhīracitto cittuttamo;

Hiricitto siricitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.345.

With a mind for the Dhamma, a mind for the goal, a steadfast mind, supreme in mind; with a mind of moral shame and a mind of glory, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Hitacitto sukhacitto, kalyāṇacitto kāruñño;

Pīticitto sīticitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.346.

With a mind for welfare, a mind for happiness, a virtuous mind, and compassionate; with a joyful mind and a cool mind, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paramavisuddhacitto, muttacitto yativaro;

Yatacitto vatacitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.347.

With a supremely pure mind, a liberated mind, the best of ascetics; with a restrained mind and a dutiful mind, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Haṭṭhacitto tuṭṭhacitto, seṭṭhacitto suṭṭhuttaro;

Santuṭṭhacitto pahaṭṭho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.348.

With a joyful mind, a contented mind, an excellent mind, the most excellent one; with a satisfied mind, thrilled with joy, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Saticitto yaticitto, sampajañño viriyavā;

Sumaticitto sappañño, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.349.

With a mindful mind, a restrained mind, clearly comprehending and energetic; with a good mind and possessing wisdom, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mettacitto dayācitto, karuṇacitto kevalī;

Modito maddavacitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.350.

With a mind of loving-kindness, a mind of kindness, a mind of compassion, the Perfect One; joyful, with a gentle mind, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Saccacitto tathacitto, suguṇacitto sīlavā;

Nillobhacitto sucitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.351.

With a truthful mind, a mind of thusness, a mind of good qualities, and virtuous; with a greedless mind and a pure mind, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Niddosacitto niddhoto, nimmohacitto nillīno;

Nimmalacitto nissaṅgo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.352.

With a faultless mind, cleansed; with a mind free from delusion, unattached; with a stainless mind, unattached, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dibbacitto bhabbacitto, dammacitto dasabalo;

Bhabbabhāvarato bhabbo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.353.

With a divine mind, a receptive mind, a tamed mind, the Ten-Powered One; delighting in the state of receptivity, the Receptive One, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ñāṇacitto medhācitto, vijjācitto vijjādhanī;

Paññācitto aññācitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.354.

With a mind of knowledge, a mind of intelligence, a mind of true knowledge, rich in true knowledge; with a mind of wisdom, a mind of final knowledge, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ditticitto juticitto, juṇhacitto ālokado;

Kanticitto khanticitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.355.

With a bright mind, a luminous mind, a radiant mind, a giver of light; with a pleasing mind and a patient mind, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Amalo odātacitto, pasannacitto pesalo;

Pāvano punītacitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.356.

Stainless, with a pure white mind, a serene mind, and amiable; purifying, with a cleansed mind, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Javacitto balacitto, thāmacitto visārado;

Ṭhitacitto thiracitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.357.

With a swift mind, a powerful mind, a steadfast mind, and skilled; with a stable mind and a firm mind, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ojacitto tejacitto, yodhacitto avicalo;

Nibbhayacitto nipphando, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.358.

With a mind of vitality, a mind of majesty, a warrior's mind, unmoving; with a fearless mind, unwavering, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paguṇo supaññacitto, saṃyatacitto saṃvuto;

Pasannacitto passaddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.359.

Accomplished, with a mind of good wisdom, with a restrained mind, controlled; with a serene mind, tranquil, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhaddacitto mokkhacitto, sabbaseṭṭho sattuttamo;

Somanassacitto santo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.360.

With an auspicious mind, a mind for liberation, the best of all, the highest of beings; with a mind of gladness, peaceful, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Santacitto dantacitto, vasīcitto vusitavā;

Sucicitto rucicitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.361.

With a peaceful mind, a tamed mind, a mind of mastery, one who has lived the holy life; with a pure mind and a radiant mind, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Guttacitto muttacitto, pasannacitto cittujū;

Sumanacitto sucitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.362.

With a guarded mind, a freed mind, a serene mind, and an upright mind; with a glad mind and a pure mind, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Santacitto sadāsanto, sadā santusito isi;

Sabbattha saṃvuto sāmī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.363.

With a peaceful mind, ever peaceful, the ever-contented sage; restrained in every respect, the master, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Rittakāmo rittataṇho, rittarāgo rittarajo;

Rittachando rittanandī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.364.

Empty of sensual desire, empty of craving, empty of lust, empty of defilement; empty of longing, empty of delight, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Rittavero rittaroso, rittuttāpo rittāvilo;

Rittakodho rittakopo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.365.

Empty of enmity, empty of anger, empty of torment, empty of turbidity; empty of wrath, empty of rage, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Saṅgaritto raṅgaritto, rāgaritto rittaratī;

Nandīritto taṇhāritto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.366.

Empty of attachment, empty of excitement, empty of lust, empty of sensual pleasure; empty of delight, empty of craving, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbabhavābādharitto, rittamahāmohatamo;

Sabbathā avijjāritto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.367.

Empty of all afflictions of existence, empty of the great darkness of delusion; in every way empty of ignorance, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Nandīsaṃyojanaritto, sabbasaṅgapariccajo;

Uttamatthaṃ hatthagato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.368.

Empty of the fetter of delight, having relinquished all attachments; having attained the ultimate goal, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Iñjāritto indhāritto, icchāritto rittaagho;

Edharitto gedharitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.369.

Empty of agitation, empty of fuel, empty of craving, empty of evil; empty of turmoil, empty of greed, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Antagato antappatto, pārappatto pāraṅgato;

Koṭigato koṭippatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.370.

Gone to the end, attained the end; attained the far shore, gone to the far shore; gone to the summit, attained the summit, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Khemappatto khemaṅgato, aggappatto aggaṅgato;

Sivappatto sivaṅgato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.371.

Attained security, gone to security; attained the highest, gone to the highest; attained the auspicious, gone to the auspicious, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Padappatto dhuvappatto, parisuddhippatto pabhū;

Maggappatto phalappatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.372.

Attained the state, attained the permanent, attained purity, the Lord; attained the path, attained the fruit, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sārappatto pāragato, pariyantappatto varo;

Pariyodātasampatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.373.

Attained the essence, gone beyond, attained the final end, the excellent one; having reached perfect purity, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Vimalo vodātappatto, nibbānappatto nimmalo;

Amalo amatappatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.374.

Stainless, having attained purity; having attained Nibbāna, spotless; immaculate, having attained the Deathless, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammappatto dhammayogī, kusalappatto kevalī;

Lokuttarappatto pujjo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.375.

Having attained the Dhamma, devoted to the Dhamma; having attained the wholesome, the Perfect One; having attained the supramundane, the one worthy of homage, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pattavijjo pattapañño, patto sambodhimuttamaṃ;

Pattasanti pattasukho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.376.

Having attained true knowledge, having attained wisdom, having attained supreme perfect enlightenment; having attained peace, having attained happiness, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pattadhammo pattabodhi, pattamaggo pattaphalo;

Pattaamato pattattho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.377.

Having attained the Dhamma, having attained enlightenment, having attained the path, having attained the fruit; having attained the Deathless, having attained the goal, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paramatthasaccaṃ patto, pattakhemo pattasivo;

Pattaaccuto pattaggo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.378.

Having attained the ultimate truth, having attained security, having attained the auspicious; having attained the immutable, having attained the highest, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pattaanomo pattiṭṭho, pattadhuvo pattakkhayo;

Pattalokuttaradhammo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.379.

Having attained the excellent, having attained a firm standing, having attained the permanent, having attained the destruction of defilements; having attained the supramundane Dhamma, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pattañāṇo pattamedho, pattadhammaniyāmako;

Pattaananto pattanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.380.

Having attained knowledge, having attained intelligence, having attained the fixed course of the Dhamma; having attained the endless, having attained the end, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pattavisuddhi pattiddhi, pattapparamatthapado;

Pattanipuṇatthadhammo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.381.

Having attained purity, having attained spiritual power, having attained the state of the ultimate goal; having attained the Dhamma with its subtle meaning, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pattakanti pattavaṇṇo, pattakitti pattayaso;

Pattapassiddhi pakhyāto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.382.

Having attained loveliness, having attained a good complexion, having attained fame, having attained glory; having attained tranquility, the renowned one, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbakappaparikkhīṇo, parimutto patāpavā;

Saṃsāroghapārappatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.383.

With all volitional formations exhausted, completely liberated, and majestic; having reached the far shore of the flood of saṃsāra, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Nayappatto vasīppatto, sacchikaraṇapāragū;

Niyyānaṃ susacchikato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.384.

He has attained the method, attained mastery, gone to the far shore of realization; he has clearly realized the way of release, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhārabhañjako bhadanto, bhuvanabhogabhindako;

Bhavamahaṇṇavaṃ tiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.385.

The breaker of the burden, the Venerable One, the shatterer of worldly enjoyments; having crossed the great ocean of becoming—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dukkare bodhissambhāre, pūretvāna asesato;

Patto sabbaññutaṃ ñāṇaṃ, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.386.

Having completely fulfilled the difficult requisites for enlightenment, he has attained the knowledge of omniscience—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Santipatto santibhūto, sītalībhūto sabbadhi;

Paramaṃ sukhaṃ sampatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.387.

He has attained peace, has become peaceful, has become cool everywhere; having attained the supreme bliss—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pannāsajjo pannāsatto, pannālambo pannālayo;

Pannasūlo pannasallo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.388.

His attachments have fallen away, his clinging has fallen away, his dependencies have fallen away, his desires have fallen away; his stakes have fallen away, his darts have fallen away—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pannāsaṅko pannātaṅko, pannākaṅkho pannaṅgaṇo;

Pannāpekkho pannābhijjho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.389.

His doubt has fallen away, his anxiety has fallen away, his longing has fallen away, his defilements have fallen away; his expectations have fallen away, his covetousness has fallen away—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pannabhāro katakicco, puṇṇaabhiññā vosito;

Sabbavosita vosānaṃ, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.390.

His burden is laid down, his task is done, perfected in the full direct knowledges; he has reached the final end of all accomplishments—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Manogutto vacīgutto, kāyagutto guttivaro;

Guttakammo guttadhammo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.391.

Guarded in mind, guarded in speech, guarded in body, supreme in guarding; guarded in action, guarded in Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Cittagutto cittadanto, saṃtata citta saṃyato;

Cittamutto cittasanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.392.

With mind guarded, with mind tamed, with mind constantly restrained; with mind liberated, with mind at peace—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Indriyagutto sugutto, attapaccakkho āsabho;

Pacurapaññāsampanno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.393.

Guarded in faculties, well-guarded, self-realized, a leader of men; endowed with abundant wisdom—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kāyagutto vacīgutto, cittagutto yo guttatto;

Sabbindriyagutto sāmī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.394.

Guarded in body, guarded in speech, guarded in mind, one with a guarded self; with all his faculties guarded, the Master—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhīrahadayo dhorayho, saṃvuto saṃyatamano;

Guttindriyo guttamāno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.395.

Steadfast in heart, patient and enduring, restrained, with a controlled mind; with faculties guarded and pride restrained—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhitiyutto matiyutto, nītiyutto yuttanayo;

Maggayutto phalayutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.396.

Endowed with resolution, endowed with wisdom, endowed with guidance, following the right method; endowed with the path, endowed with the fruit—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Maggaladdho aggaladdho, antaladdho laddhapatho;

Paramatthasaccaladdho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.397.

He has attained the path, attained the supreme, attained the end, attained the way; he has attained the ultimate truth—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Iṭṭhaladdho siddhaladdho, sītiladdho laddhasivo;

Dullabhanibbānaladdho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.398.

He has attained the desired, attained success, attained coolness, attained the auspicious; he has attained the hard-to-attain Nibbāna—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammaladdho atthaladdho, muttiladdho laddhatatho;

Paladdhatiṃsapāramī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.399.

He has attained the Dhamma, attained the goal, attained release, attained the truth; he has attained the thirty perfections—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Santiladdho khantiladdho, kantiladdho laddhayaso;

Dittiladdho kittiladdho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.400.

He has attained peace, attained patience, attained beauty, attained glory; he has attained splendor, attained fame—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dibbaladdho bhabbaladdho, sabbaladdho laddhabalo;

Sududdasaṃ sivaṃ laddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.401.

He has attained the divine, attained the auspicious, attained all, attained strength; he has attained the bliss so very hard to see—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Suddhiladdho buddhiladdho, iddhiladdho laddhanidhī;

Upaladdhachaḷabhiñño, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.402.

He has attained purity, attained wisdom, attained psychic power, attained the treasure; he has attained the six direct knowledges—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kalyāṇaṃ kusalaṃ laddho, pītiladdho laddhasukho;

Upaladdhaamatasindhū, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.403.

He has attained the good and the wholesome, attained rapture, attained happiness; he has attained the ocean of the deathless—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Upaladdhayogakkhemo, sotthiladdho laddhadhuvo;

Dasabalaladdho vīro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.404.

He has attained security from bondage, attained well-being, attained the stable; the hero who has attained the ten powers—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bodhiladdho ñāṇaladdho, medhāladdho laddhanayo;

Paññāladdho vijjāladdho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.405.

He has attained enlightenment, attained knowledge, attained intelligence, attained guidance; he has attained wisdom, attained true knowledge—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sumatiladdho dhīladdho, laddhavimuttisampattī;

Parama’pavaggaladdho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.406.

He has attained a good mind, attained wisdom, attained the accomplishment of liberation; he has attained the supreme deliverance—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Punītaṃ pavittaṃladdho, laddhauttamamaṅgalo;

Paramaṃ avayaṃ laddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.407.

He has attained the purified and the pure, attained the highest blessing; he has attained the supreme security—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ukkaṭṭhaṃ kevalaṃ laddho, sumuttiladdho sabbato;

Ananta’nītikaṃ laddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.408.

He has attained the supreme and the whole, attained complete freedom in every way; he has attained the endless state free from affliction—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sattabojjhaṅgupaladdho, laddhaabhiñño sabbato;

Bhavataṇhakkhayaṃ laddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.409.

He has attained the seven factors of enlightenment, attained the direct knowledges in every way; he has attained the destruction of the craving for becoming—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Parisuddhapanthaladdho, saccaladdho anūpamo;

Paripuṇṇadhammaladdho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.410.

The incomparable one who has attained the perfectly pure path, attained the truth; he has attained the fullness of the Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sampuṇṇapariññāladdho, aññāladdho sabbuttamo;

Paṭisambhidāsuladdho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.411.

The highest of all, he has attained complete understanding, attained final knowledge; he has well attained the analytical knowledges—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paccakkhavimuttiladdho, laddhachaḷabhiññāvaro;

Paripuṇṇasantiladdho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.412.

He has attained direct liberation, attained the six excellent direct knowledges; he has attained perfect peace—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Yathābhūtasaccaladdho, tathatāladdho sabbadhi;

Samantasamatāladdho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.413.

He has attained the truth as it really is, attained suchness everywhere; he has attained complete equanimity—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Nimmalacakkhupaladdho, vimalavijjāvāridhi;

Nipuṇatthadhammaladdho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.414.

He has attained the stainless eye, an ocean of pure knowledge; he has attained the subtle meaning and the Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Thāmaladdho javaladdho, samaladdho patāpavā;

Lokuttarasaccaladdho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.415.

He has attained strength, attained speed, attained balance, he is possessed of majesty; he has attained the supramundane truth—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhitiladdho suciladdho, saṃsuddhiladdho suddhimā;

Sudhāladdho sumedhāvī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.416.

He has attained resolution, attained purity, attained perfect purity, the one of purity; the greatly wise one who has attained the nectar—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Laddhaaddho bhaddaladdho, modaladdho laddhasubho;

Sabbattha santosaladdho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.417.

He has attained success, attained the auspicious, attained delight, attained good fortune; he has attained contentment in all things—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Tīraladdho pāraladdho, sāraladdho laddhaphalo;

Maṅgalamuttamaṃ laddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.418.

He has attained the near shore, attained the far shore, attained the essence, attained the fruit; he has attained the supreme blessing—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Aṃsuladdho raṃsiladdho, ojaladdho laddhajutī;

Uggaaggaābhāladdho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.419.

He has attained sunbeams, attained rays of light, attained vital energy, attained radiance; he has attained a mighty, supreme splendor—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pasaṃsāpasiddhiladdho, visesavissavissuto;

Pūjanaṃ thomanaṃ laddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.420.

He has attained praise and success, he is widely famed for his distinction; he has attained honor and veneration—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mohandhakāravimutto, ālokaladdho lokajī;

Appameyyappabhāladdho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.421.

Freed from the darkness of delusion, the world-conqueror has attained the light; he has attained immeasurable radiance—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Visallo kusalo satthā, kammākammassakovido;

Pāragū sabbakammānaṃ, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.422.

The Teacher, dart-free and skilled, expert in action and non-action; gone to the far shore of all actions—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kusalassa kovido ca, akusalassa kovido;

Kusalassupasampanno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.423.

Expert in the wholesome and expert in the unwholesome; he is accomplished in the wholesome—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammassa kovido cāpi, adhammassāpi kovido;

Pāragū sabbadhammānaṃ, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.424.

Expert in the Dhamma and also expert in what is not Dhamma; gone to the far shore of all things—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Puññassa kovido cāpi, apuññassāpi kovido;

Pāpapuññamatikkanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.425.

Expert in merit and also expert in demerit; he has transcended both evil and merit—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Maggassa kovido cāpi, amaggassāpi kovido;

Aggamaggamugghāṭesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.426.

Expert in the path and also expert in the non-path; he has opened up the supreme path—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sukhassa kovido cāpi, dukkhassa cāpi kovido;

Paramasukhadassāvī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.427.

Expert in happiness and also expert in suffering; the seer of the supreme bliss—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Iṭṭhassa kovido cāpi, aniṭṭhassāpi kovido;

Paramiṭṭhaṃ paññāpesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.428.

Expert in the agreeable and also expert in the disagreeable; he has made known the supreme agreeable—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Antassa kovido cāpi, anantassāpi kovido;

Anantaobhāsaṃ sakkhī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.429.

Expert in the finite and also expert in the infinite; a witness to the infinite radiance—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Matassa kovido cāpi, amatassāpi kovido;

Amatassasindhuladdho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.430.

Expert in death and also expert in the deathless; he has attained the ocean of the deathless—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Niccassa kovido cāpi, aniccassāpi kovido;

Niccasaccaṃ pakāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.431.

Expert in the permanent and also expert in the impermanent; he has illuminated the permanent truth—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhavassa kovido cāpi, vibhavassāpi kovido;

Sabbaso bhavaṃ bhañjesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.432.

Expert in becoming and also expert in non-becoming; he has completely shattered all becoming—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Rāgassa kovido cāpi, arāgassāpi kovido;

Sabbarāgaṃ virañjesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.433.

Expert in passion and also expert in passionlessness; he is dispassionate towards all passion—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dosassa kovido cāpi, adosassāpi kovido;

Sabbadosaṃ viddhaṃsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.434.

Expert in aversion and also expert in non-aversion; he has demolished all aversion—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mohassa kovido cāpi, amohassāpi kovido;

Sabbamohavītivatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.435.

Expert in delusion and also expert in non-delusion; he has overcome all delusion—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Verassa kovido cāpi, averassāpi kovido;

Sabbaveramatikkanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.436.

Expert in enmity and also expert in non-enmity; he has transcended all enmity—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Klesassa kovido cāpi, niklesassāpi kovido;

Sabbakilesaṃ jhāpesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.437.

Expert in defilement and also expert in non-defilement; he has burnt up all defilements—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Tamassa kovido cāpi, ālokassāpi kovido;

Loke ālokaṃ jotesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.438.

Expert in darkness and also expert in light; he made light shine in the world—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Neḷassa kovido cāpi, aneḷassāpi kovido;

Aneḷako akkileso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.439.

Expert in what is flawed and also expert in the flawless; he is flawless and without defilements—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Saṅgassa kovido cāpi, asaṅgassāpi kovido;

Sabbasaṅgavinimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.440.

Expert in attachment and also expert in non-attachment; he is completely freed from all attachments—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kovido ca vedanāya, avedanāya kovido;

Sabbassa vedanātīto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.441.

Expert in feeling and expert in non-feeling; he has gone beyond all feeling—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ārambhassa kovido ca, anārambhassa kovido;

Sabbārambhapariccāgī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.442.

Expert in undertaking and expert in non-undertaking; he has relinquished all undertakings—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ālayassa kovido ca, anālayassa kovido;

Sabbālayavinimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.443.

Expert in attachment and expert in non-attachment; he is completely freed from all attachments—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ālambassa kovido ca, anālambassa kovido;

Sabbālambanā vigato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.444.

Expert in dependence and expert in non-dependence; he has gone beyond all supports—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Taṇhāya kovido cāpi, vītataṇhāya kovido;

Taṇhājālaṃ vidālesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.445.

Expert in craving and also expert in freedom from craving; he has torn asunder the net of craving—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mānassa kovido cāpi, avamānassa kovido;

Mānāvamānasamako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.446.

Expert in honor and also expert in dishonor; he is impartial toward honor and dishonor—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kāmassa kovido cāpi, nikkāmassāpi kovido;

Sabbakāmaparikkhīṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.447.

Skilled in sensual pleasure and also skilled in renunciation; with all sensual desires completely exhausted—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kopassa kovido cāpi, nikkopassāpi kovido;

Sabbakopagginibbuto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.448.

Skilled in anger and also skilled in non-anger; with the fire of all anger extinguished—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kodhassa kovido cāpi, nikkodhassāpi kovido;

Kodhānalaṃ nibbāpesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.449.

Skilled in anger and also skilled in its absence; he has extinguished the fire of anger—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Chandassa kovido cāpi, nicchandassāpi kovido;

Sabbabhavacchandacchinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.450.

Skilled in desire and also skilled in its absence; with all desire for existence severed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Lobhassa kovido cāpi, nillobhassāpi kovido;

Lobhalālasānillitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.451.

Skilled in greed and also skilled in non-greed; unstained by the craving of greed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sokassa kovido cāpi, nissokassāpi kovido;

Sokasūlaṃ abbāhesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.452.

Skilled in sorrow and also skilled in sorrowlessness; he has plucked out the dart of sorrow—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Snehassa kovido cāpi, nisnehassāpi kovido;

Sabbasnehā vinimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.453.

Skilled in affection and also skilled in non-affection; completely freed from all affection—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Khobhassa kovido cāpi, nikkhobhassāpi kovido;

Sabbakammakhobhakhīṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.454.

Skilled in agitation and also skilled in non-agitation; with all agitation from kamma destroyed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sallassa kovido cāpi, visallassāpi kovido;

Sabbasallaṃ sandālesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.455.

Skilled in darts and also skilled in being without darts; he has completely shattered all darts—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Khayassa kovido cāpi, akkhayassāpi kovido;

Sabbathā akkhayaṃ patto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.456.

Skilled in destruction and also skilled in the undecaying; having in every way attained the undecaying—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Lokassa kovido cāpi, lokantassāpi kovido;

Lokuttaraṃ sacchikato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.457.

Skilled in the world and also skilled in the world's end; having realized the supramundane—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Suddhassa kovido cāpi, asuddhassāpi kovido;

Suddhimaggaṃ visodhesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.458.

Skilled in the pure and also skilled in the impure; he has purified the path to purity—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kālassa kovido cāpi, akālassāpi kovido;

Kālānusārī desesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.459.

Skilled in the timely and also skilled in the untimely; he taught in accordance with the time—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Chalassa kovido cāpi, nicchalassāpi kovido;

Sabbathā chalavimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.460.

Skilled in deceit and also skilled in non-deceit; in every way freed from deceit—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sumanassa kovido ca, dummanassāpi kovido;

Sabbadhi sumano santo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.461.

Skilled in gladness of mind and also skilled in sadness of mind; everywhere glad-minded and peaceful—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kovido lokacakkassa, dhammacakkassa kovido;

Lokacakkaṃ viddhaṃsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.462.

Skilled in the wheel of the world, skilled in the wheel of the Dhamma; he shattered the wheel of the world—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Tīrassa kovido cāpi, pārassa cāpi kovido;

Ghoraṃ oghaṃ pāraṅgato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.463.

Skilled in the near shore and also skilled in the far shore; he has gone beyond the dreadful flood—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bodhassa kovido cāpi, dubbodhassāpi kovido;

Sabbathā sabbaṃ bodhesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.464.

Skilled in what is to be understood and also skilled in what is difficult to understand; he has understood everything in every way—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Yogassa kovido cāpi, khemassa cāpi kovido;

Yogakkhemamanuppatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.465.

Skilled in bondage and also skilled in security; he has attained the supreme security from bondage—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Āvilassa kovido ca, anāvilassa kovido;

Sabbāvilaṃ vidhopesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.466.

Skilled in the turbid and also skilled in the unturbid; he has dispelled all turbidity—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Saṃsayassa kovido ca, asaṃsayassa kovido;

Sabbattha saṃsayamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.467.

Skilled in doubt and also skilled in certainty; everywhere freed from doubt—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sārajjassa kovido ca, visārajjassa kovido;

Vesārajjañāṇupeto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.468.

Skilled in timidity and also skilled in intrepidity; endowed with the knowledge of perfect confidence—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Vikārassa kovido ca, nibbikārassa kovido;

Vikārāni vikkhambhesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.469.

Skilled in distortion and also skilled in the undistorted; he has suppressed all distortions—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sammohassa kovido ca, asammohassa kovido;

Sabbasammohā vimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.470.

Skilled in delusion and also skilled in non-delusion; freed from all delusion—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Santāpassa kovido ca, asantāpassa kovido;

Santāpānaṃ santikaro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.471.

Skilled in torment and also skilled in non-torment; the maker of peace from torments—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paṭighassa kovido ca, appaṭighassa kovido;

Paṭighasaññāsuñño yo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.472.

Skilled in aversion and also skilled in non-aversion; he who is void of perceptions of aversion—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Āsavassa kovido ca, anāsavassa kovido;

Sabbāsavaparikkhīṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.473.

Skilled in the taints and also skilled in the taintless; one in whom all taints are utterly destroyed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Suguṇassa kovido ca, dugguṇassāpi kovido;

Sabbasuguṇasaṃyutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.474.

Skilled in virtues and also skilled in faults; endowed with all virtues—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kalyāṇassa kovido ca, akalyāṇassa kovido;

Kalyāṇatthamanuppatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.475.

Skilled in the wholesome and also skilled in the unwholesome; having attained the ultimate good, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Vipulapañño pabalo, maggāmaggassa kovido;

Paramatthadhammaladdho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.476.

Of vast wisdom and powerful, skilled in the path and the non-path; one who has attained the ultimate truth of the Dhamma, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Cetosamathakovido, jhānasamādhikovido;

Samāpattisamāpanno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.477.

Skilled in the calming of the mind, skilled in absorption and concentration; one who has entered upon the attainments, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Maggakovido maggaññū, dhammaññū dhammakovido;

Aggakovido aggaññū, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.478.

Skilled in the path, knower of the path; knower of the Dhamma, skilled in the Dhamma; skilled in the supreme, knower of the supreme, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sampajaññassa kovido, sammāsatiyā kovido;

Satipaṭṭhāne kovido, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.479.

Skilled in clear comprehension, skilled in right mindfulness, skilled in the establishments of mindfulness, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sattabojjhaṅgakovido, sabbavibhaṅgakovido;

Vijjākovido tevijjo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.480.

Skilled in the seven factors of enlightenment, skilled in all the analyses; skilled in true knowledge, the one with the three true knowledges, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Cetovimuttikovido, paññāvimuttikovido;

Ubhatovimuttippatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.481.

Skilled in the liberation of mind, skilled in the liberation by wisdom; one who has attained liberation in both ways, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Iddhipādesu kovido, cetopariyakovido;

Chaḷabhiññāhi sampanno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.482.

Skilled in the bases of spiritual power, skilled in penetrating the minds of others; endowed with the six direct knowledges, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Appamaññāsu kovido, aṭṭhaṅgamaggaṃ pāragū;

Sabbathā nikkhayappatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.483.

Skilled in the immeasurable states, one who has gone to the far shore of the Eightfold Path; having in every way attained complete destruction, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammaniyāme kovido, dhammatāyapi kovido;

Dhammaṭṭhitiyā kovido, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.484.

Skilled in the fixed order of phenomena, skilled also in the nature of phenomena, skilled in the stability of phenomena, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kāyasaṃvarasampanno, cittasaṃvarakovido;

Vacīsaṃvarasaṃvuto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.485.

Endowed with restraint of body, skilled in restraint of mind, and restrained in speech, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Lokajeṭṭho lokaseṭṭho, tilokajeṭṭho tevijjo;

Purisajeṭṭho visiṭṭho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.486.

Foremost in the world, best in the world; foremost in the three worlds, possessor of the three true knowledges; foremost among persons, the most distinguished, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbaseṭṭho sudhammaṭṭho, yoniso sabbaniggaho;

Visaṃsaggo visaṃsaṭṭho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.487.

The best of all, established in the good Dhamma, one who wisely restrains all; detached and unassociated, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbajeṭṭho sabbaseṭṭho, sabbaggo sabbanāyako;

Sabbanātho sabbasāmī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.488.

Foremost of all, best of all, highest of all, leader of all; refuge of all, lord of all, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Maccujayī mārajayī, māradheyyaviddhaṃsako;

Mārapāsaṃ vināsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.489.

Conqueror of Death, conqueror of Māra, destroyer of Māra's domain; one who destroyed Māra's snare, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Maccuhāyī mārahāyī, maccudheyyaviddhaṃsako;

Maccupāsaṃ vināsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.490.

One who has left Death behind, one who has left Māra behind, destroyer of Death's domain; one who destroyed the snare of Death, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mārajayo maccujayo, antakadheyyaddhaṃsako;

Maccukhettaṃ padālesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.491.

Conqueror of Māra, conqueror of Death, destroyer of the Ender's domain; one who shattered the field of Death, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Parakkamapuṇṇo vīro, māracamūnimmaddano;

Maccutiṇṇo maccujito, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.492.

The hero full of exertion, the crusher of Māra's army; one who has crossed beyond Death, the conqueror of Death, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Māramāyaṃ vimaddesi, mohanamāraghātako;

Māramadanamaddano, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.493.

One who crushed Māra's deceit, slayer of the deluding Māra, subduer of Māra's intoxication, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pabalo māraṃ maddesi, sabalo maccumaddako;

Namucināsako nāgo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.494.

The mighty one who crushed Māra, the powerful crusher of Death; the great being, destroyer of Namuci, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pāpīmārabalānīkaṃ, nimmaddesi mahājino;

Paraṃ sukhaṃ adhippatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.495.

The Great Conqueror who crushed the army of the evil Māra; having attained supreme happiness, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pāpajayo puññajayo, ahaṃjayo jitamamo;

Kaṅkhājayo saṅkājayo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.496.

Conqueror of evil, conqueror of merit, conqueror of 'I-making,' one who has conquered 'my-making'; conqueror of doubt, conqueror of hesitation, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Jitamoho jitamāyo, jitamakkho mārañjito;

Jitamaccharo jiticcho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.497.

One who has conquered delusion, conquered deceit, conquered contempt, and conquered Māra; one who has conquered stinginess and conquered desire, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Jitakodho jitakāmo, jitalobho nandījito;

Jitataṇho jitarāgo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.498.

One who has conquered anger, conquered sensual desire, conquered greed, and conquered delight; one who has conquered craving and conquered passion, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Jitavero jitasneho, jitamāno jitamado;

Jitātaṅko jitāpekkho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.499.

One who has conquered enmity, conquered affection, conquered conceit, and conquered arrogance; one who has conquered fear and conquered expectation, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbadā vijayī vīro, sabbaji samaṇuttamo;

Khemadāyako khemindo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.500.

The ever-victorious hero, the all-conquering supreme ascetic; the giver of security, the lord of security, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbajito sabbavasī, sabbavijayamaṅgalo;

Sabbajayo sakkasīho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.501.

Conqueror of all, master of all, auspicious in all victories; the all-conquering Lion of the Sakyans, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dantathīno dantamiddho, dantapamādo dantiñjo;

Dantārambho dantalīno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.502.

One whose sloth is tamed, whose torpor is tamed, whose negligence is tamed, whose agitation is tamed; whose wrong initiative is tamed, whose sluggishness is tamed, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dantachambho dantadaro, dantabhīti dantabhayo;

Dantabhesmo dantabhīmo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.503.

One whose rigidity is tamed, whose anxiety is tamed, whose fear is tamed, whose dread is tamed; whose terror is tamed, whose fright is tamed, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dohadanto dosadanto, dessadanto dantadiso;

Ḍāhadanto dāhadanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.504.

One whose craving is tamed, whose hatred is tamed, whose malice is tamed, whose aversion is tamed; whose burning passion is tamed, whose burning grief is tamed, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sammāpadhāno vikkanto, mahāvikkamamānaso;

Parakkamī mārañjayo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.505.

Of right exertion, valiant, with a mind of great courage; energetic, the conqueror of Māra—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sammāpadhānakusalo, saṃvuto atisaṃyato;

Pāramitāparipuṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.506.

Skilled in right exertion, restrained, supremely restrained; one who has completely fulfilled the perfections—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Hirottappī ātāpī ca, sudanto atisaṃvuto;

Sakyaseṭṭho sabbajeṭṭho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.507.

Possessed of conscience and fear of wrongdoing, and ardent; well-tamed and exceedingly restrained; best of the Sakyans, foremost of all—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dasabaladhārī daḷho, dhīro asayhasāhino;

Purisapuṅgavo sūro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.508.

Wielder of the ten powers, firm, steadfast, and invincible; bull among persons, the hero—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dvipaduttamo damako, danto daḷhaparakkamo;

Dosāpagato dosaññū, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.509.

Supreme among two-footed beings, a tamer, self-tamed, of firm exertion; gone beyond defilement, a knower of defilement—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Upakkamī parakkamī, viriyārambho vikkamī;

Pāramī paripūresi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.510.

Striving and energetic, one who initiates energy, courageous; he who fulfilled the perfections—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Patāpī pāramīppatto, padhāniko paggāhako;

Pāpīmāraṃ parājesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.511.

Ardent, having attained the perfections, striving, encouraging; he vanquished the Evil One, Māra—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Javasattisusampanno, sūro vikkamasekharo;

Dhitidhārako sudhīro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.512.

Endowed with swift power, the hero, the crest of the valiant; the bearer of fortitude, the supremely wise—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Vikkamo duratikkamo, akilanto parakkamo;

Amatāgadhaṃ sampatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.513.

Heroic, hard to surpass, tireless in striving; having reached the unfathomable Deathless—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Satisātaccasampanno, sammappadhāna pāragū;

Ariyadassanadassī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.514.

Endowed with constant mindfulness, one who has gone beyond in right exertion, the seer of the noble vision—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sammāviriyasampanno, sammāpadhānapāragū;

Sabbatthappatto siddhattho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.515.

Endowed with right energy, one who has gone beyond in right exertion, who has attained all his goals, the Accomplished One—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sūro vijitasaṅgāmo, vīro vijitaveriko;

Dhīro purisadhoreyyo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.516.

The hero, victor in battle; the brave one, conqueror of enmity; the wise one, the foremost of men—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pūresi pāramī sabbā, narāsabho parakkamī;

Vimuttajātimaraṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.517.

He fulfilled all the perfections, the striving bull among men, the one freed from birth and death—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Vikkamo ca parakkamo, pāpakammaṃ pahāṇako;

Paggāhako upakkamo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.518.

The courageous and striving one, the abandoner of evil deeds, the encourager and enterprising one—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbasattahitatthāya, parikkāmesi vikkamī;

Muttāyanaṃ anvesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.519.

For the welfare and benefit of all beings the valiant one strove; he sought the path to liberation—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhavavaṭṭaṃ suacchecchi, narasaddūlo niddaro;

Paramiṭṭhaṃ susampatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.520.

He thoroughly transcended the round of existence, the fearless tiger among men; he perfectly attained the supreme good—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Hatamoho hatamāyo, hatamakkho hatamado;

Hatamāno hatamāro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.521.

He who has destroyed delusion, destroyed illusion, destroyed contempt, destroyed intoxication, destroyed conceit, and destroyed Māra—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dambhahantā gabbahantā, vaṭṭahantā arahanto;

Bījahantā bādhāhantā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.522.

The destroyer of hypocrisy, the destroyer of pride, the destroyer of the round, the Arahant; the destroyer of seeds, the destroyer of afflictions—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhavanettiṃ sammāhantā, saṅkhatamuttamānaso;

Sabbadhi avijjāhantā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.523.

The one who has rightly destroyed the guide to existence, whose mind is composed and supreme, the destroyer of ignorance everywhere—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Saṃvutatto saṃyatatto, samitatto samāhito;

Vimuttatto virattatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.524.

His self restrained, his self controlled, his self pacified, well-composed; his self liberated, his self detached—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pāpaghacco puññaghacco, ahaṃghacco ghaccamamo;

Kaṅkhāghacco saṅkāghacco, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.525.

The destroyer of evil and the destroyer of merit, the destroyer of ‘I’ and the destroyer of ‘mine’; the destroyer of doubt and the destroyer of uncertainty—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kappaghacco kālaghacco, kilesaghacco sabbathā;

Mūlaghacco sūlaghacco, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.526.

The destroyer of the eon, the destroyer of time, the destroyer of defilements in every way; the destroyer of roots, the destroyer of stakes—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ghaccajāti ghaccamaccu, ghaccaloko ghaccabhavo;

Ghaccavibhavo ghaccogho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.527.

The destroyer of birth, the destroyer of death, the destroyer of the world, the destroyer of becoming; the destroyer of non-becoming, the destroyer of the floods—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ghaccakhobho ghaccadukkho, ghaccasallo ghaccakhilo;

Ghaccābhimāno ghaccedho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.528.

The destroyer of agitation, the destroyer of suffering, the destroyer of the dart, the destroyer of mental barrenness; the destroyer of conceit, the destroyer of craving—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dosabhañjo dohabhañjo, kāmabhañjo bhañjatamo;

Diṭṭhibhañjo dukkhabhañjo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.529.

The breaker of hatred, the breaker of malice, the breaker of sensual desire, the breaker of darkness; the breaker of views, the breaker of suffering—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhogabhañjo sokabhañjo, rogabhañjo bhañjarajo;

Sūlabhañjo sallabhañjo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.530.

The breaker of enjoyments, the breaker of sorrow, the breaker of disease, the breaker of dust; the breaker of the stake, the breaker of the dart—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhañjathīno bhañjamiddho, bhañjabandho bhañjaggalo;

Bhañjārambho bhañjalīno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.531.

The breaker of sloth, the breaker of torpor, the breaker of bonds, the breaker of bolts; the breaker of agitated striving, the breaker of indolence—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhañjāsaṅko bhañjātaṅko, bhañjāsaṃso bhañjussuko;

Bhañjābhirato bhañjiccho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.532.

The breaker of fear, the breaker of distress, the breaker of longing, the breaker of zeal; he who delights in destruction, he who wishes for destruction—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhañjānutāpo bhañjāgu, bhañjaṅgaṇo bhañjāsavo;

Mānābhimānavibhañjo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.533.

The breaker of remorse, the breaker of faults, the breaker of stains, the breaker of taints; the destroyer of conceit and pride—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sallabhañjo sūlabhañjo, mūlabhañjo bhañjamalo;

Sabbabhavabandhabhañjo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.534.

The breaker of the dart, the breaker of the stake, the breaker of the root, the breaker of stains; the breaker of all bonds of existence—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kappakkhayo kālakkhayo, kilesakkhayo sabbathā;

Mūlakkhayo sūlakkhayo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.535.

He for whom the eon is destroyed, for whom time is destroyed, for whom defilements are destroyed in every way; for whom roots are destroyed, for whom stakes are destroyed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dosakkhayo dohakkhayo, kāmakkhayo khīṇamado;

Diṭṭhikkhayo dukkhakkhayo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.536.

He for whom hatred is destroyed, for whom malice is destroyed, for whom sensual desire is destroyed, whose intoxication is destroyed; for whom views are destroyed, for whom suffering is destroyed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kodhakhīṇo kopakhīṇo, klesakhīṇo khīṇakuho;

Verakhīṇo dessakhīṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.537.

One whose anger is destroyed, whose wrath is destroyed, whose defilements are destroyed, whose deceit is destroyed; one whose enmity is destroyed, whose hatred is destroyed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Lobhakhīṇo lolakhīṇo, rosakhīṇo khīṇaraṇo;

Kicchākhīṇo icchākhīṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.538.

One whose greed is destroyed, whose craving is destroyed, whose anger is destroyed, whose conflict is destroyed; one whose trouble is destroyed, whose desire is destroyed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Khīṇarāgo khīṇadoso, khīṇamoho khīṇabhavo;

Khīṇamāno khīṇamakkho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.539.

One whose passion is destroyed, whose hatred is destroyed, whose delusion is destroyed, whose becoming is destroyed; one whose conceit is destroyed, whose contempt is destroyed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Khīṇagedho khīṇalobho, khīṇakāmo ca khīṇiñjo;

Khīṇasārambho khīṇindho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.540.

One whose greed is destroyed, whose avarice is destroyed, whose sensual desire and agitation are destroyed; one whose contention is destroyed, whose fuel is destroyed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Khīṇāsayo khīṇabījo, aviruḷhichando vasī;

Khīṇataṇho khīṇabhijjho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.541.

One whose inclinations are destroyed, whose seed is destroyed, whose desire does not grow, the master of himself; one whose craving is destroyed, whose covetousness is destroyed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Khayāsaṅko khayātaṅko, khayākaṅkho khayaṅgaṇo;

Khayāpekkho khayābhijjho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.542.

One for whom fear is destroyed, for whom distress is destroyed, for whom longing is destroyed, for whom stains are destroyed; for whom expectation is destroyed, for whom covetousness is destroyed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Khayataṇho khayalobho, khayasallo khayakhilo;

Khayabhogo khayarogo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.543.

One for whom craving is destroyed, for whom greed is destroyed, for whom the dart is destroyed, for whom mental barrenness is destroyed; for whom enjoyments are destroyed, for whom disease is destroyed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Khayachambho khayadaro, khayabhismā khayabhayo;

Khayasaṃsayo khayiñjo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.544.

One for whom trembling is destroyed, for whom dread is destroyed, for whom terror is destroyed, for whom fear is destroyed; for whom doubt is destroyed, for whom agitation is destroyed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Khayasaṃsaṭṭho khayoko, khayāgāro khayagaho;

Khayasūlo khayamūlo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.545.

One for whom association is destroyed, for whom the home is destroyed, for whom the household is destroyed, for whom grasping is destroyed; for whom the stake is destroyed, for whom the root is destroyed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ahaṅkhayo mamaṅkhayo, saṅkhatakkhayo sabbadhi;

Saṅkilesakkhayappatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.546.

He for whom ‘I’ is destroyed, for whom ‘mine’ is destroyed, for whom the conditioned is destroyed everywhere; he who has attained the destruction of defilements—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Suññataṇho suññarāgo, suññaraṅgo suññarajo;

Suññachando suññanandī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.547.

Empty of craving, empty of passion, empty of attachment, empty of dust; empty of desire, empty of delight—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Suññaroso suññadoso, suññadoho suññadaho;

Suññasāpo suññatāpo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.548.

Empty of anger, empty of hatred, empty of malice, empty of burning; empty of curses, empty of torment—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Suññakodho suññakopo, suññakleso suññakuho;

Suññaverī suññasattu, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.549.

Empty of anger, empty of wrath, empty of defilement, empty of deceit; empty of enmity, empty of hostility—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Suññajāti suññamaccu, suññaloko suññabhavo;

Suññatā’bhirato sugato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.550.

Empty of birth, empty of death, empty of the world, empty of becoming; the Well-farer, delighting in emptiness—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ītisuñño bhītisuñño, chambhasuñño suññabhayo;

Sabbathā sārajjasuñño, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.551.

Empty of calamity, empty of terror, empty of trembling, empty of fear; in every way empty of timidity—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Suññagantho suññarajju, sabbathā suññabandhano;

Suññadiṭṭhi suññanetti, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.552.

Empty of knots, empty of ropes, in every way empty of bonds; empty of views, empty of the guide to existence—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sallasuñño sūlasuñño, mūlasuñño suññamalo;

Sabbānusayehi suñño, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.553.

Empty of darts, empty of stakes, empty of roots, empty of stains; empty of all underlying tendencies—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbadhi santāpasuñño, santāsasuñño sabbathā;

Tasasuñño tāsasuñño, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.554.

Everywhere empty of torment, in every way empty of terror; empty of craving, empty of fear—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Āsāsuñño issāsuñño, ejāsuñño suññaiṇo;

Chandasuñño bandhasuñño, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.555.

Empty of hope, empty of envy, empty of agitation, empty of debt; empty of desire, empty of bondage—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pāpasuñño puññasuñño, ahaṃsuñño suññamamo;

Kaṅkhāsuñño saṅkāsuñño, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.556.

Empty of evil, empty of merit, empty of ‘I,’ empty of ‘mine’; empty of doubt, empty of uncertainty—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbakammajaho cāgī, cattābhilāso sabbadhi;

Sabbathā cattasaṃsaṭṭho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.557.

The abandoner of all kamma, generous, who has relinquished craving everywhere; who has in every way given up association—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dosacāgī dohacāgī, kujjhanacāgī cattindho;

Kodhacāgī kopacāgī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.558.

One who has relinquished hatred, who has relinquished malice, who has relinquished irritation, who has relinquished fuel; one who has relinquished anger, who has relinquished rage—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbarājabhogacāgī, mahāoghamummujjako;

Paramatthadhammadassī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.559.

The relinquisher of all royal enjoyments, one who has emerged from the great flood, the seer of the ultimate truth—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Cattakāmo cattakleso, cattāsatto cattāsavo;

Cattapāpo cattuttāpo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.560.

One who has relinquished sensual desire, relinquished defilements, relinquished attachments, relinquished taints; one who has relinquished evil, relinquished torment—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Īticuto bhīticuto, chambhacuto cuttabhayo;

Ḍāhacuto dāhacuto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.561.

Fallen from calamity, fallen from terror, fallen from trembling, with fear fallen away; fallen from burning, fallen from scorching—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbacatto sabbacajo, sabbacāgī sabbaccago;

Sabbaritto sabbañjaho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.562.

One who has given up all, renounced all, relinquished all, abandoned all; one who is empty of all, the abandoner of all—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Anāsaṅko anātaṅko, anākaṅkho anaṅgaṇo;

Sadā asaṅgacetaso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.563.

Without fear, without anxiety, without longing, without stain; always with an unattached mind—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Anusūyo anāsaṃso, anāso ca anussuko;

Anāsavo anapekkho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.564.

Without envy, without longing, without craving and without zeal; without taints, without expectation—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ākāso viya paññāya, alitto anilo yathā;

Anissito vihāsi yo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.565.

He whose wisdom is like the sky, who is unsullied like the wind; he who dwelt unsupported—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Āraddhaviriyo ātāpī, uyyujjanto padhāniko;

Akusīto analaso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.566.

With energy aroused, ardent, ever striving, resolute in the endeavor; not lazy, not indolent—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Anupamo satthā jeṭṭho, sabbaseṭṭho ovādako;

Ariyadhammaṃ aññāsi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.567.

The incomparable Teacher, the foremost, the best of all, the instructor; he who understood the Noble Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Amatena atappi lokaṃ, dhammameghapavassako;

Assāsesi yathā cando, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.568.

He satisfied the world with the Deathless, raining down the cloud of the Dhamma; he gave comfort like the moon—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Avikampī anabhīto, asantāsī anāturo;

Abhīruko yo acchambhī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.569.

Unshaken, unterrified, untrembling, unafflicted; he who is fearless and intrepid—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Amaccharī amāyāvī, ajeguccho aniṭṭhuro;

Appaduṭṭho akuṭilo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.570.

Not stingy, not deceitful, not contemptuous, not harsh; not malicious, not crooked—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Anabhijjho apihālu, akuhako alolupo;

Anupavajjo amatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.571.

Not covetous, not envious, not deceitful, not greedy; blameless, not intoxicated—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Appagabbho appaṭigho, appāvilo amucchito;

Apesuṇiko adoso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.572.

Not proud, without aversion, not agitated, not infatuated; not a slanderer, without hatred—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Akopo cāpi aduṭṭho, vītussuko adūsako;

Vītakopo vītakuho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.573.

Without wrath and without malice, free from zeal, non-corrupting; free from anger, free from deceit—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Akampito akampako, avero akutobhayo;

Sabbadā chambharahito, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.574.

Unshaken and unshakeable, without enmity, fearless from any quarter; always devoid of terror—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Anatimānī akhilo, akuppano akujjhano;

Anabhisajjo akkodho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.575.

Not arrogant and without defect, imperturbable and not irritable; unattached and without anger—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Akakkaso ca akkopo, akaṭuko akūṭako;

Appadesso anunnalo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.576.

Not harsh and not agitated, not acrimonious and not deceitful; without malice and not haughty—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Anapekkhī anupayo, anāsaṅgo anāvilo;

Analitto anālambo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.577.

Without expectation and without contrivance, unattached and untroubled; unstained and unsupported—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Aggamahattatto muni, abhinibbuttatto yatī;

Visuddho abhisambuddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.578.

The sage of supreme greatness, the striving one with self utterly quenched; purified and fully self-enlightened—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Abbūḷhasallo arajo, akkhadakkhī anāsavo;

Anomadakkhī anaṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.579.

With dart drawn out and dustless, a seer of the imperishable, free from taints; with flawless vision and free from debt—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Atthapatiṭṭho atthagū, anappaatthapāragū;

Accanto atthakusalo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.580.

Established in the goal, a knower of the goal, one who has gone to the far shore of the great goal; supremely skilled in the goal—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ariyaddhānaṃ sampatto, atthatto atthapaṇḍito;

Mocesi bandhanāsatte, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.581.

Having attained the noble wealth, with purpose achieved, wise in the goal; he freed those bound by attachments—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Asajjamānaso alīno, sadā asaṅgamānaso;

Appamādarato nātho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.582.

With mind unhindered and unshrinking, always with a mind unattached; the Protector who delights in heedfulness—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Asaṅgacitto akleso, āsā yassa na vijjati;

Asaṅkiliṭṭho akkuho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.583.

With mind unattached and without defilements, one for whom no longing is found; unstained and without deceit—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Upekkhacitto acalo, upādānakkhayo ujū;

Abhinibbido appiñjo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.584.

With equanimous mind, unshakeable, the destruction of clinging, upright; fully dispassionate, without affection—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Abhiññāpāramīpatto, abhipañño abhigato;

Anupādā vimutto yo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.585.

Having attained the perfection of direct knowledge, profoundly wise, one who has arrived; he who is liberated without clinging—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Aviruddho asāratto, santiladdho anappako;

Amitaamatappatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.586.

Unopposed and without passion, having attained peace, the great one; having reached the immeasurable Deathless—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ākaṅkhiñjāritto, anupādāno uttamo;

Appaneho ca appiho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.587.

Devoid of craving and agitation, without clinging, the supreme one; without affection and without longing—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Atisīlo atisanto, ativiññū arahato;

Aggatthapatto aggatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.588.

Of supreme virtue, supremely tranquil, of supreme wisdom, the Arahant; who has attained the highest goal, the supreme being—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Adhisīlo adhicitto, adhipañño adhigato;

Apalokitadassāvī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.589.

Of higher virtue, higher mind, and higher wisdom, one who has realized; the seer who looks straight ahead—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Attadīpo attasaraṇo, attanātho adhivaro;

Accantuttamo aggabhū, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.590.

An island unto himself, his own refuge, his own protector, the most excellent one; utterly supreme, the highest being—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Anabhikaṅkho aniccho, arāgo anapekkhako;

Ananugiddho alobho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.591.

Without longing and without desire, without passion and without expectation; not greedy and without greed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Anabhilāso anedho, aviruḷhitaṇho isi;

Ucchinnarāgo alaggo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.592.

The sage without longing, without fuel, whose craving has not grown; with passion cut off, unattached—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Naṭṭharāgo naṭṭhadoso, naṭṭhamoho naṭṭhamado;

Naṭṭhakodho naṭṭhakopo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.593.

With lust destroyed, hate destroyed, delusion destroyed, and conceit destroyed; with anger destroyed and ill will destroyed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Vinaṭṭhiñjo vinaṭṭhindho, vinaṭṭhiccho vinaṭṭhiṇo;

Vinaṭṭhāsā vinaṭṭhissā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.594.

With agitation destroyed, fuel destroyed, desire destroyed, and debt destroyed; with longing destroyed and envy destroyed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Naṭṭhakaṅkhā naṭṭhasaṅkā, naṭṭhabhanti naṭṭhabhamo;

Naṭṭhamāyā, naṭṭhāvijjā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.595.

With doubt destroyed, uncertainty destroyed, error destroyed, and confusion destroyed; with deceit destroyed and ignorance destroyed, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Amāyo aparicchinno, ujuko ativissuto;

Anomacitto aneḷo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.596.

Without deceit, boundless, upright, and exceedingly renowned; with a faultless mind, stainless, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Atthapassī atthacaro, atthakkhāyī atthakaro;

Atthakāmo attamano, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.597.

Seer of the goal, practitioner of the goal, expounder of the goal, accomplisher of the goal; one who desires the goal, with a delighted mind, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Abbhuto ca acchariyo, cittakathī ovādako!

Aggamaggaṃ suakkhāsi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.598.

Amazing and wonderful, a speaker who delights the mind, an instructor; you have well proclaimed the supreme path, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Akalaṅko akaluso, akasāvo apaṇṇako;

Akukkucco ca akaṅkhī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.599.

Spotless and unblemished, unstained and infallible; without remorse and without doubt, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbaso vijayo sūro, abhayo abhayappado;

Anākulo appabhīto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.600.

The all-conquering hero, fearless, the giver of fearlessness; untroubled and unterrified, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Upekkhako upasanto, anusayaritto isi;

Appaṭibaddho abaddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.601.

Equanimous and peaceful, the sage devoid of underlying tendencies; unattached and unbound, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Atthacārī atthakārī, atthasārī attharato;

Atthañāṇī atthabhāṇī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.602.

Practicing for welfare, accomplishing welfare, pursuing welfare, delighting in welfare; knowing welfare and speaking of welfare, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Anadhivaro asoko, usabho isipuṅgavo;

Anomanāmo anomo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.603.

Unsurpassed and sorrowless, a bull, a chief among sages; of noble name, the noble one, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Anavassutahadayo, ananvāhatacetaso;

Anabhirato aniñjo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.604.

With heart not leaking, with mind not afflicted; not delighting, unshaken, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Appāsaṅko appātaṅko, appākaṅkho appaṅgaṇo;

Appalolo appakampī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.605.

Without anxiety and without affliction, without longing and without blemish; not fickle and unshaken, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Atthaviññū atthavidū, atthakusalo atthavā;

Kusalatthamanuppatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.606.

Discerner of the goal, knower of the goal, skilled in the goal, possessor of the goal; having attained the wholesome goal, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Avicalito akuppo, kesarī’va achambhito;

Avikampito abbhayo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.607.

Unwavering, imperturbable, unterrified like a lion; unshaken and fearless, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Akiñcano anādāno, amataññū amatogadho;

Aggaphalamanuppatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.608.

Without possessions and not grasping, knower of the Deathless, absorbed in the Deathless; having attained the supreme fruit, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Amitaanuggahaṃ katvā, akālikadhammadado;

Accantupakārī nātho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.609.

Having given immeasurable help, the giver of the timeless Dhamma; the Protector, the supreme benefactor, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Anupalitto lokena, toyena padumaṃ yathā;

Nissaṅgacitto nissatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.610.

Unstained by the world, as a lotus by water; with mind detached, free from clinging, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Abbūḷhesiko isi, ukkhittapaligho ujū;

Anuddhato atandito, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.611.

The sage who has pulled out the stake, with crossbar lifted, upright; unagitated and unwearied, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Agiddho alittacitto, ādīnavavidālako;

Pakatatto pahitatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.612.

Not greedy, with mind unsmeared, a destroyer of danger; with self composed, with self resolute, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Aggapuñño aggaviññū, aggīsi aggasekharo;

Aggamaggamanuppatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.613.

Of foremost merit, of foremost wisdom, the foremost sage, the supreme crest; having attained the supreme path, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Aggagocaro aggagū, aggattho aggadesako;

Aggañāṇī aggabhāṇī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.614.

With the supreme resort, gone to the supreme, the supreme goal, the supreme teacher; of supreme knowledge and supreme speech, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Aggapañño aggaṭṭhito, aggasiddho aggayatī;

Aggabhūto aggapati, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.615.

Of supreme wisdom, established in the supreme, perfected in the supreme, striving for the supreme; become the supreme, master of the supreme, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Aggasamaṇo lokaggo, paññaggo aggamaggagū;

Aggadhammaṃ ugghāṭesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.616.

The supreme recluse, the world's highest, pinnacle of wisdom, one who has gone by the supreme path; he revealed the supreme Dhamma, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Aggadhammamanuppatto, aggadhammappakāsako;

Apāyadukkhaṃ nāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.617.

Having attained the supreme Dhamma, the proclaimer of the supreme Dhamma; he destroyed the suffering of the plane of misery, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Aggapuggalo aggaggo, ñāṇaggo ñāṇapuṅgavo;

Dhammapuggalo dhammaggo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.618.

The supreme person, the highest of the high, the pinnacle of knowledge, the chief in knowledge; the person of Dhamma, the supreme in Dhamma, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Aggapassī aggadassī, aggapanthapakāsako;

Aggesī aggadassāvī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.619.

The seer of the supreme, the beholder of the supreme, the proclaimer of the supreme path; the seeker of the supreme, the perceiver of the supreme, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Anantajhānī jhānindo, anantapaññavāridhī;

Anantamedhāsampanno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.620.

Possessor of endless jhāna, lord of jhāna, the ocean of endless wisdom; endowed with endless intelligence, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Anantavijjāvāridhi, anantapaññāsāgaro;

Anantakaruṇāsindhu, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.621.

The ocean of endless true knowledge, the ocean of endless wisdom; the ocean of endless compassion, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbuttamo sattuttamo, sumano samaṇuttamo;

Januttamo jinuttamo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.622.

Highest of all, highest of beings, of good mind, highest of recluses; highest of people, highest of conquerors, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kalyāṇamitto kalyāṇo, maṅgalamitto maṅgalo;

Dhammamitto dhammadado, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.623.

An admirable friend, the admirable one, an auspicious friend, the auspicious one; a friend in the Dhamma, the giver of the Dhamma, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammiṭṭho cāpi dhammaṭṭho, paññiṭṭho cāpi paññaṭṭho;

Suddhiṭṭho cāpi suddhaṭṭho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.624.

Desiring Dhamma and standing in Dhamma, desiring wisdom and standing in wisdom; desiring purity and standing in purity, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kāyāsakkhī cittasakkhī, dhammasakkhī sakkhīvaro;

Vedanāsakkhī susakkhī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.625.

The body-witness, the mind-witness, the Dhamma-witness, the best of witnesses; the feeling-witness, the excellent witness, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sīghapañño sunipuṇo, santipatto sukhādhipo;

Kalyāṇakārī kusalo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.626.

Of swift wisdom, very subtle, having attained peace, the lord of happiness; a doer of good, skillful, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Susīlaggo sucittaggo, supaññaggo susobhito;

Aggamatthamanuppatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.627.

The pinnacle of good virtue, the pinnacle of good mind, the pinnacle of good wisdom, most splendid; having attained the highest goal, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Upariṭṭho ujjugato, ukkaṭṭho amatāvaho;

Appadambho appagabbo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.628.

Standing above, gone straight, pre-eminent, the bringer of the Deathless; without ostentation and without conceit, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Appasoko apparogo, appabhīti appabhayo;

Appapāpo appatāpo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.629.

Without sorrow and without illness, without dread and without terror; without evil and without anguish, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Apparoso appadoso, appadoho appindhano;

Appābhisajjo appīgho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.630.

Without anger and without aversion, without malice and without fuel; without hostility and without affliction, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Accantakaruṇākārī, accantamettacetaso;

Accantamudito santo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.631.

A doer of extreme compassion, with a mind of extreme loving-kindness; extremely joyful and peaceful, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ekappatto ekanātho, eko appaṭipuggalo;

Ekova sabbalokasmiṃ, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.632.

Having attained the one, the one protector, the one without a counterpart; the only one in all the world, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Atuliyo atitulo, adutiyo anuttaro;

Appameyyo appaṭimo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.633.

Incomparable and beyond compare, without a second and unsurpassed; immeasurable and without a counterpart, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Atuladassī atulo, asarikkho anupamo;

Asamo yo asadiso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.634.

The seer of the incomparable, the incomparable one, without likeness and peerless; he who is without equal, without a similar, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Amito aparimito, amitañāṇasāgaro;

Aparitto appamāṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.635.

Immeasurable and unbounded, an ocean of limitless knowledge; unlimited and without measure, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Asīmo asamasamo, atulyo amitaguṇo;

Anupameyyo agādho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.636.

Without limit, equal to the unequalled, incomparable, of immeasurable virtues; beyond compare and unfathomable, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Nipparicchinno nissīmo, nappamañño jinavaro;

Nappamāṇo nirupamo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.637.

Unbounded and limitless, not to be conceived, the best of conquerors; without measure and without compare, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Natthi añño etādiso, nipako ekapuggalo;

Ekantasukhasaṃvedī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.638.

There is no other such wise and unique individual; one who experiences exclusive happiness, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Aggavasabho asamo, asamapaṭipuggalo;

Ekako ekapuriso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.639.

The chief bull, the unequalled one, the person with no equal counterpart; the solitary one, the unique person, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbattha āsattisuñño, sabbathāmuttamānaso;

Sabbadā ekasadiso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.640.

Devoid of all attachments, whose mind is utterly liberated; always constant, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pāpasūlaṃ vibhañjesi, pāpamūlassa chedako;

Pāparajjuṃ vikantesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.641.

You shattered the stake of evil, the cutter of the root of evil; you cut the rope of evil, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pāpanettiṃ nijjaresi, pāpakammavināsako;

Sabbapāpaparikkhīṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.642.

You destroyed the leading-string of evil, the destroyer of evil deeds; whose evil is utterly exhausted, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Samūlaṃ khaṇito pāpaṃ, pāpavāridhipāragū;

Pāpaganthiṃ vimocesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.643.

Who has uprooted evil with its root, who has gone to the far shore of the ocean of evil; you released the knot of evil, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pāpacakkaṃ vicuṇṇetvā, dhammacakkaṃ pavattayī;

Bahūjane uddhāresi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.644.

Having crushed the wheel of evil, he set the Wheel of Dhamma in motion; he rescued many people, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Nijjaresi pāpakammaṃ, mahāpāpoghapāragū;

Sabbapāpavītivatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.645.

You wore away evil deeds, who has gone to the far shore of the flood of great evil; who has transcended all evil, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbapāpamatikkanto, sabbapāpasamūhato;

Sabbapāpasamucchinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.646.

Who has surpassed all evil, who has uprooted all evil; who has utterly cut off all evil, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pāpasotaṃ visosesi, pāpatāpapanūdano;

Pāpachandaṃ vicchindesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.647.

You dried up the stream of evil, the dispeller of the fever of evil; you severed the desire for evil, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pāpatāsavinimutto, pāpapāvakanibbuto;

Pāpabandhaṃ vimocesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.648.

Who is completely freed from the terror of evil, whose fire of evil is extinguished; you released the bond of evil, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pāpasallaṃ vicuṇṇesi, pāpacakkavidālako;

Pāparogasokakhīṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.649.

You crushed the dart of evil, the breaker of the wheel of evil; whose evil-disease and sorrow are exhausted, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pāpamohaparimutto, pāpasaṃtāsanāsako;

Pāpakhobhaṃ vikkhambhesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.650.

Who is completely freed from the delusion of evil, the destroyer of the terror of evil; you suppressed the agitation of evil, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kaṇhakalusaṃ dhopesi, pāpamalapakkhālako;

Sabbaklesaṃ vodāpesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.651.

You washed away darkness and stain, the cleanser of the filth of evil; you purified all defilements, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pāpapariḷāhamutto, vimuttapāpatassanā;

Pāpasantāpavimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.652.

Who is freed from the burning of evil, who is liberated from the craving for evil; who is completely freed from the torment of evil, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pāpaupadhimucchinno, pāpapalighabhaggavā;

Pāpadheyyavītivatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.653.

Who has cut off the basis for evil, who has broken the bar of evil; who has transcended the dominion of evil, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pāpapuññapanūdako, bhavabandhanabhañjako;

Pāpakaṇṭakakantako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.654.

The dispeller of evil and merit, the breaker of the bonds of existence; the remover of the thorns of evil, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbapāpaṃ padālesi, sabbapāpaṃ pariccajī;

Sabbapāpehi nissaṭo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.655.

You shattered all evil, you abandoned all evil; escaped from all evil, I bow to that Buddha.
