Sabbapāpaṃ pavāhesi, sabbapāpajigucchako;

Sabbapāpehi vimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.656.

You washed away all evil, the detester of all evil; completely freed from all evil, I bow to that Buddha.

Sabbapāpaṃ virajjesi, sabbapāpaniddhotako;

Sabbapāpaṃ niddhaṃsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.657.

You cleansed away all evil, the cleanser of all evil; you destroyed all evil, I bow to that Buddha.

Pāpaṇṇavasamuttiṇṇo, chinnabhavasaṃyojano;

Punabbhavaparikkhīṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.658.

Who has completely crossed over the ocean of evil, whose fetters of existence are cut; whose rebirth is utterly exhausted, I bow to that Buddha.

Pāpapaṅkaṃ pakkhālesi, dhammasotapavāhako;

Saddhammaṃ patiṭṭhāpesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.659.

You washed away the mire of evil, the one who made the Dhamma-stream flow; you established the true Dhamma, I bow to that Buddha.

Pāputtāpaṃ samucchinno, mohuttāpaṃ sītikaro;

Sabbuttāpaṃ nibbāpesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.660.

Who has utterly cut off the fever of evil, the cooler of the fever of delusion; you extinguished all fever, I bow to that Buddha.

Pāpahārī tāpahārī, sukhakārī khemaṅkaro;

Mettāvihārī mārārī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.661.

The remover of evil, the remover of suffering, the maker of happiness, the bringer of security; abiding in loving-kindness, the enemy of Māra, I bow to that Buddha.

Saṃyojanaparikkhīṇo, pāpatāpaviddhaṃsako;

Antimabhavasampatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.662.

Whose fetters are utterly exhausted, the destroyer of the fever of evil; who has attained the final existence, I bow to that Buddha.

Paṭipadāpaṭipanno, dukkhadāhapanūdano;

Pāpatāpaparikkhīṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.663.

Who has entered upon the path, the dispeller of the burning of suffering; whose fever of evil is utterly exhausted, I bow to that Buddha.

Puññapāpaparikkhīṇo, sokasallavinodano;

Padhānapahitatto yo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.664.

Whose merit and evil are utterly exhausted, the dispeller of sorrow's dart; he who has exerted himself in striving, I bow to that Buddha.

Cakkhumā sabbadhammesu, sabbapāpappabhañjako;

Sabbasaṃyojanahantā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.665.

The one with vision in all phenomena, the breaker of all evil; the destroyer of all fetters, I bow to that Buddha.

Sabbapāpaṃ pakkhālesi, sabbakilesasodhako;

Sabbattha vimalo suddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.666.

You washed away all evil, the cleanser of all defilements; stainless and pure in every way, I bow to that Buddha.

Sabbapāpaṃ nimmaddesi, sabbatāpasamūhato;

Sabbasantāpaṃ hāpesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.667.

You crushed all evil, who has uprooted all torment; you removed all anguish, I bow to that Buddha.

Sabbūpadhīnaṃ nissaggo, sabbāsānaṃ virajjako;

Sabbapāpaṃ pabhañjesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.668.

The relinquishment of all substrata, the one who is dispassionate towards all desires; you shattered all evil, I bow to that Buddha.

Pāragū pārasampatto, āsavasotasosako;

Sabbapāpaṃ pahāsi yo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.669.

Who has gone to the far shore, who has attained the far shore, the dryer-up of the streams of influxes; he who abandoned all evil, I bow to that Buddha.

Sabbāsavaṃ viddhaṃsesi, sabbamohasamūhato;

Sabbapāpaṃ vivajjesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.670.

You destroyed all influxes, who has uprooted all delusion; you avoided all evil, I bow to that Buddha.

Sabbāsā samatikkanto, sabbarāgaggi nibbuto;

Sabbapāpaṃ parimaddi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.671.

Who has completely surpassed all desires, whose fire of all passion is extinguished; you crushed all evil, I bow to that Buddha.

Sabbakāmabhavatiṇṇo, sabbavibhavavajjito;

Sabbapāputtāpakkhīṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.672.

Who has crossed over all sensual existence, who is free from all craving for non-existence; whose fever of all evil is exhausted, I bow to that Buddha.

Sabbanīvaraṇātīto, sabbapāsappamocano;

Māramarīcikā bhañji, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.673.

Who has gone beyond all hindrances, the liberator from all snares; he shattered Māra's mirage, I bow to that Buddha.

Saraṇadāyako sāmī, parittāṇappadāyako;

Ārakkhaṇadātā satthā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.674.

The giver of refuge, the Lord, the giver of protection; the giver of safety, the Teacher, I bow to that Buddha.

Saccassa kovido cāpi, asaccassāpi kovido;

Catusaccaṃ ugghosesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.675.

Skilled in truth and also skilled in untruth; you proclaimed the Four Noble Truths, I bow to that Buddha.

Bhaddassa kovido cāpi, abhaddassāpi kovido;

Sabbato bhaddaṃ dassāvī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.676.

Skilled in the good and also skilled in the bad; the one who shows the good in every way, I bow to that Buddha.

Ākaṅkhāya kovido ca, anākaṅkhāya kovido;

Sabbesu ca nirākaṅkho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.677.

Skilled in longing and skilled in non-longing; and without longing in all things, I bow to that Buddha.

Lolassa kovido cāpi, alolassāpi kovido;

Nillobho api nillolo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.678.

Skilled in craving and also skilled in non-craving; without greed and without craving, I bow to that Buddha.

Papañcassa kovido ca, nippapañcassa kovido;

Nippapañco ca nicchalo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.679.

Skilled in proliferation and skilled in non-proliferation; free from proliferation and unwavering, I bow to that Buddha.

Sabbe dhamme pabodhesi, sabbapāpehi mocako;

Sabbadukkhavītivatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.680.

You awakened to all phenomena, the liberator from all evil; who has transcended all suffering, I bow to that Buddha.

Viduro sabbadhammesu, pāpappahānapāragū;

Sabbasaṅkhārūpasamo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.681.

The wise one in all phenomena, who has gone to the far shore of abandoning evil; for whom all formations are stilled, I bow to that Buddha.

Saccadhammaṃ vitthāresi, sabbapāpavināsako;

Pāpāyatanā vimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.682.

You expounded the true Dhamma, the destroyer of all evil; completely freed from the sphere of evil, I bow to that Buddha.

Sabbadhammānupassī yo, sabbapāpaṃ visosayī;

Sabbataṇhā pahāsi yo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.683.

He who contemplates all phenomena, who dried up all evil; he who has abandoned all craving, I bow to that Buddha.

Pāpapāsaṃ pamocesi, sabbadhammāna vedagū;

Sabbupadhīhi vimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.684.

You released from the snare of evil, the knower of all phenomena; completely freed from all substrata, I bow to that Buddha.

Cakkavattī dhammarājā, dhammacakkavibhūsito;

Adhammacakkaṃ cuṇṇesī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.685.

The Wheel-turning King of Dhamma, adorned with the Wheel of Dhamma; He crushed the wheel of unrighteousness, to that Buddha I bow.

Saddhammamuddhābhisito, dhammamahārañño budho;

Dhammasāsana ṭhāpesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.686.

Consecrated with the True Dhamma, the enlightened great king of Dhamma; He established the Dispensation of the Dhamma, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammanātho dhammassāmī, dhammīso dhammasāsako;

Dhammasūro dhammasatthā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.687.

The Lord of Dhamma, the Master of Dhamma, the Dhamma-lord, the Dhamma-ruler; The Dhamma-hero, the Dhamma-Teacher, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammarañño dhammapati, dhammavattī dhammissaro;

Dhammabhūpo dhammādhipo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.688.

The king of Dhamma, the master of Dhamma, the turner of Dhamma, the lord of Dhamma; The king of Dhamma, the sovereign of Dhamma, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammamahārājādhipo, sabbathā dhammasammato;

Dhammasamattho dhammīso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.689.

The great sovereign king of Dhamma, in every way esteemed in the Dhamma; Capable in Dhamma, the lord of Dhamma, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammatāṇo dhammaleṇo, dhammasaraṇadāyako;

Dhammasakko dhammāsayo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.690.

The Dhamma-protection, the Dhamma-shelter, the giver of the Dhamma-refuge; Capable in Dhamma, the abode of Dhamma, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammadātā dhammatātā, dhammakkhātā dhammasakhā;

Dhammasukho dhammahito, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.691.

The giver of Dhamma, the father of Dhamma, the expounder of Dhamma, the friend of Dhamma; Whose happiness is Dhamma, the benefactor through Dhamma, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammanāgo dhammasīho, dhammājañño dhammusabho;

Dhammadhurandharo dhīro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.692.

The Dhamma-elephant, the Dhamma-lion, the Dhamma-thoroughbred, the Dhamma-bull; The bearer of the Dhamma-burden, the wise one, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammasiṅgo dhammatuṅgo, dhammameru dhammagiri;

Dhammakūṭo dhammasīso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.693.

The peak of Dhamma, the height of Dhamma, the Meru of Dhamma, the mountain of Dhamma; The peak of Dhamma, the head of Dhamma, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammādicco dhammappabho, dibbo dhammavibhākaro;

Dhammabhākaro dhammābho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.694.

The sun of Dhamma, the radiance of Dhamma, the divine illuminator of Dhamma; The illuminator of Dhamma, the splendor of Dhamma, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammasiromaṇi sāmī, dhammasikharasekharo;

Dhammacūḷāmaṇi cāru, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.695.

The Dhamma crest-jewel, the Lord; the crest of the Dhamma-peak; The beautiful Dhamma crest-jewel, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammabhūsanabhūsito, dhammālaṅkāra’laṅkito;

Dhammābharaṇasobhito, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.696.

Adorned with the ornament of Dhamma, decorated with the embellishment of Dhamma; Shining with the adornment of Dhamma, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammadīpo dhammobhāso, dhammaloko dhammajuti;

Dhammapakāso dhammaṃsu, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.697.

The lamp of Dhamma, the light of Dhamma, the world of Dhamma, the splendor of Dhamma; The manifestation of Dhamma, the ray of Dhamma, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammagutto pāpamutto, dhammayutto dhammālayo;

Dhammañatto dhammasañño, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.698.

Guarded by Dhamma, freed from evil, yoked to Dhamma, whose abode is Dhamma; Renowned in Dhamma, who perceives Dhamma, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammadassī dhammapassī, dhammapekkhī vicakkhaṇo;

Dhammavipassī supassī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.699.

Seer of Dhamma, observer of Dhamma, who regards Dhamma, the discerning one; Who sees Dhamma with insight, who sees Dhamma well, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammadakkhī dhammasakkhī, dhammarakkhī dhammadharo;

Saddhammapekkhī upekkhī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.700.

Seer of Dhamma, witness to Dhamma, protector of Dhamma, bearer of Dhamma; Who regards the True Dhamma, the equanimous one, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammasañño dhammapañño, dhammaabhiñño dhammañño;

Dhañño dhammaparāyaṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.701.

Perceiver of Dhamma, wise in Dhamma, with direct knowledge of Dhamma, knower of Dhamma; The blessed one, whose ultimate resort is Dhamma, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammañāṇī dhammabhāṇī, dhammavācī dhammavidū;

Mantabhāṇī madhubhāṇī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.702.

The knower of Dhamma, the speaker of Dhamma, the utterer of Dhamma, the wise in Dhamma; The speaker of sacred texts, the speaker of honeyed words, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammavā dhammasampanno, ñāṇavā ñāṇasāgaro;

Paññavā paññāvāridhi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.703.

Endowed with Dhamma, accomplished in Dhamma, possessing knowledge, an ocean of knowledge; Possessing wisdom, a sea of wisdom, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammakkho dhammarakkhako, buddhakkho bodhirakkhako;

Paññakkho cāpi medhakkho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.704.

The eye of Dhamma, the protector of Dhamma; the Buddha-eye, the protector of enlightenment; The eye of wisdom and also the eye of intelligence, to that Buddha I bow.

Adhammamutto dhammabhā, aññāṇamutto ñāṇabhā;

Avijjāmutto vijjābhā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.705.

Freed from unrighteousness, he shines with righteousness; freed from ignorance, he shines with knowledge; Freed from spiritual ignorance, he shines with true knowledge, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammasindhu dhammodadhi, anantadhammasāgaro;

Saddhammaratnākaro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.706.

The ocean of Dhamma, the ocean of Dhamma, the endless ocean of Dhamma; The mine of jewels of the True Dhamma, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammapaṭibhānappatto, dhammappaṭipadāvidū;

Paṭisambhidāmaggaññū, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.707.

Attained to eloquence in Dhamma, skilled in the practice of Dhamma; Knower of the path of analytical knowledge, to that Buddha I bow.

Tuṭṭho dhammasantuṭṭhito, dhīro dhammapatiṭṭhito;

Aggapatiṭṭhito aggo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.708.

Pleased and contented with the Dhamma, the wise one established in the Dhamma; Established in the highest, the supreme one, to that Buddha I bow.

Mokkhaddhajaṃ unnāmesi, māraddhajaṃ onāmako;

Pāpahārī sokahārī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.709.

Sabbāsavaṃ vivajjitvā, pattadhammaniyyāniko;

Samiddhiguṇasampanno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.710.

He raised the banner of liberation, he lowered the banner of Māra; the dispeller of evil, the remover of sorrow, to that Buddha I bow. Having abandoned all cankers, he attained the Dhamma leading to deliverance; endowed with flourishing virtues, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammadhārā pavāhesi, pāpadhārānirodhako;

Atittānaṃ sutosesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.711.

He caused the stream of Dhamma to flow, the stopper of the streams of evil; He thoroughly satisfied the unsatisfied, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammamegho paññāmegho, dhammāmataṃ pavassayī;

Rajojallaṃ vidhovesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.712.

The rain-cloud of Dhamma, the rain-cloud of wisdom; He caused the deathless Dhamma to rain down; He washed away the defilement of dust, to that Buddha I bow.

Dhammañatto dhammakkhāyī, dhammamaggassa desako;

Dhammiṃ dhammaṃ vitthāresi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.713.

The knower of Dhamma, the declarer of Dhamma, the teacher of the path of Dhamma; He spread the righteous Dhamma, to that Buddha I bow.

Sabbañeyyadhammaṃ ñatvā, ñattadhammaṃ suvitarī;

Abhiññāñāṇasampuṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.714.

Having known all knowable phenomena, having well crossed over the known phenomena; Complete with the knowledge of direct apprehension, to that Buddha I bow.

Sabbasaddhammasampatto, tiṇṇasaṃsārasāgaro;

Sabbapāpaparimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.715.

Accomplished in all True Dhamma, having crossed the ocean of saṃsāra; Completely freed from all evil, to that Buddha I bow.

Siddhadhammo siddhaattho, sabbasaṃsārapūjito;

Sabbuttamo sammacārī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.716.

He whose Dhamma is accomplished, whose aim is accomplished, revered by all in saṃsāra; the highest of all, of right conduct—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammakārī dhammadhārī, dhammacārī dhammayuto;

Dhammasārī dhammabhāvī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.717.

The doer of Dhamma, the upholder of Dhamma, the practitioner of Dhamma, united with Dhamma; whose essence is Dhamma, the developer of Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammajeṭṭho dhammaseṭṭho, dhammasuddho dhammasucī;

Dhammaketu dhammaddhajo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.718.

The foremost in Dhamma, the supreme in Dhamma, the pure in Dhamma, the stainless in Dhamma; the banner of Dhamma, the standard of Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammakāmo dhammabhūto, dhammābhiramanto muni;

Dhammānurāgī virāgī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.719.

The lover of Dhamma, he who has become Dhamma, the sage delighting in Dhamma; the one devoted to Dhamma, the dispassionate one—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammañāto pāpanudo, dhammavihārī dhammiko;

Dassesi amataṃ dhammaṃ, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.720.

The one who knows Dhamma, the dispeller of evil, the one who dwells in Dhamma, the righteous one; he revealed the deathless Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammakāyo dhammarūpo, dhammanāmo dhammamudo;

Dhammapatiṭṭho muttiṭṭho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.721.

The Dhamma-body, the Dhamma-form, the Dhamma-name, the Dhamma-seal; established in Dhamma, established in liberation—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammadhārako dhoreyho, dhammapuṇṇo’va puṇṇindu;

Amatogadhaṃ sampatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.722.

The upholder of Dhamma, the foremost one, full of Dhamma like the full moon; who has attained the profound Deathless—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammatikicchako dakkho, visallo sallakantako;

Mahopakārī bhisakko, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.723.

The physician of Dhamma, the skillful one, the dart-free one, the extractor of darts; the great benefactor, the healer—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammasakko dhammasiddho, dhammamakuṭasobhano;

Dhammakanto dhammasanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.724.

The mighty in Dhamma, the accomplished in Dhamma, radiant with the crown of Dhamma; delighting in Dhamma, tranquil in Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammāñāṇī dhammakathī, dhammadāyī maggadado;

Dhammavibhū dhammapabhū, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.725.

The knower of Dhamma, the speaker of Dhamma, the giver of Dhamma, the bestower of the path; the master of Dhamma, the source of Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Amatadhammaṃ ñāpesi, santāresi bahujanaṃ;

Āturānaṃ tikicchako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.726.

He made known the Deathless Dhamma, he brought many people to safety; the healer of the afflicted—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammabhāgī pāpacāgī, dhammabhajī pāpaccajo;

Dhammarāgī dhammaramo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.727.

A partaker of Dhamma, an abandoner of evil, a devotee of Dhamma, a rejecter of evil; one who is fond of Dhamma, one who rejoices in Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammaviññū dhammavidvā, dhammavijjo dhammayuto;

Dhammavattā dhammavyatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.728.

The discerner of Dhamma, the wise in Dhamma, the one with Dhamma-knowledge, endowed with Dhamma; the speaker of Dhamma, the articulate in Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammānudhammadhārako, tāsānutāsanāsako;

Bhayānubhayabhañjako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.729.

The upholder of Dhamma and what accords with Dhamma; the destroyer of terror and trembling; the breaker of fear and dread—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Visuddhadhammadhārako, sabbadhi upakārako;

Cārucaraṇacārako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.730.

The upholder of pure Dhamma, the benefactor in every way; the one whose conduct is lovely—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammamakarandapāyī, muducitto madhukaro;

Saddhammasudhāmathano, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.731.

The drinker of the Dhamma-nectar, the gentle-hearted one, the honey-maker; the churner of the nectar of the True Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ehipassikaṃ dhammadado, sandiṭṭhikamakālikaṃ;

Opanayikaṃ svākkhātaṃ, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.732.

The giver of the well-proclaimed Dhamma, which is 'come and see', directly visible, timeless, and leading onward—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Suddhadhammāmataṃ pāyī, subhuñjesi muttiphalaṃ;

Santibhojī sukhabhakkhī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.733.

The drinker of the pure Deathless Dhamma, he well enjoyed the fruit of liberation; the feeder on peace, the consumer of happiness—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mānito mahimāvanto, mahiddhiko mahīyato;

Mahāsaddhammādhipati, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.734.

The honored one, the majestic one, the one of great power, the greatly revered one; the sovereign lord of the Great True Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Attasaraṇamanusāsi, nepakko anusāsako;

Dhammasaraṇamanusāsi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.735.

He taught self-refuge, the skillful instructor; he taught Dhamma as refuge—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bodhipakkhiyadhammānaṃ, bhāvito bahulīkato;

Sammāsambodhisampatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.736.

He who has developed and made abundant the factors of enlightenment; who has attained perfect self-enlightenment—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Matimā dhitisampanno, dhīravaro sudhammiko;

Dhammaṭṭhappaṭisaṃyutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.737.

The intelligent one, endowed with resolve, the noble steadfast one, the truly righteous one; connected with the established Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhamma’jjhāsayakusalo, dhammesu akathaṃkathī;

Paramatthamanuppatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.738.

Skilled in the inclination towards Dhamma, without doubt regarding the Dhammas; who has attained the ultimate goal—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhitibalo dhitidharo, dhitidhārī dhīsekharo;

Uttamadhammesī dhīro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.739.

Strong in resolve, upholding resolve, bearing resolve, the crown of wisdom; the steadfast seeker of the highest Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbasantāpaṃ dhaṃsesi, sabbapāpavinodano;

Sabbadhammaphalappatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.740.

He destroyed all torment, the dispeller of all evil; who has attained the fruit of all Dhammas—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Virato sabbapāpehi, sabbādhammehi muñcito;

Sabbadukkhabyantikaro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.741.

Abstained from all evil, released from all unrighteousness; the one who brings an end to all suffering—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbadhi pāpavimutto, suddhadhamme patiṭṭhito;

Sabbadukkhā pamocesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.742.

Liberated from evil in every way, established in pure Dhamma; he liberated from all suffering—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbapāpappahīno yo, sabbadhammāna pāragū;

Ghoradukkhoghauttiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.743.

He who has abandoned all evil, who has gone beyond all phenomena; crossed over the flood of terrible suffering—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbādhammaṃ viddhaṃsesi, sabbapāpakkhayaṅkaro;

Kattako sabbabandhānaṃ, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.744.

He destroyed all unrighteousness, the one who brings about the destruction of all evil, the cutter of all bonds—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbādhidhammaṃ pālesi, sabbapāpapanūdano;

Sabbadukkhaṃ niddhaṃsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.745.

He protected all supreme Dhamma, the dispeller of all evil; he destroyed all suffering—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dukkhamocako mokkhado, samattho satthavāhako;

Pāpanāsī dhammabhāsī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.746.

The liberator from suffering, the giver of liberation, the capable one, the caravan leader; the destroyer of evil, the speaker of Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammabhajo pāpaccajo, rāgaccajo cāgabhajo;

Sukhabhajo dukkhaccajo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.747.

The partaker of Dhamma, the abandoner of evil, the abandoner of lust, the partaker of generosity; the partaker of happiness, the abandoner of suffering—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sammājīvī dhammājīvī, suddhājīvī sucijīvī;

Sīlajīvī saccājīvī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.748.

One of right livelihood, one of Dhamma-livelihood, one of pure livelihood, one of immaculate livelihood; one of virtuous livelihood, one of truthful livelihood—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paññājīvī aññājīvī, ñāṇājīvī varajīvī;

Vijjājīvī viññājīvī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.749.

One whose livelihood is wisdom, one whose livelihood is final knowledge, one whose livelihood is knowledge, one whose livelihood is supreme; one whose livelihood is true knowledge, one whose livelihood is discernment—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Khantijīvī santijīvī, sūjujīvī ujujīvī;

Pītijīvī pītibhojī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.750.

One whose livelihood is patience, one whose livelihood is peace, one whose livelihood is perfectly straightforward, one whose livelihood is straightforward; one whose livelihood is joy, one who feeds on joy—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dibbājīvī bhabbājīvī, bhaddājīvī samajīvī;

Modajīvī mettājīvī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.751.

One whose livelihood is divine, one whose livelihood is fitting, one whose livelihood is auspicious, one whose livelihood is balanced; one whose livelihood is delight, one whose livelihood is loving-kindness—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Guttājīvī muttājīvī, dantājīvī yatajīvī;

Dakkhājīvī seṭṭhājīvī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.752.

One whose livelihood is guarded, one whose livelihood is freed, one whose livelihood is tamed, one whose livelihood is restrained; one whose livelihood is skillful, one whose livelihood is excellent—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Hitajīvī sukhajīvī, jhānajīvī vatajīvī;

Sudhājīvī subhājīvī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.753.

One whose livelihood is beneficial, one whose livelihood is happy, one whose livelihood is meditative absorption, one whose livelihood is vows; one whose livelihood is pure, one whose livelihood is auspicious—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Visuddhajīvī saṃsuddho, santuṭṭhajīvī sutuṭṭho;

Pavarajīvī pakaṭṭho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.754.

One whose livelihood is purified, the thoroughly purified one, one whose livelihood is contentment, the well-contented one; one whose livelihood is supreme, the pre-eminent one—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mettādhārī mettācārī, mettākārī mettālayo;

Mettāsayo mettāsārī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.755.

The upholder of loving-kindness, one whose conduct is loving-kindness, the maker of loving-kindness, the abode of loving-kindness; the foundation of loving-kindness, the essence of loving-kindness—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Santidhārī santikārī, santicārī santimano;

Santisārī santicitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.756.

The upholder of peace, the maker of peace, one whose conduct is peace, the peaceful-minded one; the essence of peace, the peaceful-hearted one—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Khantidhārī khantikārī, khanticārī khantimano;

Khantiyutto khantisārī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.757.

The upholder of patience, the maker of patience, one whose conduct is patience, the patient-minded one; endowed with patience, the essence of patience—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sukhakārī sukhacārī, sukhadhārī sukhāvaho;

Sukhasārī sukhacitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.758.

The maker of happiness, one whose conduct is happiness, the upholder of happiness, the bringer of happiness; the essence of happiness, the happy-minded one—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Hitakārī hitadhārī, hitacārī hitāvaho;

Hitasārī hitesino, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.759.

The doer of welfare, the upholder of welfare, one whose conduct is welfare, the bringer of welfare; the essence of welfare, the seeker of welfare—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paññācārī paññāsārī, paññādhārī paññālayo;

Paññābhānu paññādicco, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.760.

One whose conduct is wisdom, the essence of wisdom, the upholder of wisdom, the abode of wisdom; the radiance of wisdom, the sun of wisdom—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Medhācārī medhāsārī, medhādhārī medhālayo;

Medhācando medhāindo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.761.

One whose conduct is intelligence, the essence of intelligence, the upholder of intelligence, the abode of intelligence; the moon of intelligence, the lord of intelligence—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mettaṅkaro modaṅkaro, kusalo kusalaṅkaro;

Medhācārī medhaṅkaro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.762.

The maker of loving-kindness, the maker of joy, the skillful one, the maker of the wholesome; one whose conduct is intelligence, the maker of intelligence—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pabhaṅkaro divaṅkaro, kāruñño karuṇaṅkaro;

Nibbhīto nibbhayaṅkaro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.763.

The maker of light, the maker of day, the compassionate one, the maker of compassion; the fearless one, the maker of fearlessness—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sivaṅkaro subhaṅkaro, sukhaṅkaro hitaṅkaro;

Sallaharo sūlaharo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.764.

The maker of welfare, the maker of auspiciousness, the maker of happiness, the maker of benefit; the remover of darts, the remover of pain—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahākhemaṅkaro khemī, mahāpaññaṅkaro pabhū;

Mahāsantikaro santo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.765.

The great maker of security, the secure one, the great maker of wisdom, the lord; the great maker of peace, the tranquil one—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Anāthānaṃ bhavanātho, bhītānaṃ abhayaṅkaro;

Saraṇaṅkaro dīnānaṃ, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.766.

A protector for the helpless, a maker of fearlessness for the fearful; a maker of refuge for the distressed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dūradassī dīghadassī, antadassī bhavantagū;

Tīradassī pāradassī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.767.

The Far-Seeing, the Long-Seeing, the End-Seeing, one who has reached the end of existence; the Near-Shore-Seeing, the Far-Shore-Seeing—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sāradassī saccadassī, sivadassī sudassiko;

Yogadassī khemadassī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.768.

The Seer of the Essence, the Seer of Truth, the Seer of the Auspicious, the Fair to See; the Seer of the Yokes, the Seer of Security—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Santidassī sukhadassī, suddhidassī suddhamano;

Aggadassī dhuvadassī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.769.

The Seer of Peace, the Seer of Happiness, the Seer of Purity, the Pure-Minded One; the Seer of the Supreme, the Seer of the Stable—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Lokuttaradhammadassī, pariyantadassī isī;

Maggadassī phaladassī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.770.

The Seer of the Supramundane Dhamma, the Sage who sees the end; the Seer of the Path, the Seer of the Fruit—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Atthadassī pathadassī, anomadassī sudassī;

Paramapassī supassī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.771.

The Seer of the Goal, the Seer of the Path, the Seer of the Excellent, the Clear-Seeing One; one who sees the supreme, one who sees well—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Khayadassī vayadassī, nayadassī sudassano;

Akkhayadassī sudassī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.772.

The Seer of Destruction, the Seer of Vanishing, the Seer of the Method, the Fair to See; the Seer of the Imperishable, the Clear-Seeing One—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Amatadassī paccakkho, pekkhako parisodhako;

Paṭisambhidāsampatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.773.

The Seer of the Deathless, one who has directly realized, the Observer, the Purifier; one who has attained the analytical knowledges—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbadhi sabbadassāvī, sabbapassī sabbavidū;

Sabbaṃ sammā abhaññāsi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.774.

Seeing all everywhere, seeing all things, knowing all things; he has directly known all things rightly—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Hantvā kāmabhavataṇhā, vibhavataṇhābhañjako;

Sabbataṇhā vicuṇṇesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.775.

Having slain craving for sensual pleasures and for existence, the destroyer of craving for non-existence; he has pulverized all craving—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Taṇhāsaṃyojanakkhayo, taṇhāsallavicuṇṇako;

Taṇhāpihā padāḷesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.776.

The destroyer of the fetter of craving, the pulverizer of the dart of craving; he has rent asunder the yearning of craving—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kāmataṇhāparikkhīṇo, bhavataṇhāupaccago;

Vibhavataṇhāvinaṭṭho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.777.

He in whom craving for sensual pleasures is exhausted, who has gone beyond craving for existence, in whom craving for non-existence is destroyed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Tuṭṭhimante tosavante, atassante sutappayī;

Taṇhātīto sadātitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.778.

The Contented, the Pleased, the Unthirsty, the Well-Satisfied; the one who has gone beyond craving, the ever-satiated—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kilesajālaviddhaṃsī, taṇhāmalapakkhālako;

Saṃsārasotaṃ sosesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.779.

The destroyer of the net of defilements, the one who washes away the stain of craving; he has dried up the stream of saṃsāra—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Upadhipaṭinissaggo, taṇhājālappabhedako;

Nīvaraṇe pavijjhesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.780.

He who has relinquished the substrates of existence, the breaker of the net of craving; he has pierced through the hindrances—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Guhāsayaparikkhīṇo, pahīnataṇhānissayo;

Chinnaavijjānusayo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.781.

He in whom dwelling in the cave is exhausted, who has abandoned craving as his support, who has severed the underlying tendency of ignorance—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Taṇhāsallaṃ hanitvāna, tevijjo maccuhāyano;

Sambodhimuttamaṃ patto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.782.

Having destroyed the dart of craving, the possessor of the threefold knowledge, the conqueror of Death; he has attained the supreme enlightenment—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Taṇhājaṭā vijaṭesi, sabbaṭhāne nehanudo;

Nandīrāgaṃ viddhaṃsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.783.

He has disentangled the tangle of craving, the dispeller of affection in all places; he has shattered delight and lust—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbāsāsamatikkanto, taṇhāsotavisosako;

Dhammasotaṃ pavāhesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.784.

He who has overcome all longings, the dryer-up of the stream of craving; he has set the stream of Dhamma in motion—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbataṇhakkhayaṃ patto, sabbadukkhappanūdano;

Sabbupadhi sandālesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.785.

He who has attained the destruction of all craving, the dispeller of all suffering; he has shattered all substrates of existence—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbataṇhā vikkhambhesi, paṭippassaddhasabbiccho;

Sabbiñjā samucchindesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.786.

He has suppressed all craving, he in whom all desire is stilled; he has cut off all disturbances—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbiñjā paribhañjesi, sabbataṇhātigo yatī;

Sabbicchā paricajjesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.787.

He has shattered all disturbances, the ascetic who has overcome all craving; he has abandoned all wishes—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Taṇhāsāgaranittiṇṇo, sabbamicchamanicchako;

Cittasallaṃ vimocesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.788.

He who has crossed the ocean of craving, desiring nothing at all; he has released the dart from the mind—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kammavaṭṭaṃ vivajjesi, kammaklesā apagato;

Sabbattha saṃvuto sūro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.789.

He has abandoned the round of kamma, he from whom the defilements of kamma are gone; the hero restrained in every way—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Asatto upasantatto, kāmakodhabhayātigo;

Bhavakammajaho cheko, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.790.

The unattached, the tranquil one, who has gone beyond lust, anger, and fear; the skillful abandoner of kamma that leads to existence—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Vigatasārado sīho, chinnachambho chinnadaro;

Bhavakammakkhayappatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.791.

The lion devoid of timidity, who has cut off rigidity and anxiety; he who has attained the destruction of kamma that leads to existence—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kammabījāni jhāpesi, mohamūlapalikhaṇo;

Nijjaresi anusaye, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.792.

He has burned up the seeds of kamma, the uprooter of the root of delusion; he has worn away the underlying tendencies—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbakammaparikkhīṇo, sabbavipākanijjaro;

Sabbapuññapāpañjaho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.793.

He in whom all kamma is exhausted, for whom all results are worn away; the abandoner of all merit and demerit—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbakammaṃ kilese ca, asesamabhivāhayī;

Sabbagaṇṭhiṃ vimocesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.794.

He has done away with all kamma and defilements without remainder; he has released all knots—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbakammehi vimutto, sabbakammapariccajo;

Sabbakāmaguṇā’peto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.795.

Freed from all kamma, the relinquisher of all kamma; devoid of all strands of sensual pleasure—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Avijjāmūlaṃ bhindesi, kammayantavighātako;

Sabbakammakkhayaṃ patto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.796.

He has shattered the root of ignorance, the destroyer of the machine of kamma; he who has attained the destruction of all kamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Natthikavādaṃ madditvā, atthikavādaṃ tiṭṭhayi;

Sammādiṭṭhiṃ viññāpesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.797.

Having crushed the doctrine of nihilism, he established the doctrine of affirmation; he made known right view—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ajjhattanhātako nātho, tibhavoghapāraṅgato;

Subhago sabbathā suddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.798.

The protector bathed internally, who has gone to the far shore of the three floods of existence; the fortunate one, pure in every way—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Nāgavaro sīhavaro, satthavāho satthuvaro;

Dhīsāgaro dhīradharo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.799.

The best of great beings, the best of lions, the caravan leader, the best of teachers; the ocean of wisdom, the upholder of wisdom—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Tāpanudo dukkhanudo, sabbahitasukhadado;

Vagguvado piyavado, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.800.

The dispeller of torment, the dispeller of suffering, the giver of all welfare and happiness; the one of charming speech, the one of endearing speech—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Caraṇayutto tevijjo, mūlaghaccasamūhato;

Pacchimajātisampatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.801.

Endowed with conduct, possessing the threefold knowledge, he by whom the root is utterly uprooted; he who has attained the final birth—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Tibhavaddhaja’cchariyo, tilokaketuabbhuto;

Saddhammapatākāseṭṭho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.802.

The wonderful banner of the three worlds, the amazing crest of the three worlds; the best banner of the good Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Yatindriyo santindriyo, pasannapasādindriyo;

Subhāvitindriyo santo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.803.

With controlled faculties, with peaceful faculties, with clear and serene faculties; with well-developed faculties, the peaceful one—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Nabhūpamamedhāyutto, dhitiyutto selasamo;

Khantiyutto dharāsamo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.804.

Endowed with wisdom like the sky, endowed with fortitude like a mountain; endowed with patience like the earth—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Appameyyasirimanto, santilaṅkāra’laṅkato;

Sukhābharaṇabhūsito, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.805.

Possessing immeasurable glory, adorned with the ornament of peace; decorated with the adornment of happiness—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Tiṃsapāramī sañcayi, suttiṇṇo tibhavaṇṇavā;

Antimajātiyutto yo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.806.

He who accumulated the thirty perfections, who has well crossed the ocean of threefold existence; he who is endowed with a final birth—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhavāsavapariññāto, pāputtāpavūpasamo;

Paramasukhadhigato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.807.

He by whom the taint of existence is fully understood, who has calmed the torment of evil; he who has attained the ultimate happiness—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhaddappatto bhaddakkhāyī, bhaddamaggappakāsako;

Bhaddakkho bhaddadassāvī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.808.

He who has attained the auspicious, the proclaimer of the auspicious, the illuminator of the auspicious path; the auspicious-eyed, the seer of the auspicious—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahāmahiddhiko dhīmā, bhavābhavaṃ byantikato;

Yo amatapphalaṃ patto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.809.

The one of great spiritual power, the wise one, who has made an end to existence and non-existence; he who has attained the fruit of the Deathless—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Nibbānabhāgī bhagavā, santippatto subhāgyavā;

Paramaṃ sivaṃ sampatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.810.

The Blessed One, a partaker in Nibbāna, he who has attained peace, the fortunate one; he who has attained the supreme security—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ācāraguṇasampanno, sabbaguṇānamākaro;

Puṇṇindu viya sampuṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.811.

Endowed with good conduct and virtue, a mine of all good qualities; complete like the full moon—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Nandīrāgaparikkhīṇo, upādānappamocano;

Narottamo yogakkhemī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.812.

He in whom delight and lust are exhausted, the one who gives release from grasping; the supreme among men, secure from the yokes—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Kiccakārī katakicco, kiccākiccappakāsako;

Kiccādhikaraṇadakkho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.813.

The doer of what is to be done, one who has done what was to be done, the proclaimer of what should and should not be done; the one skilled in the matter of duty—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Supassaddho munivaro, paguṇo munipuṅgavo;

Isipuṅgavo uttamo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.814.

The utterly serene, the best of sages, the accomplished one, the bull among sages; the bull among seers, the supreme one—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pahīnabhayabheravo, parisāsu visārado;

Narasīhanādaṃ nadi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.815.

He who has abandoned fear and dread, confident in the assemblies; he who roars the lion's roar of a man—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pāragāmī pāragato, pavaro pariyantagū;

Lokavidū lokantagū, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.816.

Gone to the far shore, one who has crossed over, the supreme, one who has gone to the end; the knower of the world, one who has gone to the world's end—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paramatthaparipuṇṇo, paramasaccadassano;

Nibbānaṃ yo sacchikato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.817.

Perfect in the ultimate meaning, the seer of the supreme truth; he who has realized Nibbāna for himself—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Lābhālābhe yasāyase, sammānaavamānane;

Sabbattha samako sāmī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.818.

In gain and loss, fame and disgrace, honor and dishonor; the Master, equal everywhere—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paramatthappatto dhīro, paramanimmalottamo;

Bhavabandhaṃ pamocesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.819.

The wise one who has attained the ultimate goal, the supreme, the utterly stainless one; he has released the bond of existence—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paṭirūpo paṭibuddho, saṃsārapaṭimocako;

Paṭipassaddhisaṃyutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.820.

He who has fully understood what should be fully understood, has developed what should be developed, has abandoned what should be abandoned—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pañcakkhandhapariññāto, paññāya paṭibhāvito;

Sabbamohaṃ nimmaddesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.821.

He who has attained peace and bliss, released from the stream of craving; he who freed many beings—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Padhāno purisavaro, pāragavesī pāragū;

Pavivekamanuppatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.822.

He who has reached the far shore of full understanding, the far shore of abandonment, the far shore of development—the master, the sovereign one—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbaklesakkhayaṃ patto, sabbānusayaddhaṃsito;

Sabbāsave pahāsi yo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.823.

Free from the fault of clinging, detached, without substratum; unfettered, completely liberated—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbāvijjā sañchindesi, tikkhavijjāvudhandharo;

Sabbāsayaṃ vināsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.824.

Having attained Nibbāna, his purpose accomplished, resolute, abandoning proliferation; awakened, the ultimate refuge—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbasokamatikkanto, sabbadukkhavināsako;

Sabbupāyāsaucchinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.825.

Established in the path and its fruit, he who has well torn the net of delusion; well-at-ease, supremely liberated—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Muttimaggaṃ pakāsesi, muttimaggagavesako;

Sabbabhavabyādhimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.826.

He has proclaimed the path to liberation, the seeker of the path to liberation; he is freed from the malady of all existence—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Yo na limpati kāmesu, āraggeriva sāsapo;

Bhavāsattivisaṃyutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.827.

He who is not smeared by sensual pleasures, like a mustard seed on an awl's point; he who is disjoined from attachment to existence—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Muni moneyya sampanno, bāhitapāpo brāhmaṇo;

Samaṇo samatāvino, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.828.

The sage endowed with sagacity, the brahmin who has banished evil; the ascetic of tranquil conduct—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhavāsavā vidhūpito, kāmāsavā vūpasamo;

Avijjāsavā vigato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.829.

He by whom the taint of existence is dispelled, by whom the taint of sensual desire is calmed; he from whom the taint of ignorance is gone—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pariññeyyaṃ pariññāto, bhāvetabbaṃ ca bhāvito;

Pahātabbaṃ pahīnaṃ yo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.830.

He who has fully understood what should be fully understood, has developed what should be developed, has abandoned what should be abandoned—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Santisukhamanuppatto, muttisotapavāhako;

Bahū satte pamocesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.831.

Having attained peace and bliss, released from the flowing stream, he freed many beings—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pariññāpāragū cāpi, pahānapāragū pabhū;

Bhāvanāpāragū vibhū, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.832.

He has reached the far shore of full understanding, the far shore of abandonment, the far shore of development—the Master, the Sovereign—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Āsattidosarahito, vippamutto nirupadhi;

Onaddhamutto omutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.833.

Free from the fault of clinging, detached, without acquisitions; unfettered, completely liberated—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Nibbānapatto siddhattho, pagabbho papañcajaho;

Pabuddho paṭisaraṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.834.

He has attained Nibbāna, his purpose is accomplished, he is resolute, has abandoned proliferation; the Awakened One, the ultimate refuge—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Maggaṭṭho api phalaṭṭho, mohajālasuphālako;

Suphāsuko sumuttimā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.835.

Established in the path and its fruit, he has skillfully torn the net of delusion; he is at ease, supremely liberated—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bodhiratanasampanno, pavarabhūrimedhaso;

Brāhmaṇo samaṇo sādhu, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.836.

Endowed with the jewel of enlightenment, possessed of vast and supreme wisdom, a true Brahmin and ascetic, a virtuous one—to that Buddha I bow down.

Micchādiṭṭhiṃ padālesi, micchācāranimmaddako;

Micchāñāṇaṃ nivāresi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.837.

He shattered wrong view, crushed wrong conduct, and averted wrong knowledge—to that Buddha I bow down.

Mohajālamatikkanto, duṭṭhantakassa antako;

Māramadaṃ nimmaddesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.838.

He crossed over the net of delusion, the ender of the Evil One; He crushed Māra's pride—to that Buddha I bow down.

Siddhattho sadatthapatto, paripuṇṇamanoratho;

Katakicco vūpasanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.839.

His purpose accomplished, his own welfare attained, his aspirations fulfilled; his task done, fully at peace—to that Buddha I bow down.

Yassa māyā ca māno ca, moho makkho ca pātito;

Vāri pokkharapattā’va, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.840.

He in whom deceit and pride, delusion and contempt have been cast off, like water from a lotus leaf—to that Buddha I bow down.

Uḷurājā’va vimalo, suddhacitto anāvilo;

Asatto sugato nātho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.841.

Like the king of the stars, he is stainless, with a pure and unclouded mind; unattached, the Well-Gone One, the protector—to that Buddha I bow down.

Kāmabhavapparikkhīṇo, rūpabhavamupaccago;

Arūpabhavaṃ bhañjesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.842.

He for whom sensual existence is exhausted, who has transcended existence in the realm of form, who has shattered existence in the formless realm—to that Buddha I bow down.

Yasī yasaābhūsito, mahesī isikuñjaro;

Hitesī hitasambhavo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.843.

Glorious, adorned with glory, the great seer, an elephant among seers; a benefactor, a source of benefit—to that Buddha I bow down.

Tapassī cāpi tejassī, yasassī ca yasodharo;

Vipassī cāpi vidassī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.844.

Austere and radiant, renowned and glory-bearing; discerning and insightful—to that Buddha I bow down.

Aggamaggagavesī ca, sukhesi sabbapāṇinaṃ;

Mahāmuni mahesī ca, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.845.

A seeker of the supreme path, one who seeks the happiness of all beings, the great sage and great seer—to that Buddha I bow down.

Atthavidū saccavidū, lokavidū lokantagū;

Addhagū addhānagato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.846.

Knower of the meaning, knower of the truth, knower of the world, one who has gone to the world's end; one who has traversed the path, who has completed the journey—to that Buddha I bow down.

Sokasallasatticuṇṇo, sabbupādānajhāyako;

Mohaṇṇavatiṇṇo vīro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.847.

He who has crushed the darts and spears of sorrow, who has burned up all clinging; the hero who has crossed the ocean of delusion—to that Buddha I bow down.

Bhayabheravamatikkanto, abhīto vītasārado;

Sabbathā chambhavimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.848.

He has gone beyond fear and terror, is fearless and without dread; in every way released from trembling—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nippapañcarato nātho, nippapañcaṃ niddesayi;

Sabbappapañcaṃ hāpesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.849.

The protector who delights in non-proliferation, he taught non-proliferation; he abandoned all proliferation—to that Buddha I bow down.

Ovādako viññāpako, tārako bahupāṇinaṃ;

Desanākusalo satthā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.850.

An adviser, an instructor, a rescuer of many beings; the Teacher skilled in exposition—to that Buddha I bow down.

Suddhahadayo saṃsuddho, vimalo virajamānaso;

Nhātako nimmalacitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.851.

Pure-hearted, thoroughly purified, stainless, with a mind free of dust; washed clean, with a spotless mind—to that Buddha I bow down.

Vikkhīṇajātisaṃsāro, antimadehadhārako;

Saṃsāre asaṃsaranto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.852.

He for whom the round of birth is destroyed, the bearer of the final body; no longer wandering in saṃsāra—to that Buddha I bow down.

Dīpaṅkarapādamūle, muttiṃ hatthagataṃ caji;

Sammāsambodhimākaṅkhī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.853.

At the feet of Dīpaṅkara, he renounced the liberation within his grasp, aspiring for perfect self-enlightenment—to that Buddha I bow down.

Piyavācī piyabhāṇī, piyavādī vacīvaro;

Vagguvācī vaggubhāṇī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.854.

Speaking pleasantly, uttering what is dear, speaking what is pleasing, excellent in speech; speaking gently, uttering what is lovely—to that Buddha I bow down.

Thiravāco khemavāco, pītivāco santivado;

Acchabhāṇī tacchabhāṇī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.855.

Steady in speech, secure in speech, joyful in speech, speaking of peace; speaking clearly, speaking the truth—to that Buddha I bow down.

Ñāṇabhāsī ñāṇavidū, kālabhāsī kālavidū;

Saccabhāsī saccavidū, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.856.

Speaking with knowledge, a knower of knowledge; speaking at the right time, a knower of the right time; speaking the truth, a knower of the truth—to that Buddha I bow down.

Karavīkabhāṇī ñāṇī, gambhīradhammadesako;

Citrakathiko vicitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.857.

Speaking with the voice of the karavīka bird, wise, a teacher of the profound Dhamma; a brilliant and eloquent speaker—to that Buddha I bow down.

Sammāsambodhisampanno, saddhammabalasobhito;

Sampannavijjācaraṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.858.

Endowed with perfect self-enlightenment, resplendent with the power of the true Dhamma, perfect in knowledge and conduct—to that Buddha I bow down.

Yassāsavā na vijjanti, vimuttacitto sabbadā;

Paramaṃ padaṃ sampatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.859.

He in whom no taints are found, whose mind is ever liberated; who has attained the supreme state—to that Buddha I bow down.

Samathappatto samaṇo, brahmavihārī brāhmaṇo;

Sabbamalaṃ pabbājesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.860.

A recluse who has attained calm, a true brahmin dwelling in the divine abodes; he has cast out all defilements—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nandībhavaparikkhīṇo, bhavoghamohamaccago;

Sabbottamamanuppatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.861.

With delight in existence exhausted, gone beyond the flood of becoming, delusion, and death; having attained the highest of all states—to that Buddha I bow down.

Pañcanīvaraṇātīto, sattavisuddhidhāraṇo;

Sattabojjhaṅgakusalo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.862.

He has gone beyond the five hindrances, possesses the seven purifications, and is skilled in the seven factors of enlightenment—to that Buddha I bow down.

Sabbapāramī pūretvā, suddhadhammagavesayī;

Mokkhamaggaṃ pakāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.863.

Having fulfilled all perfections, he sought the pure Dhamma; he revealed the path to liberation—to that Buddha I bow down.

Lokābhibhū lokajito, lokaññū lokanāyako;

Tilokanātho lokīso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.864.

Conqueror of the world, victor over the world, knower of the world, leader of the world; lord of the three worlds, master of the world—to that Buddha I bow down.

Suvatthināmo sunāmo, saccanāmo sukhāvaho;

Saccasandho saccavādo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.865.

Of auspicious name, of good name, of truthful name, a bringer of happiness; true to his promise, a speaker of truth—to that Buddha I bow down.

Chandarāgavippahīno, amamo ca anāsayo;

Asatto sugato suddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.866.

Having abandoned sensual desire, without possessiveness or dependence; unattached, well-gone, pure—to that Buddha I bow down.

Puṇṇavositavosāno, abhiññābalavosito;

Jātimaccukkhayaṃ patto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.867.

He who has fully completed his task, perfected in higher knowledge and power; having attained the destruction of birth and death—to that Buddha I bow down.

Māyāmohasamucchinno, dosadohavidālako;

Lobhalālasā dhaṃsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.868.

He who has cut off deceit and delusion, who has torn apart hatred and malice, who has destroyed greed and longing—to that Buddha I bow down.

Dosasallasamucchinno, appaṭibaddhamānaso;

Daḷhacitto asantāpī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.869.

The dart of hate is plucked out, his mind is unattached; firm of mind, untroubled—to that Buddha I bow down.

Paphullamānaso satthā, sadā pasannamānaso;

Muditamānaso sāmī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.870.

With heart elated, the Teacher, ever with mind serene; with joyous heart, the Lord—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nirajamānaso nātho, sadā vimalamānaso;

Visuddhamānaso suddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.871.

The Lord, whose mind is without defilement, ever with stainless mind; with mind purified, the Pure One—to that Buddha I bow down.

Tejassī ca tejadhano, tapassī ca tapodhano;

Ñāṇesī ca ñāṇadhano, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.872.

Radiant and rich in radiance, austere and rich in austerity, seeking wisdom and rich in wisdom—to that Buddha I bow down.

Pamodito pamodesi, tosesi paritosako;

Parirakkhako rakkhesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.873.

One who delights and causes delight, one who pleases and supremely satisfies; a protector who protects—to that Buddha I bow down.

Puṇṇavositavosāno, abhiññābalavosito;

Jātimaccukkhayaṃ patto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.874.

He who has fully completed his task, perfected in higher knowledge and power; having reached the destruction of birth and death—to that Buddha I bow down.

Māyāmohasamucchinno, dosadohavidālako;

Lobhalālasā dhaṃsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.875.

He who has cut off deceit and delusion, who has torn apart hatred and malice, who has crushed greed and longing—to that Buddha I bow down.

Dosasallasamucchinno, appaṭibaddhamānaso;

Daḷhacitto asantāpī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.876.

The dart of hate is plucked out, his mind is unbound; with steadfast heart, untroubled—to that Buddha I bow down.

Sahetudhammaṃ ñāpesi, ātāpī jhāyī brāhmaṇo;

Sabbā kaṅkhā vinodesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.877.

The ardent, meditative brahmin revealed the Dhamma with its cause; he dispelled all doubt—to that Buddha I bow down.

Sabbā kaṅkhāyo vāpesi, ātāpī jhāyī brāhmaṇo;

Paccayānaṃ khayaṃ vidvā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.878.

All doubts he has dispelled, the ardent, meditative brahmin; having known the exhaustion of conditions—to that Buddha I bow down.

Mārasenā parājitvā, suriyo’va obhāsasi;

Saccadhammamanuppatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.879.

Having defeated Māra's army, he shines like the sun; having attained the True Dhamma—to that Buddha I bow down.

Tiṃsapāramīpūresi, sabbalokahitaṅkaro;

Catusaccaṃ anvesayi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.880.

He fulfilled the thirty perfections, the benefactor of all the world; he sought the Four Noble Truths—to that Buddha I bow down.

Catusaccaṃ sacchikatvā, taṇhānaṃ khayamajjhagā;

Visaṅkhāragatacitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.881.

Having realized the Four Noble Truths, he attained the destruction of craving; with mind gone to the unconditioned—to that Buddha I bow down.

Bhaddakāyo bhaddavāco, bhaddacitto bhaddāsayo;

Samantabhaddo subhaddo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.882.

Blessed in body, blessed in speech, blessed in mind, blessed in resolve; wholly blessed, supremely blessed—to that Buddha I bow down.

Santakāyo santavāco, santavā santacetaso;

Santisamuddaṃ sampatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.883.

Peaceful in body, peaceful in speech, the peaceful one of tranquil mind; having reached the ocean of peace—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Lokabandhu lokasāmī, lokādhipo lokissaro;

Lokamitto lokasakhā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.884.

Kinsman of the world, lord of the world, ruler of the world, sovereign of the world; friend of the world, companion of the world—to that Buddha I bow down.

Akkharānaṃ sannipāte, pubbāparānaṃ pāragū;

Niruttipadakovido, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.885.

In the combination of syllables, a master of what comes before and after; skilled in language and expression—to that Buddha I bow down.

Santisukhapadāyako, vijjānidhi vināyako;

Bāhūjane sahāyako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.886.

Giver of the state of peace and happiness, a treasure of knowledge, the great guide; a helper to many people—to that Buddha I bow down.

Vijayanto bodhimūḷe, patto sambodhimuttamaṃ;

Antakassa antakaro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.887.

Victorious at the foot of the Bodhi tree, he attained supreme enlightenment; the maker of an end to the Ender—to that Buddha I bow down.

Vinayavādī viratto, dhammavādī dhammagato;

Atthavādī atthappatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.888.

A speaker of discipline, detached; a speaker of Dhamma, abiding in Dhamma; a speaker of the goal, having attained the goal—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Santivādī muduvādī, saccavādī saccarato;

Bhūtavādī bhaddavādī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.889.

A speaker of peace, a speaker of gentleness; a speaker of truth, delighting in truth; a speaker of the actual, a speaker of the auspicious—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Yatakāyo yatavāco, yatacitto yatindriyo;

Sabbavidhe yato yati, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.890.

Restrained in body, restrained in speech, restrained in mind, restrained in senses; restrained in every way, the ascetic—to that Buddha I bow down.

Niriccho ceva chinniccho, vīticcho icchāucchinno;

Gaticcho icchānicchāto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.891.

Without craving, with craving cut off; gone beyond craving, with craving uprooted; his craving is gone, he is past craving and non-craving—to that Buddha I bow down.

Acchacitto acchacārī, anacchaicchāriñcako;

Sabbicchā’nicchā ucchinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.892.

With mind transparent, of transparent conduct, the abandoner of impure desires; with all desire and aversion cut off—to that Buddha I bow down.

Accito accisajjito, accucco ca accuttamo;

Accutaṃ akataṃ patto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.893.

Honored and supremely revered, lofty and most excellent; he has attained the permanent, the uncreated—to that Buddha I bow down.

Suddho maggaṃ visodhesi, siddho samaṇasekharo;

Buddho bodhiṃ pakāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.894.

The Pure One has purified the path, the Accomplished One, crest of ascetics; the Buddha has revealed enlightenment—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paṇīto ca punīto ca, pavitto cāpi pāvano;

Pesalo pavaro mejjho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.895.

Refined and purified, spotless and purifying; amiable, excellent, and worthy—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Khemo khīṇapariḷāho, khemayutto khemakaro;

Khemakkhetto khemaṭṭhāno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.896.

Secure, with feverish torment extinguished, endowed with security, the maker of security; the field of security, the abode of security—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Gatabhogo gatarogo, gatasoko gatāvilo;

Gatānutāpo gatindho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.897.

Gone beyond possessions, gone beyond disease, gone beyond sorrow, gone beyond stain; gone beyond remorse, gone beyond fuel—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pāragū sabbadukkhānaṃ, pariññāṇañca pāragū;

Nirodhaṃ susacchikato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.898.

He has gone to the far shore of all suffering, and gone to the far shore of full understanding; he has directly realized cessation—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sattavisuddhisampanno, suddho visuddhamānaso;

Vimuttamānaso vidvā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.899.

Endowed with the seven purifications, pure, with a purified mind; with a liberated mind, the wise one—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhavābhave anubhavitvā, patto sumuttimuttamaṃ;

Brahmacakkaṃ pavattesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.900.

Having experienced one state of becoming after another, he attained the supreme, excellent liberation; he set rolling the Wheel of Brahma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhūripañño bhūrimedho, bhūribodhipakāsako;

Bhūriñāṇaṃ pasāresi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.901.

Of vast wisdom, of vast intelligence, the illuminator of vast enlightenment; he spread forth vast knowledge—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhāranikkhepako vīro, bhavasinehanāsako;

Bhavanettiṃ vibhañjesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.902.

The hero who has laid down the burden, the destroyer of affection for becoming; he has shattered the conduit of becoming—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhāvitatto bhavamutto, bhāvito bhāvanārato;

Bhāvitindriyo bhagavā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.903.

With self developed, freed from becoming; developed and delighting in development; with faculties developed, the Blessed One—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mahābhisakko bhesajjo, bhavarogatikicchako;

Bhavoghatārako bhaddo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.904.

The great physician, the medicine, the healer of the disease of becoming; the auspicious one who helps cross the flood of becoming—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhavābhavataṇhābhaggo, bhavāsattivibhañjako;

Bhavasaṃyojanaṃ chindesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.905.

He who has broken the craving for becoming and non-becoming, the shatterer of attachment to becoming; he has cut the fetter of becoming—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhavantadassī subhaddo, bhavaggātīto nibbuto;

Bhavantagū bhagavanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.906.

The seer of the end of becoming, the very auspicious one; gone beyond the peak of becoming, extinguished; gone to the end of becoming, the Blessed One—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhāvito ariyamaggo, bhavabandhanasamūhato;

Bhayabheravaṃ bhedesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.907.

He by whom the noble path was cultivated, who has uprooted the bonds of becoming; he has shattered fear and dread—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhāvesi kusalaṃ dhammaṃ, bojjhaṅgaratanissaro;

Bhavasotaṃ tiṇṇo nātho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.908.

He cultivated wholesome qualities, the lord of the jewel-like factors of enlightenment; the protector who has crossed the stream of becoming—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhavasaṃyojanaṃ chetvā, bhavasaṅgātigo isi;

Bhavasāgaramuttiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.909.

Having cut the fetter of becoming, the sage has gone beyond attachment to becoming; having crossed the ocean of becoming—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhavayogavītivatto, bhavasantāpanibbuto;

Bhavamohodadhitiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.910.

He who has gone beyond the yoke of becoming, for whom the fever of becoming is quenched; he who has crossed the ocean of the delusion of becoming—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhavabhogavisaṃyutto, bhāramutto bhavajayo;

Bhavayogavippamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.911.

Disjoined from the enjoyments of becoming, released from the burden, the conqueror of becoming; completely freed from the yoke of becoming—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhavabhogoghanittiṇṇo, bhavajālasandālako;

Bhavasaṃsaraṇātīto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.912.

Having crossed the dense flood of the enjoyments of becoming, the tearer apart of the net of becoming; gone beyond the wandering on in becoming—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhīrutāṇo bhīruleṇo, bhīrusaraṇadāyako;

Bhīruārakkhako bhiyyo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.913.

Protection for the fearful, a refuge for the fearful, the giver of shelter to the fearful; and further, a guardian for the fearful—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbāsaraṇasaraṇo, tāṇo leṇo surakkhako;

Janānaṃ nandako bhiyyo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.914.

The refuge for all the refugeless, a protection, a shelter, a good guardian; and further, a bringer of joy to people—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbābādhaṃ accagamo, taṇhātimirantakaro;

Kāmāsavaṃ pajahi yo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.915.

He has overcome all afflictions, the maker of an end to the darkness of craving; he who abandoned the influx of sensual desire—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbadhammābhisambuddho, mokkhamaggaganvesako;

Paramatthadhammappatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.916.

Fully self-awakened to all dhammas, a seeker of the path to liberation; he who has attained the Dhamma of the ultimate meaning—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbadhammaṃ sambodhesi, antakantakaro jino;

Pariyantadhammappatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.917.

He awakened to all dhammas, the Conqueror who makes an end to the End-Maker; he who has attained the Dhamma at its conclusion—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbalokaṃ pariññāsi, sabbalokapanūdano;

Lokuttaradhammappatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.918.

He fully comprehended the whole world, the dispeller of the whole world; he who has attained the supramundane Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbalokahitatthāya, bodhesi karuṇāpatī;

Nipuṇatthadhammappatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.919.

For the welfare and benefit of the whole world, the lord of compassion awakened; he who has attained the Dhamma of subtle meaning—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbaiddhī abhiññāsi, sabbataṇhā nirodhako;

Nirodhaṃ sakkhiṃ akāsi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.920.

He directly knew all supernormal powers, the bringer of cessation to all craving; he directly witnessed cessation—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbasaccaṃ abhiññāsi, sabbaññū samaṇuttamo;

Paṇḍito sabbadhammesu, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.921.

He directly knew all truths, the all-knower, the supreme ascetic; wise in all dhammas—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbasiddhatthasiddho ca, samantabhaddo sabbathā;

Sabbuttamaṃ dhammaṃ laddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.922.

He for whom all aims are accomplished, auspicious in every way, on all sides; he who has obtained the supreme Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Upeto bodhidhammehi, vimutto sabbabhavehi ca;

Iṭṭhapatto muttā’niṭṭho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.923.

Endowed with the qualities of enlightenment, and freed from all states of becoming; he who has attained the desirable and is released from the undesirable—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbāhārapariññāto, sabbāhāramanissito;

Sabbādhārapariccāgī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.924.

He who has fully understood all nutriment, who is unreliant on any nutriment; the relinquisher of all supports—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbamohanisā hantvā, sabbaṃ rāgaṃ dosaṃ nudo;

Suddhidhammaṃ pakāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.925.

Having destroyed the night of all delusion, the dispeller of all lust and hatred; he revealed the Dhamma of purity—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbakilesaṃ sosesi, sabbādānapanūdano;

Sabbasokaṃ vināsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.926.

He dried up all defilements, the dispeller of all grasping; he destroyed all sorrow—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbindriyagutto sāmī, sabbakaṅkhicchāsaṃvuto;

Sabbalokesanācāgī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.927.

The lord with all faculties guarded, restrained as to all doubt and desire; the relinquisher of all seeking in the world—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbāsavapariccāgī, jātimaccu nivārayī;

Bhavadukkhaṃ viddhaṃsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.928.

The relinquisher of all influxes, he warded off birth and death; he utterly destroyed the suffering of becoming—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbakammaklesajaho, vītasaṅkhāracetaso;

Antimasārīrappatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.929.

The abandoner of all kamma and defilements, he whose mind is without formations; he who has attained the final body—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbabandhaṃ vighātesi, sabbantarāyanāsako;

Bodhaññū dubbuddhimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.930.

He has shattered all bonds, the destroyer of all obstacles; the knower of enlightenment, freed from wrong understanding—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbamohaparikkhīṇo, sabbaññū sabbakovido;

Sabbābhibhū sabbavidū, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.931.

He in whom all delusion is utterly destroyed, the all-knower, skilled in all things; the overcomer of all, the knower of all—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbābhinivesā suñño, sabbaguṇapatiṭṭhito;

Sabbaklese visodhesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.932.

Void of all adherence, established in all virtues; he purified all defilements—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbarāgaṃ virājesi, sabbadosaviddhaṃsako;

Sabbamohavinimutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.933.

He caused all lust to fade away, the destroyer of all hatred; completely freed from all delusion—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbadukkhapariññāto, sabbadukkhakkhayaṅkaro;

Sabbadhi bhavabhañjano, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.934.

He by whom all suffering is fully understood, the maker of the destruction of all suffering; the breaker of becoming in every way—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbadukkhamatikkanto, sabbadukkhassa antagū;

Sabbadukkhappahīno ca, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.935.

He who has overcome all suffering, who has gone to the end of all suffering; and he by whom all suffering is abandoned—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Saṃsārasāgaruttiṇṇo, sabbabhavāna pāragū;

Antimadehaṃ dhāresi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.936.

He who has crossed over the ocean of saṃsāra, who has gone to the far shore of all becomings; he bears his final body—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbathā ākaṅkhātīto, sabbasaṅgātigo sudhī;

Sabbesu anūpalitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.937.

Gone beyond longing in every way, the wise one who has gone beyond all attachments; unsullied by all things—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Giddhiñjaho taṇhakkhayo, sabbacāgesu saṇṭhito;

Sabbattha upasammato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.938.

The abandoner of greed, he in whom craving is destroyed, established in all relinquishments; he who is calmed in every way—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbāmitte vasīkatvā, sabbajino sabbābhibhū;

Sabbaveravippamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.939.

Having mastered all enemies, the all-conqueror, the all-overcomer; completely freed from all enmity—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbabhogamatikkanto, sabbakāmaraticajo;

Sabbakaṅkhicchā ucchinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.940.

He who has overcome all possessions, the renouncer of all sensual pleasures; he in whom all longing and desire is cut off—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbītiyo vītivatto, sabbabhītivināsako;

Sabbadosadohadanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.941.

He who has overcome all calamities, the destroyer of all fears; he who has tamed all corruption and malice—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbakopakodhakhīṇo, kāmaklesamatikkamo;

Sabbamohamāyāmutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.942.

He in whom all anger and rage are destroyed, who has overcome the defilement of sensual desire; freed from all delusion and illusion—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbasaṃyojane chetvā, sabbasaṃsaya’pagato;

Sabbupādānupacchinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.943.

Having cut all fetters, he from whom all doubt has departed; he in whom all clinging is cut off—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbābhilāsā hāpesi, sabbaklesavisodhako;

Sabbiñjā paṭippassaddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.944.

He who has abandoned all desires, the purifier of all defilements; he in whom all agitation is allayed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbādānapariccāgī, sabbasaṃyojanātigo;

Sabbasaṅgavisaṃyutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.945.

The relinquisher of all grasping, he who has gone beyond all fetters; disjoined from all attachments—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbattha sumano sāmī, sabbasotthiṃ padāyako;

Sabbesaṃ sampasīdesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.946.

The lord with a good mind everywhere, the giver of all well-being; he is gracious to all—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbānalaṃ nibbāpesi, sabbasantāpamaddako;

Sabbadhi sumutto santo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.947.

He extinguished all fires, the crusher of all torment; wholly freed and peaceful in every way—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbāsavaṃ parijāni, sabbabyādhivināsako;

Sabbasokakkhayaṃ patto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.948.

He fully understood all influxes, the destroyer of all sickness; having attained the destruction of all sorrow—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbarāgatamaṃ dhaṃsī, sabbadosatamaṃ nudo;

Sabbamohatamaṃ hantā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.949.

He shattered the darkness of all lust, the dispeller of the darkness of all hatred, the destroyer of the darkness of all delusion—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbadhi sabbatthappatto, sabbatthadassāvī isi;

Sabbapāsaṇḍaṃ maddesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.950.

The sage who has attained all things everywhere and sees all things everywhere; he crushed all heresies—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbapāramīsambhūto, sabbapariññāpūrito;

Sabbaabhiññāsampuṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.951.

Arisen from all perfections, filled with all full understanding, complete in all direct knowledges—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbadukkhakkhayaṃ patto, sabbasokamatikkamo;

Saṃsārasindhunittiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.952.

He who has attained the destruction of all suffering, who has overcome all sorrow; he who has crossed over the ocean of saṃsāra—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sīlasamādhisaṃyutto, vijjāvāridhi paññavā;

Sabbāsavāchinnabhinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.953.

Endowed with virtue and concentration, an ocean of knowledge, the wise one; he by whom all influxes are cut and shattered—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbatāpavippamutto, samucchinnasabbūpadhi;

Sabbapapañcūpasamo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.954.

Completely freed from all torment, he by whom all acquisitions are cut off; the stilling of all conceptual proliferation—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbaahaṅkāramutto, sabbamamaṅkārakkhayo;

Sabbāsatti vippamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.955.

Freed from all I-making, he in whom all my-making is destroyed; completely freed from all attachment—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Vijitasabbasaṅgāmo, sabbattha aparājito;

Sabbe vattesi sabbaso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.956.

Victorious in all battles, unconquered everywhere; He wielded power over all in every way—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbaññū tilokaseṭṭho, sabbasaṃyojanā nudo;

Sabbaoghe nittharesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.957.

All-knowing, supreme in the triple world, the dispeller of all fetters; He has brought beings across all floods—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbabandhavinimutto, sabbagaṇṭhivikhaṇḍito;

Sabbapāsehi mocesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.958.

Freed from all bonds, He shattered all knots; He released beings from all snares—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbaganthasamucchinno, sabbayogavisaṃyuto;

Hatakkhobho hatālayo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.959.

He has cut off all ties, is disjoined from all yokes; with perturbation and attachment destroyed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbasaṃyojanā suñño, sabbavaṭṭavināsako;

Sabbajaṭā vijaṭesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.960.

Empty of all fetters, the destroyer of the entire round of rebirth; He has unraveled all tangles—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbesu anicchāsaññī, sabbābhijjhātigo isi;

Sabbasaṃyoga’saṃyogo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.961.

Perceiving impermanence in all, the sage who has overcome all covetousness; unbound by all bonds—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbasaṅkhārehi ritto, sabbāsatti panūdano;

Sabbākusalapamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.962.

Devoid of all formations, the dispeller of all attachment; freed from all that is unwholesome—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbapakārasampanno, sabbasamantabhaddako;

Sabbākāraparipuṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.963.

Perfect in every way, auspicious in every respect; complete in every aspect—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabba vijjā anuppatto, sabbasuguṇasaṅgaho;

Bhaddako subhaddakārī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.964.

Having attained all true knowledge, a repository of all good qualities; auspicious and a doer of what is auspicious—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbāsave pariññāto, sabbāsave byantikato;

Sabbesaṃ paricajjesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.965.

Having fully understood all taints, having brought all taints to an end; He has relinquished them all—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbabandhavippamutto, sabbasaṅkappapūrito;

Sabbakkhemaṃ vikāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.966.

Completely freed from all bonds, fulfilled in all his resolves; He has made manifest the ultimate security—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbattha kusalo satthā, sabbattha kovido vidū;

Sabbattha vimalo suddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.967.

The Teacher skilled in all things, the wise one expert in all; stainless and pure in every way—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbocca sabbatobhaddo, sabbathā maññitaṃ cajo;

Sammādassanasampanno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.968.

Supreme over all, auspicious in every way, He has abandoned all conceit; endowed with right vision—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Anavasesañāṇaññū, sabbato suvijānako;

Sabbaaññāṇamucchinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.969.

Knower of knowledge without remainder, who understands well in every way; He has cut off all ignorance—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbaññāsampatto nātho, āsattiritto sabbadhi;

Sabbattha sabbavisiṭṭho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.970.

The Protector who has attained omniscience, devoid of attachment in every way; the most distinguished of all in every respect—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbakilesehi suñño, sabbaganthippamocako;

Sabbabandhaṃ vicchindesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.971.

Empty of all defilements, the liberator from all ties; He has severed every bond—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbabhavaupacchinno, ucchinnalokabandhano;

Sabbaoghaṃ nittharaṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.972.

He has cut off all existence, has severed the bonds of the world; He has crossed over all floods—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Nittiṇṇabhavasāgaro, katakicco yatissaro;

Sabbaso sītalībhūto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.973.

He has crossed the ocean of becoming, his task is done, the lord of ascetics; He has become utterly cool in every way—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbalokamabhibhūto, sabbalokavidū isi;

Sabbalokaṃ sudassāvī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.974.

The conqueror of all the world, the sage who knows all the world; the one who sees all the world clearly—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbalokavisaṃyutto, sabbalokavirajjako;

Sabbalokaṃ nirodhesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.975.

Disjoined from all the world, dispassionate toward all the world; He has brought all the world to cessation—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbaloke anāsaṃso, sabbaloke anūpayo;

Sabbaloke saṅgasuñño, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.976.

Without longing in all the world, without clinging in all the world; empty of attachment in all the world—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbaabhiññā sampatto, sabbadhi sumativaro;

Sabbasaccaṃ vitthāresi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.977.

Having attained all the direct knowledges, the best of the wise in every way; He has expounded all truth in detail—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbapāramīsampanno, sabbaguṇānupāgato;

Sabbathā pariññāpatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.978.

Endowed with all perfections, possessed of all virtues; having attained full understanding in every way—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhavasotaṃ visosesi, sabbaklesamupaccago;

Sabbāsavaṃ vikkhālesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.979.

He has dried up the stream of becoming, has passed beyond all defilements; He has washed away all taints—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbathā sampaṭinissajji, sabbaṃ santarabāhiraṃ;

Sabbaṃ sotthiṃ sacchikato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.980.

He has in every way completely relinquished all that is internal and external; He has realized the ultimate well-being—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbappapañcaṃ pahāsi yo, pariccāgesu saṇṭhito;

Sabbasaṅgaṃ vivajjesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.981.

He who has abandoned all conceptual proliferation, who is established in renunciation; He has forsaken all attachment—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabba’ntarāyavihato, sabba’ghānalanibbuto;

Sabbasārambhaṃ bhañjesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.982.
