With all obstacles destroyed, the fire of all evils extinguished; He has broken all contention—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbābhilāsā ucchinno, sabbābhimānabhindako;

Sabbā’nusayaṃ nissaṭṭho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.983.

He has cut off all longing, is the shatterer of all pride; He has discarded all underlying tendencies—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbasaṃsaṭṭhavicchinno, sabbasaṃyojanā cajo;

Sabbagedhapabhedako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.984.

Cut off from all association, He has abandoned all fetters; the shatterer of all greed—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbupādānūpasamo, sabbākaṅkhāvikhaṇḍito;

Sabbasallaṃ vicuṇṇesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.985.

The stiller of all clinging, the shatterer of all longing; He has pulverized all darts—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbamohaṃ vinodesi, sabbamānanimaddako;

Sabbabhītiṃ vītivatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.986.

He has dispelled all delusion, is the crusher of all conceit; He has transcended all fear—I revere that Buddha.

Kāmāsattiṃ byantikato, sabbachandasañchindako;

Sabbā icchā ucchedesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.987.

He has brought attachment to sensual pleasures to an end, has cut off all desire; He has eradicated all craving—I revere that Buddha.

Sabbasammohaṃ maddesi, sabbasotavisosako;

Sabbaganthiṃ niddhaṃsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.988.

He has crushed all delusion, has dried up all streams; He has destroyed all knots—I revere that Buddha.

Sabbissā samatikkanto, sabbato iñjā riñcako;

Sabbadosadohaṃ hantā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.989.

He has transcended all jealousy, has abandoned all agitation; the slayer of all hatred and malice—I revere that Buddha.

Sabbakodhamatikkanto, sabbakāmanimaddako;

Sabbamohasamucchinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.990.

He has overcome all anger, is the crusher of all sensual desire; He has cut off all delusion—I revere that Buddha.

Bhavanettiṃ sañchindesi, sabbabhogajigucchako;

Sabbabodhiguṇupeto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.991.

He has severed the guide to becoming, is disgusted with all enjoyments; endowed with all the qualities of enlightenment—I revere that Buddha.

Sabbatthadohātīto yo, sabbamānātigo muni;

Sabbadosaṃ vippamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.992.

He who has gone beyond all malice, the sage who has transcended all pride; completely freed from all hatred—I revere that Buddha.

Sabbasaṃsayā vimutto, sabbasaṃyojanācuto;

Sabbasaṃsāroghaṃ tiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.993.

Freed from all doubt, fallen away from all fetters; He has crossed the flood of all saṃsāra—I revere that Buddha.

Sabbamhi anūpalitto, sabbavidū sabbābhibhū;

Sabbantarāyavikkhīṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.994.

Unsoiled by anything, all-knowing, all-conquering; He has destroyed all obstacles—I revere that Buddha.

Sabbūpadhimatikkanto, sabbāsavakkhayaṅkaro;

Sīho’va anutrāso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.995.

He has transcended all substrata of existence, has brought about the destruction of all taints; fearless like a lion—I revere that Buddha.

Sabbamamaṅkāramutto, sabbaahaṃkārakkhayo;

Sabbadosamatikkanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.996.

Freed from all mine-making, He has destroyed all I-making; He has transcended all hatred—I revere that Buddha.

Sabbabhavapathaṃ khinno, sabbasaṅkhāranibbuto;

Sabbabandhanaṃ bhañjesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.997.

He has exhausted all paths of becoming, is extinguished in regard to all formations; He has broken all bondage—I revere that Buddha.

Sabbīghā samugghātesi, sabbindhā parinissago;

Sabbissā paṭinissaṭṭho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.998.

He has uprooted all distress, has relinquished all fuel; He has cast off all jealousy—I revere that Buddha.

Sabbagiddhāgijjhācāgī, sabbamohamāyānudo;

Sabbābhijjhā vibhañjesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.999.

Abandoner of all greed and longing, dispeller of all delusion and deceit; He has shattered all covetousness—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Sabbakopakodhātīto, sabbadosaklesaccajo;

Sabbadohamatikkanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1000.

He has gone beyond all anger and wrath, has abandoned all hatred and defilements; He has transcended all malice—I revere that Buddha.

Sabbarāgaṃ virajjesi, sabbadosavināsako;

Sabbamohamatikkanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1001.

He has become dispassionate toward all lust, is the destroyer of all hatred; He has overcome all delusion—I revere that Buddha.

Sabbamalaṃ pakkhālesi, sabbasallasandālako;

Sabbakhilaṃ viddālesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1002.

He has washed away all stains, has uprooted all darts; He has shattered all spiritual barrenness—I revere that Buddha.

Sabbabhayamatikkanto, sabbabhītibyantikato;

Sabbachambhisamucchinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1003.

He has transcended all fear, has brought all dread to an end; He has cut off all terror—I revere that Buddha.

Sabbalobhaabbuḷhanto, sabbarajaṃ virājesī;

Sabbanandī vicchindesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1004.

He has uprooted all greed, is free from all dust; He has cut off all delight—I revere that Buddha.

Sabbasokasamucchinno, sabbadosavisosako;

Sabbanettiṃ nivāresi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1005.

He has cut off all sorrow, has dried up all hatred; He has warded off all cravings—I revere that Buddha.

Sabbajātiṃ nijjaresi, sabbabhavagginibbuto;

Sabbalokamatikkanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1006.

He has worn out all birth, has extinguished the fire of all existence; He has transcended all the world—I revere that Buddha.

Sabbasaṃsaṭṭhavirato, sabbasaṃsaggaārako;

Sabbāsattinirattako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1007.

He abstains from all association, is far from all intimacy; He has cast off all attachment—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Sabbākaṅkhāparikkhīṇo, sabbāpekkhāvikkhambhako;

Sabbābhilāsā bhañjesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1008.

He has exhausted all longing, has suppressed all expectations; He has shattered all cravings—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Sabbāsaṅkā nivāresi, sabbātaṅkāvicchindako;

Sabbāvilaṃ vikkhālesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1009.

He has dispelled all doubt, has cut off all anxiety; He has washed away all turbidity—I revere that Buddha.

Sabbālayasamucchinno, sabbāsayaanissito;

Sabbāsave visosesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1010.

He has cut off all attachments, is dependent on no support; He has dried up all taints—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Sabbupadhiṃ niddhovesi, sabbārammaṇariñcako;

Sabbābhimānaṃ bhañjesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1011.

He has washed off all substrata, has abandoned all sense objects; He has shattered all pride—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Sabbupāyāsaṃ ukkhitto, sabbupādānaṃ ujjhito;

Sabbābhijjhā ucchedesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1012.

He has cast off all despair, has relinquished all clinging; He has eradicated all covetousness—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Sabbasaṃyojanaṃ chetvā, suddhājīvī suddhācaro;

Saṅgātigo oghatiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1013.

Having cut off all fetters, of pure livelihood and pure conduct; He has transcended attachment and crossed the flood—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Sabbadomanassasanto, sabbasaṃyojanā hato;

Sabbāsaṅkāvippamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1014.

He has stilled all discontent, has destroyed all fetters; He is completely freed from all doubt—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Sabbasokavītivatto, sabbanīvaraṇānudo;

Sabbadomanassamutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1015.

He has transcended all sorrow, is the dispeller of all hindrances; He is freed from all discontent—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Sabbabhītibhayātīto, chambhanasuñño sabbaso;

Sabbākulatā nāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1016.

He has gone beyond all fear and dread, is utterly devoid of trembling; He has destroyed all agitation—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Sabbabhoge pahāsi yo, sabbāsattipariccajo;

Sabbakāmamatikkamo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1017.

He who has abandoned all enjoyments, has renounced all attachment; He has overcome all sensual desires—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Sabbottamayogakkhemī, sabbottamaṃ adhigamo;

Sabbottamasantimanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1018.

Possessing the supreme security from bondage, He has made the supreme attainment; He is endowed with the supreme peace—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Sabbavositavosāno, katakicco yatindriyo;

Mahāmohaṇṇavuttiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1019.

He has lived the holy life to fulfillment, his task is done, his faculties are restrained; He has crossed the great ocean of delusion—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Dukkhakkhandhaṃ pariññāto, taṇhāpāsavicchedako;

Sabbasantāsasumutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1020.

He has fully understood the aggregate of suffering, has severed the snare of craving; He is completely freed from all terror—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Sabbayogavītivatto, sītibhūto nirūpadhi;

Bhavarāgaparikkhīṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1021.

He has transcended all yokes, has become cool and without substratum; He has utterly destroyed the craving for existence—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Sabbejā samatikkanto, sabbadhiṃ samatāṭhito;

Sadā sumatidāyako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1022.

He has overcome all agitation, is established in equanimity in every respect; He is always a giver of good understanding—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Bhavena nirāsattiko, paramoyaṃ samussayo;

Pahāsi sabbasaṅkhāre, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1023.

He is without attachment to existence, this is his final body; He has abandoned all formations—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Sabbadukkhaparimutto, dosadohapadhaṃsako;

Bhavaganthaṃ padālesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1024.

He is completely freed from all suffering, the destroyer of hate and malice; He has shattered the bond of existence—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Aṃsumālī accimālī, raṃsimālī dhammaravi;

Moha’māvasī nāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1025.

Garlanded with rays, garlanded with flames, garlanded with beams, the sun of the Dhamma; He has dispelled the new-moon darkness of delusion—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Candimā’va santikaro, candimā’va pabhākaro;

Santipabhā saṇṭhapesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1026.

Like the moon, a bringer of peace, like the moon, a maker of light; He established the radiance of peace—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Aggikkhandho’va suppabho, bhabbatejo sukhālayo;

Obhāsito ukkādhārī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1027.

Like a mass of fire, brightly shining, of auspicious splendor, an abode of bliss; Illumined, a torch-bearer—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Accimā’va pabhassaro, sabbaloke ālokadā;

Atiruciro obhāso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1028.

Like a flame, luminous, a giver of light to all the world; His radiance is exceedingly beautiful—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Byāmappabhāya suppabho, karuṇāruṇaujjalo;

Rucirābhāya sampanno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1029.

Brilliant with a fathom's light, radiant with the crimson glow of compassion; Endowed with beautiful splendor—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Induviya’mbaramajjhe, saṅghamajjhe virocayi;

Ñāṇālokaṃ vikiresi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1030.

Like the moon in the midst of the sky, in the midst of the Saṅgha he shone; He scattered the light of wisdom—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Ādiccabandhu ātāpī, dibbarūpo virocano;

Sabbalokaṃ pajjotesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1031.

Kinsman of the Sun, ardent, of divine form, radiant; He illuminates all the world—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Majjhe samaṇasaṅghassa, ādicco’va virocayī;

Vijjālokakaro loke, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1032.

In the midst of the Saṅgha of ascetics, he shone forth like the sun; A maker of the light of knowledge in the world—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Tārā’va samaṇamajjhe, puṇṇindusamasobhito;

Sabbasattuttamo sāmī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1033.

Like a star in the midst of ascetics, shining like the full moon; The supreme lord of all beings—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Cāruñāṇasikhādhārī, sobhājotisamujjalo;

Pabhassaraṃ pabhāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1034.

Bearing the crest of beautiful wisdom, blazing with beautiful light; He radiated pure light—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Dinakaro tamonudo, ditto timiraddhaṃsako;

Chabbaṇṇaraṃsīsobhito, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1035.

The Day-maker, dispeller of darkness, blazing, destroyer of gloom; Adorned with six-colored rays—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Indu’va nimmalo suddho, paramapuriso yasī;

Varalakkhaṇasampuṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1036.

Like the moon, stainless and pure, the glorious supreme person; Perfect in noble marks—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Puṇṇindasadiso juṇho, sampuṇṇasantamānaso;

Sabbato sītalībhūto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1037.

Like the full moon, bright and radiant, with a mind fully perfected and peaceful; He has become cool in every way—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Cārudassī piyadassī, aṅgīraso ālokito;

Pabhaṅkaro’va ujjoto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1038.

Lovely to see, delightful to behold, the radiant Aṅgīrasa; Shining like the maker of light—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Indu’va ambaratale, abhivaṇṇo susobhito;

Mahādhammappabhāyutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1039.

Like the moon in the sky, of excellent complexion and beautifully adorned; Endowed with the great light of the Dhamma—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Ārocito obhāsito, suriyo’va virocito;

Atulatejo tejassī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1040.

Declared and illumined, shining like the sun; Of unsurpassed splendor, the powerful one—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Sahassaraṃsī bhagavā, padumāmalasucchavī;

Dhammappabhāparivuto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1041.

The Blessed One with a thousand rays, his skin the hue of a spotless lotus; Enveloped in the light of the Dhamma—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Sobhito sālarājā’va, puṇṇamāyaṃ’va candimā;

Obhāsesi disā sabbā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1042.

Glorious as a kingly sāl tree, like the moon on a full-moon night; He illuminated all directions—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Raṃsijālaparikkhitto, padumānanalocano;

Kanakaṃ’va virocesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1043.

Encircled by a net of rays, with a lotus-like face and eyes; He shines like gold—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Mettāpabhāparivuto, dhammabhānupabhāsito;

Paññappabhā vikāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1044.

Enveloped in the light of loving-kindness, illuminated by the sun of the Dhamma; He caused the light of wisdom to shine forth—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Paridhoto parisuddho, candimā iva puṇṇako;

Saddhamaṃ parisodhesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1045.

Cleansed and purified, like the full moon; He purified the true Dhamma—I pay homage to that Buddha.

Ñāṇaraṃsiṃ vikiresi, dhammādiccasubhāsuro;

Muttipanthaṃ pakāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1046.

Scattering rays of wisdom, the well-shining Dhamma-sun; he revealed the path to liberation, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Antalikkhe puṇṇindu’va, sītalapabhādāyako;

Suddhadhammajotikaro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1047.

Like the full moon in the sky, bestowing cool radiance; illuminating the pure Dhamma, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Saddhammajotiṃ jotesi, jutivanto jutindharo;

Sabbalokaṃ pajjotesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1048.

He who lit the light of the true Dhamma, radiant and bearing radiance; he illuminated the entire world, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pabhassaro tilokaggo, saddhammassa sudīpako;

Ariyañāṇaṃ vitthāresi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1049.

Brilliant, the pinnacle of the three worlds, the great lamp of the true Dhamma; he expanded the Noble Knowledge, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Candimā’va gaganatale, sītābhā suppakāsako;

Anūnasukhadāyako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1050.

Like the moon in the sky, cool-rayed, shining brightly; bestower of undiminished happiness, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Jalitappadīpaṃ ditto, tilokassatimiraharo;

Ariyamaggaṃ jotesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1051.

A blazing, shining lamp, dispeller of the darkness of the three worlds; he illuminated the Noble Path, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Jutiṅkaro jotidharo, pabhaṅkaro pabhādharo;

Ābhādharo ābhākaro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1052.

The maker of radiance, the bearer of light, the maker of brilliance, the bearer of splendor; the bearer of radiance, the maker of light, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Avijjātamaṃ dhaṃsesi, vijjābhānusamujjalo;

Dhammavibhā vibhāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1053.

He destroyed the darkness of ignorance, blazing forth with the light of wisdom; he illuminated the distinctions of the Dhamma, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Tilokatimiraṃ hantā, medhāmuddhāsamujjalo;

Lokālokakaro nātho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1054.

The destroyer of the darkness of the three worlds, radiant with the height of wisdom; the world-illuminating lord, the refuge, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mohatimiraṃ dhaṃsesi, pabhākaro padīpako;

Saccadhammaṃ pakāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1055.

He dispelled the darkness of delusion, the light-maker, the lamp; he revealed the true Dhamma, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Karuṇāruṇo āloko, paññāraṃsipakāsako;

Avijjāvaraṇabhinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1056.

The dawn-light of compassion, illuminator of wisdom's rays, the breaker of the veil of ignorance; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Karuṇākaro paññābho, tibhave ālokaṅkaro;

Santirasmī vipphāresi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1057.

The maker of compassion, whose splendor is wisdom, the light-maker in the three worlds; he spread forth the rays of peace, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Puṇṇindu viya suditto, ñāṇindu ñāṇapuṇṇiko;

Saddhammābhā pabhāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1058.

Like the full moon, shining brightly, the wisdom-moon, full of wisdom; he radiated the light of the true Dhamma, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mohatimisikāchinno, dīpaṅkaro sudīpito;

Bodhippabhā pabhāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1059.

He who has cut through the darkness of delusion, the lamp-maker, well-illumined; he radiated the light of awakening, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Avijjānisā nāsesi, tiloke dhammasūriyo;

Dhammaraṃsiṃ vikiresi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1060.

He dispelled the night of ignorance, the sun of Dhamma in the three worlds; he scattered the rays of Dhamma, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Avijjācchādite loke, vijjālokaṃ vikāsayī;

Dhammappabhā visajjesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1061.

In a world shrouded by ignorance, he made manifest the light of wisdom; he sent forth the radiance of Dhamma, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mohanisā vināsesi, dhammujjalo divākaro;

Lokālokaṃ ālokesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1062.

He destroyed the night of delusion, the sun blazing with Dhamma; he illuminated the world and what is beyond, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Tibhavassa tamohantā, tejassī jinasūriyo;

Ābhassaro ābhāpuṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1063.

The dispeller of darkness in the triple world, radiant, the victorious sun; the shining one, full of splendor, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhaddatejo mahātejo, tibbatejo tejabahū;

Jhānatejo ñāṇatejo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1064.

Of auspicious splendor, of great splendor, of intense splendor, abundant in radiance; the splendor of jhāna, the splendor of wisdom, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Uggatejo aggatejo, puṇṇatejo tejissaro;

Brahmatejo dhammatejo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1065.

Supreme in radiance, foremost in radiance, full of radiance, lord of splendor; Brahma's radiance, Dhamma's radiance, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbadisā pabhāsesi, sabbatthaṃ sampakāsako;

Mahāpabhassaro uggo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1066.

He illuminates all directions, revealing all meaning; the great, radiant, and sublime one, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bhūripañño pabhaṅkaro, sabbathā tamanāsako;

Ariyasaccaṃ jotesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1067.

Of vast wisdom, the light-maker, the dispeller of darkness in every way; he illuminated the Noble Truths, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Raṃsimanto raṃsidharo, jotimanto jotikaro;

Sahassaraṃsi jotindo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1068.

Radiant, the bearer of rays, shining, the maker of light; the thousand-rayed lord of splendor, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Puṇṇindu sadisaditto, paññāobhāso bhāsuro;

Mahāmohatamaṃ bhinno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1069.

Shining like the full moon, gleaming with the light of wisdom; he shattered the great darkness of delusion, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mohaamāvasīnāsī, mahādicco mahappabho;

Sabbalokaṃ pabhāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1070.

Destroyer of the new moon of delusion, the great sun, of great radiance; he illuminated the entire world, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Aṅgāriva accimanto, vijjādīpo rasmidharo;

Mohatimisaṃ dhaṃsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1071.

Like a charcoal ember ablaze, radiant, the wisdom-lamp, bearer of rays; he dispelled the gloom of delusion, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Tamacchanne bhave sabbe, ñāṇālokena mocayī;

Dhammajotiṃ ujjotesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1072.

In all worlds covered by darkness, he freed them with the light of knowledge; he kindled the light of the Dhamma, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Avijjāndhakārahantā, sabbaññū sabbato pabho;

Vijjāyanaṃ pabhāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1073.

Destroyer of the darkness of ignorance, the All-Knowing, radiant in every way; he illuminated the path to wisdom, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbatamantarahito, sabbathā sabbato pabho;

Sabbasaccaṃ pakāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1074.

Free from all darkness, radiant in every way and from every side; he revealed all truth, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbalokānukampāya, sabbaloke jotiṅkaro;

Sabbaloke tamoharo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1075.

Out of compassion for all the world, the light-maker in all the world; the dispeller of darkness in all the world, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbaññutaṃ pakāsesi, sabbassa dassāvī isi;

Mohamūlaṃ viddhaṃsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1076.

He proclaimed omniscience, the seer of all, the sage; he destroyed the root of delusion, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Jalanto ñāṇatejena, tikkhatejo atisayo;

Ñāṇaṃsumālī ātāpī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1077.

Burning with the radiance of wisdom, exceedingly sharp in radiance; the sun of knowledge, full of fervor, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Mohatamaṃ vināsesi, dhammajotipakāsako;

Bahujane pahāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1078.

He destroyed the darkness of delusion, the illuminator of the Dhamma's light; he gladdened the many people, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ghoratamācchannaloke, mahāñāṇena mocayī;

Dhammālokaṃ pakāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1079.

In a world shrouded in dreadful darkness, he freed beings with great wisdom; he revealed the light of Dhamma, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Karuṇāruṇo jotido, sabbasokapanūdano;

Taṇhātimiraṃ dhaṃsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1080.

The dawn of compassion, giver of light, dispeller of all sorrow; he destroyed the darkness of craving, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Tamācchanne sabbaloke, dhammālokakaro pabhū;

Avijjābhantibhañjako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1081.

In a world cloaked in darkness, the powerful one, bringer of the Dhamma's light; the destroyer of the delusion of ignorance, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Avijjāvaraṇaṃ chetvā, vijjālokapabhāsako;

Paññāppabhā pabhaṃkārī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1082.

Having cut through the veil of ignorance, radiating the light of knowledge, the maker of wisdom's radiance; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ghoratamaṃ niddhaṃsesi, viddhaṃsesi tayo bhave;

Narādicco varādicco, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1083.

He dispelled the dreadful darkness, he shattered the three realms of existence; the sun of men, the excellent sun, to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ñāṇajotiṃ pajjotesi, mohāvaraṇanāsako;

Dhammappabhaṃ pabhāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1084.

He kindled the light of wisdom, the destroyer of the veil of delusion; he caused the radiance of the Dhamma to shine forth—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammajotiṃ vijotesi, pāpatamaniddhaṃsako;

Mokkhāyanaṃ pakāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1085.

He shone with the light of the Dhamma, the destroyer of the darkness of evil; he revealed the path to liberation—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammadīpaṃ padīpesi, pāpāvaraṇa chedako;

Muttipathaṃ paññāpesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1086.

He lit the lamp of the Dhamma, the cutter of evil's obstructions; he made known the path to liberation—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paññābhānu paññāppabho, uddhataṃ andhatamaṃ haro;

Lokālokaṃ pakāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1087.

The sun of wisdom, the radiance of wisdom, dispeller of the arisen blinding darkness; he revealed the light unto the world—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammadīpapajjalito, mohatamasandālako;

Varadhammamujjotesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1088.

Blazing with the lamp of the Dhamma, burning away the darkness of delusion; he illuminated the excellent Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paññā’lokaṃ pajjalesi, tamakkhandhappadālako;

Pañcakkhandhaṃ nirodhesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1089.

He lit the light of wisdom, the dispeller of the mass of darkness; he brought the five aggregates to cessation—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammabhānu bhabbappabho, sabbañāṇatamaṃ hato;

Ujjalo jotijjalito, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1090.

The sun of the Dhamma, a light for the worthy, destroyer of the darkness of all ignorance; resplendent, blazing with light—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paññājotippabhandharo, sabbalokālokakaro;

Mokkhadhammaṃ pabhāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1091.

The bearer of the radiance of wisdom's light, the maker of light for all the world; he proclaimed the Dhamma of liberation—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Avijjāndhakāraṃ hantā, dhammālokappakāsako;

Pabhākārī ābhākārī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1092.

The destroyer of the darkness of ignorance, the illuminator of the Dhamma's light; the maker of radiance, the maker of light—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Ojassī ojasampanno, tapassī tapasekharo;

Tejabhūsito tejassī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1093.

Vigorous, endowed with vigor, an ascetic supreme in austerity; adorned with radiance, full of majesty—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Dhammamaṅgalasammato, dhammābhāparimaṇḍito;

Dhammaṃ suṭṭhuparivutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1094.

Revered as the blessing of the Dhamma, adorned with the Dhamma's splendor; well-encompassed by the Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Paññābhāparimaṇḍito, paññābhāparidīpako;

Paññaṃsumā paññaccimā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1095.

Adorned with the splendor of wisdom, illuminating with the splendor of wisdom; possessing wisdom’s rays, possessing wisdom’s flame—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Aṃsumanto ābhāvanto, tejavanto jutikaro;

Pabhāvanto sobhāvanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1096.

Possessing rays, possessing splendor, possessing majesty, the maker of brilliance; possessing power, possessing beauty—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Koṭibhānusamappabho, candimā’va samujjalo;

Abhikkanto adhikkanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1097.

With a radiance like ten million suns, shining brightly like the moon; excellent, supreme—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbakaṅkhā nivāresi, mārasena vidhūpako;

Sabbalokaṃ obhāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1098.

He dispelled all doubts, the scatterer of Māra’s army; he illuminated the whole world—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Amitābho atulābho, loke amitālokadā;

Amitojo atulojo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1099.

Of immeasurable radiance, of peerless radiance, giver of boundless light to the world; of immeasurable vigor, of unequaled vigor—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Sabbalokaṃ pabhāsesi, abbhāmutto’va candimā;

Suddhaṃ saddhammaṃ dīpesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1100.

He illuminated the entire world, like the moon freed from clouds; he revealed the pure, true Dhamma—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Aggi yathā pajjalito, devatā’va virocayi;

Mahā’vijjā viddhaṃsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1101.

Blazing like a fire, shining like a deity, he shattered great ignorance; to that Buddha I pay homage.

Santidhammaṃ pakāsesi, suriyo tamacchaddako;

Santataṃ santiṃ pasāresi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1102.

He revealed the Dhamma of peace, a sun that dispels darkness; he constantly spread peace—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Lokamitto lokahito, lokabandhu lokasakhā;

Tiloke ālokakaro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1103.

The world's friend, the world's benefactor, the world's kinsman, the world's companion; the light-maker in the three worlds—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pītipassaddhikhāyī ca, pītirasapāyī bahū;

Pītibhakkhī pītimano, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1104.

Experiencer of joy and serenity, the great one who drinks the nectar of joy; one who feeds on joy, with a joyful mind—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Pītipāmojjajanako, paramaṃ sukhaṃ bhojako;

Paṭisallānanirato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1105.

The generator of joy and gladness, one who bestows supreme happiness; delighting in seclusion—to that Buddha I pay homage.

Bodhitejo bodhiraṃsi, bodhippabhāya maṇḍito;

Brahmatejo brahmaraṃsi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1106.

The splendor of awakening, the rays of awakening, adorned with the light of awakening; the splendor of Brahmā, the rays of Brahmā—to that Buddha I bow down.

Brahmakāyo brahmarūpo, brahmadhammo yo brāhmaṇo;

Brahmapatto brahmabhūto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1107.

The body of Brahmā, the form of Brahmā, the Dhamma of Brahmā—that Brahmin; attained to Brahmā, become Brahmā—to that Buddha I bow down.

Brahmapañño brahmacārī, brahmavihārī brahmaññū;

Brahmacakkhu dhammacakkhu, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1108.

Of Brahmā-wisdom, living the holy life, abiding in the divine abodes, knowing the supreme; the Brahmā-eye, the Dhamma-eye—to that Buddha I bow down.

Bahūpakārī mettāvā, mahākāruññamānaso;

Muttinayaṃ niddesesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1109.

Benefactor of many, full of loving-kindness, with a heart of great compassion; he taught the path to liberation—to that Buddha I bow down.

Bahū bhavoghā tāresi, sudakkho nāviko yathā;

Mettāvegaparakkamī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1110.

Like a skilled boatman, he carried many across the flood of becoming; mighty in the surge of loving-kindness—to that Buddha I bow down.

Kalyāṇakāmī nibbano, anantakaruṇālayo;

Anūnaka kalyāṇaññū, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1111.

Seeking the good, free from craving, an abode of boundless compassion; perfect, knowing the good—to that Buddha I bow down.

Cakkhudado ñāṇadado, sabbalokānukampako;

Karuṇāpuṇṇamānaso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1112.

Giver of the eye, giver of wisdom, compassionate to all the world; his heart filled with compassion—to that Buddha I bow down.

Kalyāṇacitto acaṇḍo, mettāmano akkodhano;

Visuddhacitto akkoso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1113.

Kind-hearted, not fierce, his mind full of loving-kindness, free from anger; pure-hearted, not scolding—to that Buddha I bow down.

Kalyāṇakārī kalyāṇo, kalyāṇapathanāyako;

Kalyāṇapāramīppatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1114.

Doer of good, the auspicious one, leader on the path of good; attained to the perfection of goodness—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nissīmakaruṇākārī, ussannakaruṇānidhī;

Gaṃbhīrakaruṇālayo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1115.

Boundless doer of compassion, a treasure of overflowing compassion; a profound abode of compassion—to that Buddha I bow down.

Mahākāruṇiko dhīro, karuṇāñāṇasāgaro;

Atīvakaruṇākārī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1116.

Greatly compassionate, wise, an ocean of compassion and wisdom; exceedingly compassionate—to that Buddha I bow down.

Kalyāṇakārī kāruñño, kalyāṇamitto mettavā;

Kalyāṇaṃ dhammaṃ desesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1117.

Doer of good, compassionate, a noble friend, full of loving-kindness; he taught the good Dhamma—to that Buddha I bow down.

Karuṇāhadayo nātho, khemino khemamānaso;

Muditacitto mādako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1118.

His heart full of compassion, a refuge, secure, his mind at peace; joyful, inspiring joy—to that Buddha I bow down.

Cakkavattiṃ vivajjitvā, patto sabbaññutaṃ budho;

Nissesaṃ karuṇāsindhu, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1119.

Renouncing universal kingship, the Awakened One attained omniscience; an ocean of compassion, without remainder—to that Buddha I bow down.

Kodhadosamahāaggiṃ, mettodakena siñcayī;

Sabbalokassa hitakārī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1120.

He quenched the great fire of anger and malice with the water of loving-kindness; working for the welfare of all the world—to that Buddha I bow down.

Karuṇāsītalacitto, sabbasattānukampako;

Kalyāṇadhammena yutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1121.

With a heart cool with compassion, compassionate toward all beings, endowed with the noble Dhamma—to that Buddha I bow down.

Dayodadhi dayānidhi, dayālu dayāsāgaro;

Dayādhipo dayānātho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1122.

An ocean of compassion, a treasure of compassion, compassionate, a sea of compassion; lord of compassion, protector of compassion—to that Buddha I bow down.

Mahādayo mahodayo, mahāsayo mahesi yo;

Sadādayo sadāsayo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1123.

Great in compassion, great in glory, a great refuge, the great seer; ever compassionate, ever a refuge—to that Buddha I bow down.

Hitado sabbasattānaṃ, sabbesānaṃ sukhadado;

Santido sabbapāṇānaṃ, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1124.

Giver of welfare to all beings, giver of happiness to all; giver of peace to all living beings—to that Buddha I bow down.

Pajjunnoriva bhūtāni, dhammameghena vassitā;

Santisukhakaro loke, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1125.

Like a rain cloud for beings, he rained down the Dhamma-cloud; a bringer of peace and happiness in the world—to that Buddha I bow down.

Parapekkhī parasevī, parasukhakārī isi;

Parahitesī paratthī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1126.

Looking to others, serving others, a sage, working for the happiness of others; seeking the welfare of others, devoted to the good of others—to that Buddha I bow down.

Catusaccaṃ pakāsesi, anukampāya pāṇinaṃ;

Bahūjane santāresi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1127.

He revealed the Four Noble Truths out of compassion for beings; he helped many people cross over—to that Buddha I bow down.

Sadā santo santidāyī, sukhito sukhadāyako;

Dhammameghaṃ pavassesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1128.

Ever peaceful, giver of peace, happy, giver of happiness; he poured down the Dhamma-cloud—to that Buddha I bow down.

Bahujanahitatthāya, anekabhavaṃ saṃsari;

Bahujanaṃ uddhāresi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1129.

For the welfare of many, he wandered through countless existences; lifting up many—to that Buddha I bow down.

Bahūnaṃ hitasukhāya, paripūresi pāramī;

Mokkhamaggaṃ gavesesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1130.

For the welfare and happiness of many, he fulfilled the perfections; seeking the path to liberation—to that Buddha I bow down.

Sabbattha samattacitto, sabbapāṇānukampako;

Sabbadā karuññacitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1131.

With a mind ever balanced, full of compassion for all beings; always with a heart of compassion—to that Buddha I bow down.

Sabbamitto sabbasakho, sabbabhūtānukampako;

Sabbasattahitakaro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1132.

A friend of all, a companion to all, compassionate to all beings; working for the good of all sentient beings—to that Buddha I bow down.

Sabbesānaṃ hitacintī, sabbesānaṃ sukhāvaho;

Sabbesānaṃ anukampī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1133.

Thinking of the welfare of all beings, bringing happiness to all beings; compassionate toward all beings—to that Buddha I bow down.

Sampuṇṇasukumāraṅgo, aṅgapaccaṅga sobhano;

Vaṇṇanīyo vandanīyo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1134.

With limbs perfectly delicate, his major and minor limbs beautiful; worthy of praise, worthy of homage—to that Buddha I bow down.

Sabbasobhā susobhito, sabbamahimāmaṇḍito;

Sabbapajānaṃ vallabho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1135.

Radiant with all beauty, adorned with all greatness; beloved by all beings—to that Buddha I bow down.

Ruciro abhiruciro, abhirūpo surūpavā;

Anomavaṇṇo suvaṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1136.

Charming, delightful, beautiful, of lovely form; of matchless complexion, golden-hued—to that Buddha I bow down.

Kantavaṇṇo kantarūpo, kantakitti kantayaso;

Kantajuṇho kantajoti, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1137.

With pleasing hue and delightful form, with glorious fame and radiant renown; of delightful brightness and shining light—to that Buddha I bow down.

Chaḷabhiññāppatto dhīmā, chavivaṇṇo sudassano;

Chabbaṇṇaraṃsī sobhano, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1138.

Having attained the six higher knowledges, wise, with skin of golden hue, beautiful to behold; splendid with six-colored rays—to that Buddha I bow down.

Kañcanagghiyasaṅkāso, niddoso kanakattaco;

Soṇṇānano suruciro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1139.

Like a golden statue, flawless, with golden skin, with a golden face, beautiful and radiant—to that Buddha I bow down.

Pabhāhi anurañjesi, mokkhapanthapakāsako;

Dhammarasmiparikkhitto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1140.

He who suffused the world with radiance, the revealer of the path to liberation; encompassed by the rays of the Dhamma—to that Buddha I bow down.

Sīhahanu’sabhakkhandho, ñāṇanibhāmaṇḍito;

Sumukho sundaro eso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1141.

With jaws like a lion, shoulders like a bull, adorned with the splendor of wisdom; with a lovely face, so beautiful—to that Buddha I bow down.

Kantiyutto kantidatto, manojo manamodano;

Sabbajane pamodesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1142.

Charming, bestower of charm, delighting the mind; bringing joy to all people—to that Buddha I bow down.

Suvaṇṇo suvaṇṇavaṇṇo, hemavaṇṇo vaṇṇuttamo;

Hiraññavaṇṇo hemāṃsu, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1143.

Golden, of golden color, of golden hue; of supreme complexion, of golden sheen, with golden rays—to that Buddha I bow down.

Kantakāyo kantasobho, kantaābho kantapabho;

Kantadassano kantimā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1144.

With a lovely body, of lovely splendor, of lovely luster, of lovely radiance; lovely to behold, endowed with loveliness—to that Buddha I bow down.

Mududhavaluṇṇo cāpi, aviraḷadantāvalī;

Ussaṅkhapādo bhagavā, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1145.

Having a soft, white brow-tuft, and unbroken rows of teeth; with high-arched insteps, the Blessed One—to that Buddha I bow down.

Dīghatanu dīghajivho, dīghabāhu dīghaṅgulī;

Sudaḷhahatthacaraṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1146.

Long-bodied, with a long tongue, long arms, and long fingers; with firm hands and feet—to that Buddha I bow down.

Brahmaghoso eṇijaṅgho, ujudeho brahmāsamo;

Rasaññū rasaggasaggī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1147.

With a voice like Brahmā, shanks like an antelope, an upright body, like unto Brahmā; knower of tastes, who has attained the supreme taste—to that Buddha I bow down.

Nīlakkhī dīghapaṇhī ca, kanakatuṅganāsiko;

Cakkavaraṅkitapādo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1148.

With dark-blue eyes and long heels, and a nose like a golden peak; with feet marked with excellent wheels—to that Buddha I bow down.

Cattālīsasamadanto, taruṇavaccha pakhumo;

Sīho’va pubbaddhakāyo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1149.

With forty even teeth, with eyelashes like a young calf’s, and with a torso like the forepart of a lion—to that Buddha I bow down.

Lomakūpekalomo ca, kañcanasadisattaco;

Odātadāḍhāsampanno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1150.

With a single hair in each pore, skin like gold, and endowed with pure white canine teeth—to that Buddha I bow down.

Sunīlamuddhaggalomo, byāmappabhāsumaṇḍito;

Jālikahatthacaraṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1151.

With deep blue-black hair on his head, adorned with a fathom-long radiance, and with webbed hands and feet—to that Buddha I bow down.

Kosohitavatthaguyho, suppatiṭṭhitacaraṇo;

Sīhahanu’ṇhīsasīso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1152.

With genitals concealed in a sheath, with well-planted feet, with a jaw like a lion’s and a head like a royal turban—to that Buddha I bow down.

Sabbamahāpurisaṅgo, battiṃsalakkhaṇadharo;

Asītānubyañjano yo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1153.

He who is endowed with all the marks of a Great Man, the bearer of the thirty-two major marks and the eighty minor characteristics—to that Buddha I bow down.

Ativiya manuñño ca, ativiya manoramo;

Ativiya manohārī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1154.

Exceedingly lovely, exceedingly delightful, and exceedingly charming—to that Buddha I bow down.

Accantakantimā kanto, sobhano piyadassano;

Battiṃsalakkhaṇapuṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1155.

Of utmost beauty, lovely, splendid, and pleasing to behold; replete with the thirty-two marks—to that Buddha I bow down.

Kantābho kañcanavaṇṇo, kañcanānanalocano;

Kañcanācalasaṅkāso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1156.

Of lovely radiance, golden-hued, with a golden face and eyes, and resembling a golden mountain—to that Buddha I bow down.

Kamanīyo kantanīyo, kañcanaṃ’va jutikaro;

Padumapattakkho rūpī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1157.

Desirable, delightful, shining like gold; with eyes like lotus petals, and beautiful in form—to that Buddha I bow down.

Kalyāṇadassano juṇho, ditto’va kanakācalo;

Jutimā dibbadassano, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1158.

Of graceful appearance, radiant, shining like a golden mountain; luminous, and of divine sight—to that Buddha I bow down.

Varalakkhaṇalaṅkito, sabbato sabbasundaro;

Somma sabbaṅgasobhano, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1159.

Adorned with excellent marks, beautiful in every way; gentle, and splendid in all his limbs—to that Buddha I bow down.

Guṇānamākaro pujjo, karavīkarutassaro;

Varalakkhaṇaākiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1160.

A mine of virtues, worthy of veneration, with a voice like the karavīka bird, and replete with excellent marks—to that Buddha I bow down.

Mahākāyo brahākāyo, brahmakāyo kāyaujū;

Kantikāyo santikāyo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1161.

With a great body, a lofty body, a Brahma-like body, an upright body, a lovely body, and a tranquil body—to that Buddha I bow down.

Accantasukhumālaṅgo, accantamudulatanu;

Accantasundaro sāmī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1162.

With limbs supremely delicate, with a body exceedingly tender, supremely lovely, the Lord—to that Buddha I bow down.

Jananetto janamoḷi, pasannanayanānano;

Kumudānanalocano, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1163.

The leader of people, the crest-jewel of humanity; with a serene face and eyes, and with a face and eyes like water-lilies—to that Buddha I bow down.

Pasannacitto pasādo, paṇīto atisobhito;

Pariyodāto paramo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1164.

With a serene mind, gracious, excellent, and most radiant; utterly pure, supreme—to that Buddha I bow down.

Bhabbarūpo bhaddarūpo, bhaddabhāṇī bhaddamukho;

Bhassaro bhāsuro bhiyyo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1165.

Of auspicious form, of excellent form, with auspicious speech and an auspicious face; radiant, luminous, and exceedingly so—to that Buddha I bow down.

Mañjughoso mañjuvāṇī, mañjubhāṇī mañjussaro;

Mañjubhāsī mudubhāsī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1166.

Of sweet sound, sweet speech, sweet utterance, and sweet voice; speaking sweetly, speaking softly—to that Buddha I bow.

Mudukāyo muducitto, muduko mudulakkhaṇo;

Mudumano mudubhaṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1167.

Gentle of body, gentle of mind, gentle, of gentle characteristics; gentle in thought, gentle in speech—to that Buddha I bow.

Varalakkhaṇasampanno, sabbaṅgasamannāgato;

Sabbaloke sabbuttamo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1168.

Endowed with excellent marks, complete in every part, supreme in all the world—to that Buddha I bow.

Susobhito sobhāyutto, santibhūsanabhūsito;

Tilokassa tuṅgaketuṃ, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1169.

Well-adorned, endowed with splendor, adorned with the ornament of peace, the lofty banner of the three worlds—to that Buddha I bow.

Ghorasaṃsāroghatiṇṇo, mokkhalaṅkāra’laṅkato;

Susobhayutto suguṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1170.

Having crossed the dreadful flood of saṃsāra, adorned with the ornament of liberation, endowed with great splendor, and virtuous—to that Buddha I bow.

Sabbaguṇasusampanno, sīlālaṅkāra’laṅkato;

Saddhammaratanaseṭṭho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1171.

Perfectly endowed with all good qualities, adorned with the ornament of virtue, the supreme jewel of the true Dhamma—to that Buddha I bow.

Anantakittivaṇṇo yo, sabbasaṃsāravandito;

Pasiddho vissuto pujjo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1172.

He who is of endless fame and glory, revered throughout all of saṃsāra, renowned, celebrated, and worthy of homage—to that Buddha I bow.

Suvandito sabbaloke, sabbaloke sambhāvito;

Sammānito sabbaloke, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1173.

Well-revered in all the world, esteemed in all the world, honored in all the world—to that Buddha I bow.

Devādhidevavandito, mahādevavināyako;

Tibhavavallabho khyāto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1174.

Revered by gods above gods, the great leader of gods, beloved and famed in the three existences—to that Buddha I bow.

Dassaneyyo thomaneyyo, pasaṃsaneyyo pāmokkho;

Vaṇṇanīyo vandanīyo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1175.

Worthy to be seen, worthy to be praised, praiseworthy, preeminent; worthy of description, worthy of homage—to that Buddha I bow.

Pasādanīyo pasīdo, pāsādiko passaddhiko;

Pūjanīyo accanīyo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1176.

Inspiring serenity, serene, charming, tranquil; worthy of veneration, worthy of honor—to that Buddha I bow.

Apacito sakkārito, pathito abhivādito;

Nissīmasilāghāppatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1177.

Respected, revered, esteemed, and saluted; having attained boundless renown—to that Buddha I bow.

Tibhavavandito bhiyyo, oraṃ tīraṃ pāraṅgato;

Anagho paññasekharo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1178.

Exceedingly revered in the three existences, gone beyond the near and the far shore; immaculate, crowned with wisdom—to that Buddha I bow.

Balavanto phalavanto, bahūhi bahumānito;

Bahūpakārī bodhindo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1179.

Mighty, fruitful, greatly honored by many; a great benefactor, the Lord of Enlightenment—to that Buddha I bow.

Sabbaloke namassito, sabbaloke sammānito;

Manujāmarasakkato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1180.

Revered in all the world, honored in all the world, worshipped by humans and gods—to that Buddha I bow.

Lokānaṃ uttamo pujjo, susakkato sagāravo;

Vaṇṇakittibhato kanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1181.

Supreme among the worlds, worthy of worship, well-honored and revered; possessed of glory and fame, beloved—to that Buddha I bow.

Khyāto pakhyāto sukhyāto, vissavikhyāto vandito;

Vaṇṇādhiko vitthāriko, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1182.

Famed, renowned, well-famed, universally famed, revered; excelling in glory, spreading wide—to that Buddha I bow.

Sabbaseṭṭho sabbajeṭṭho, sabbasuddho sabbuttamo;

Sabbavandito mānito, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1183.

Best of all, greatest of all, purest of all, supreme among all; revered and honored by all—to that Buddha I bow.

Sabbe devenuvattesi, sabbadevavināyako;

Tilokamahito nātho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1184.

Followed by all gods, the leader of all gods; the protector, honored in the three worlds—to that Buddha I bow.

Sabbalokassa vinetā, sabbalokatikicchako;

Sabbesaṃ seṭṭho dhammaṭṭho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1185.

The guide of all the world, the physician of all the world, foremost of all beings, steadfast in Dhamma—to that Buddha I bow.

Sabbalokābhibhū vīro, sabbalokuttamo jino;

Vissuto sabbalokamhi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1186.

The conqueror of all the world, the hero; the victor, supreme in all the world; renowned in all the world—to that Buddha I bow.

Sabbābhiññāparipuṇṇo, sabbalokasmiṃ vissuto;

Samantacakkhū pasiddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1187.

Complete in all the higher knowledges, famed in all the world; the All-Seeing One, renowned—to that Buddha I bow.

Sabbalokahito nātho, sabbalokasukhāvaho;

Supūjito sabbaloke, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1188.

The benefactor and protector of all the world, the bringer of happiness to all the world, highly revered in all the world—to that Buddha I bow.

Sabbicchaṃ anicchanto, ucchinnachando sabbadhi;

Mahātaṇhāṇṇavuttiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1189.

Desiring no desire, with volition cut off everywhere, having crossed the great ocean of craving—to that Buddha I bow.

Yathāpekkhī tathā’kkhāsi, sabbathā saccamānaso;

Avitathaṃ viññāpesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1190.

As he perceived, so he spoke, his mind truthful in every way; he declared the unerring truth—to that Buddha I bow.

Ñāṇaññū ñāṇasampanno, paññāsampanno paññavā;

Dhammojo dhammappasanto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1191.

Knower of knowledge, endowed with knowledge, endowed with wisdom, the wise one; born of Dhamma, serene in Dhamma—to that Buddha I bow.

Suguṇesu susampanno, sādhu sappuriso sudhī;

Mahāsumatisāgaro, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1192.

Well-endowed with excellent virtues, virtuous, a true person, wise; a great ocean of profound understanding—to that Buddha I bow.

Nāmadassī rūpadassī, tapassī ca taponidhi;

Yathābhūtaṃ vipassī ca, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1193.

Seer of name and seer of form, an ascetic and a treasure of austerity, one who sees things as they truly are—to that Buddha I bow.

Upeto buddhadhammehi, aṭṭhārasahi nāyako;

Iddhippatto mahāsiddho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1194.

The leader, endowed with the eighteen qualities of a Buddha; possessed of psychic powers, the greatly accomplished one—to that Buddha I bow.

Sīlañca samādhiṃ patto, patto paññaṃ niyyānikaṃ;

Paripuṇṇadhammappatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1195.

Having attained virtue and concentration, having attained the wisdom that leads to liberation, having attained the perfected Dhamma—to that Buddha I bow.

Parisuddhadhammappatto, sabbavipallāsanudo;

Tibhavāṇṇavanittiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1196.

Having attained the purified Dhamma, dispeller of all distortions, and crossed over the ocean of the three existences—to that Buddha I bow down.

Rāgānalaṃ nibbāpesi, sītibhūto sītaṅkaro;

Santisudhā vassāpesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1197.

He extinguished the fire of passion, having become cool, the maker of coolness; he rained down the nectar of peace—to that Buddha I bow down.

Titthiye sunimmadditvā, ṭhapetvā micchāmaññitaṃ;

Saccadhamme patiṭṭhesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1198.

Having thoroughly crushed the sectarians and removed false conceits, he established beings in the true Dhamma—to that Buddha I bow down.

Vimutto mānusāsattiṃ, dibbāsattiṃ upaccago;

Sabbāsattivinimmutto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1199.

Freed from human clinging, having transcended divine clinging, completely released from all clinging—to that Buddha I bow down.

Tibhavetimiraharo, bodhiñāṇappabhandharo;

Dhammappadīpako bhabbo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1200.

Dispeller of the darkness of the three existences, bearer of the stream of enlightenment-knowledge, the capable illuminator of the lamp of the Dhamma—to that Buddha I bow down.

Jhāniko jhānasampanno, aññāṇadheyyadhaṃsako;

Suṭṭhujhānī mahājhānī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1201.

A meditator accomplished in jhāna, destroyer of the dominion of ignorance, a most excellent meditator, a great meditator—to that Buddha I bow down.

Taṇhakkhayappatto nāgo, bhavanettipacchindako;

Āsattiṃ parimaddesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1202.

The great one who has attained the destruction of craving, the cutter of the cord of becoming, who crushed clinging—to that Buddha I bow down.

Sabbakammakilesāni, anavasesa vāhayī;

Dhammagaṅgā pavāhesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1203.

He washed away all karmic defilements without remainder; he set flowing the river of Dhamma—to that Buddha I bow down.

Damappatto yamappatto, samappatto samācaro;

Dhuvaṃ sassataṃ sampatto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1204.

Having attained self-taming, having attained restraint, having attained peace, of even conduct; having attained the stable and eternal—to that Buddha I bow down.

Buddhimā mutimā ceva, matimā ca matissaro;

Muttimā mettamānaso, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1205.

Wise, intelligent, and discerning, a lord of intellect; liberated, with a mind of loving-kindness—to that Buddha I bow down.

Ṭhānāṭhānesu kusalo, dhammesu atikovido;

Laddhamedho sumedhāvī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1206.

Skilled in the possible and impossible, deeply versed in the teachings, possessing gained wisdom, supremely wise—to that Buddha I bow down.

Bhavadukkhoghanittiṇṇo, taṇhāpahānapāragū;

Aññāṇanisā nāsesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1207.

Crossed over the flood of the suffering of existence, gone to the far shore by abandoning craving; he destroyed the night of ignorance—to that Buddha I bow down.

Dhīrahadayo dhorayho, saṃvuto saṃyatamano;

Guttindriyo guttamāno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1208.

Steadfast-hearted, a mighty leader, restrained, with a controlled mind; with guarded senses and a guarded mind—to that Buddha I bow down.

Susaṃvuto santindriyo, santuṭṭho susamāhito;

Vijjācaraṇasampuṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1209.

Well-restrained, with senses calmed, content, well-composed; perfect in knowledge and conduct—to that Buddha I bow down.

Jāticakkaṃ vicuṇṇesi, natthi dāni punabbhavo;

Tilokaoghanittiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1210.

He shattered the wheel of birth, there is now no renewed existence; crossed over the flood of the three worlds—to that Buddha I bow down.

Asaṃsaṭṭho agārehi, anāgārehi cūbhayaṃ;

Anokasārī asaṅgo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1211.

Unassociated with both householders and the homeless; wandering without a home, unfettered—to that Buddha I bow down.

Pahīnajātimaraṇo, anupādāya nibbuto;

Saṃsārasāgaraṃ tiṇṇo, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1212.

He has abandoned birth and death, extinguished without clinging; having crossed the ocean of saṃsāra, to that Buddha I bow down.

Maggagavesī maggagū, maggakkhāto tathāgato;

Amatamaggaṃ dassāvī, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1213.

Seeker of the path, knower of the path, proclaimer of the path, the Tathāgata; revealer of the deathless path, to that Buddha I bow down.

Yathā adakkhi akkhāsi, bhagavā bhūrimedhaso;

Nikkāmo nimmalo nātho, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1214.

As he saw, so he proclaimed, the Blessed One of vast wisdom; free from desire, stainless, the protector, to that Buddha I bow down.

Moneyyaseṭṭho munindo, ratiṃ ca aratiṃ cuto;

Bhayātīto bhavātīto, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1215.

Foremost in sagehood, lord of sages, passed beyond delight and aversion; gone beyond fear, gone beyond existence, to that Buddha I bow down.

Pabuddhapariṇāyako, atiuccupakārako;

Sabboccasukhadāyako, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1216.

Leader of the awakened, of supreme beneficence; bestower of all highest bliss, to that Buddha I bow down.

Amalino amaliccho, nimmalo malamajjano;

Madamaddo vītamado, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1217.

Stainless, undefiled, pure, a cleanser of impurities; crusher of conceit, free from intoxication, to that Buddha I bow down.

Apacito namassito, pathito abhivādito;

Devamanujaaccito, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1218.

Revered, honored, praised, saluted; worshipped by gods and humans, to that Buddha I bow down.

Byāmappabhābhirucito, dvattiṃsalakkhaṇaddharo;

Anubyañjanasampanno, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1219.

Adorned with a fathom's radiance, bearer of the thirty-two marks, endowed with the minor characteristics, to that Buddha I bow down.

Adaṇḍena asatthena, dhammena anusāsayi;

Uddharesi bahū satte, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1220.

Without rod or weapon, he instructed by the Dhamma; he rescued many beings, to that Buddha I bow down.

Dhañño dhammasudhā pāyī, taṇhāvisaviddhaṃsako;

Sabbāvijjaṃ vicuṇṇesi, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1221.

Fortunate, drinker of the nectar of Dhamma, destroyer of the poison of craving; he crushed all ignorance, to that Buddha I bow down.

Dhañño nātho! Dhañño sāmī! Dhañño mārābhibhū munī!

Dhañño vijitasaṅgāmo! Buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1222.

Fortunate protector! Fortunate lord! Fortunate sage, conqueror of Māra! Fortunate victor in the struggle! To that Buddha I bow down.

Jhānārāmo jhānarato, dhammārāmo dhammarato;

Aho! Aho! Pāraṅgato, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1223.

Delighting in jhāna, devoted to jhāna, delighting in Dhamma, devoted to Dhamma; Ah! Ah! Gone to the far shore, to that Buddha I bow down.

Puṇḍarīko’va nillitto, passa tassa visuddhataṃ;

Alaggamānaso eko, buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1224.

Like a white lotus, unstained, behold his purity; unattached in mind, alone, to that Buddha I bow down.

Aho! Buddho! Aho! Suddho! Aho! Saṃsuddhamānaso!

Aho! Aho! Mettāsindhu! Buddhaṃ taṃ paṇamāmyahaṃ.1225.

Ah! Awakened One! Ah! Pure One! Ah! With utterly purified mind! Ah! Ah! Ocean of loving-kindness! To that Buddha I bow down.

Sīlavisuddho, cittavisuddho, diṭṭhivisuddho namo namo;

Dhammavihārī, maṅgalakārī, janahitakārī namo namo.

Pure in virtue, pure in mind, pure in view—homage, homage! Dweller in the Dhamma, maker of the auspicious, worker for the welfare of beings—homage, homage!

Mahātapassī, dhammavipassī, akkhayadassī namo namo;

Maggagavesī, lokahitesī, buddhamahesī namo namo.

Great ascetic, seer of the Dhamma, seer of the imperishable—homage, homage! Seeker of the path, benefactor of the world, the Buddha, the great sage—homage, homage!
